# Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit

ברטנורא על משנה שביעית

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit)

חיבור התשתית הקדום של חז״ל, המתעד מגוון דעות הלכתיות ממסורת התורה שבעל פה. נחתם בשנת 200 לספירה לערך.

16th-century commentary printed in most editions of the Mishnah, including summaries of talmudic discussions and legal conclusions.

Sections: Chapter-Mishnah-Comment

Location: Mishnah/Rishonim on Mishnah/Bartenura/Seder Zeraim

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): On Your Way (Segments: 429, License: Public Domain)

Version (En): Bartenura on Mishnah, trans. by Rabbi Robert Alpert, 2020 (Segments: 427)


## Chapter 1


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:1:1)


**עַד אֵימָתַי חוֹרְשִׁים בִּשְׂדֵה הָאִילָן.** לְקַמָּן מְפָרֵשׁ אֵיזֶהוּ שְׂדֵה אִילָן:


עד מתי חורשין בשדה האילן – further on (i.e., in Mishnah 2), it explains what is a שדה אילן /a field containing at least three trees within a distance of a Se’ah (i.e., a cultivated field in which trees grow; an orchard).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:1:2)


**עֶרֶב שְׁבִיעִית.** שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לְהוֹסִיף מִשָּׁנָה שִׁשִּׁית עַל הַשְּׁבִיעִית בְּאִסּוּר עֲבוֹדַת הָאָרֶץ, וּלְקַמָּן יָלְפִינַן לַהּ מִקְּרָאֵי:


ערב שביעית – that it is necessary to add from the sixth year on to the seventh year in the prohibition of working the land, and further on (i.e., Mishnah 4), we will derive it from Biblical verses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:1:3)


**כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהוּא יָפֶה לַפֶּרִי.** כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁהַחֲרִישָׁה יָפָה לַפְּרִי, הֲוֵי מְתַקֵּן הַקַּרְקַע לְצֹרֶךְ הַפְּרִי שֶׁל שִׁשִּׁית וְלֹא לְצֹרֶךְ שְׁבִיעִית:


כל שמן שהוא יפה לפרי – that is to say that the ploughing is auspicious for the produce, as he would prepare the ground for the needs of the sixth year and not for the needs of the seventh [year].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:1:4)


**עַד הָעֲצֶרֶת.** דְּעַד הָעֲצֶרֶת יָפֶה לַפְּרִי וְתוּ לֹא, וּמִן הָעֲצֶרֶת וְאֵילָךְ נִרְאֶה כִּמְתַקֵּן לְצֹרֶךְ שְׁבִיעִית. וּבִשְׂדֵה לָבָן שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ אִילָן עַד הַפֶּסַח. וּמִשְׁנָה זוֹ הִיא מִשְׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה וְאֵינָהּ הֲלָכָה, דְּרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל וּבֵית דִּינוֹ נִמְנוּ עַל שְׁנֵי פְרָקִים הַלָּלוּ שֶׁהֵם פֶּסַח בִּשְׂדֵה לָבָן וַעֲצֶרֶת בִּשְׂדֵה אִילָן וְהִתִּירוּם, וּמֻתָּר לַחֲרֹשׁ עַד רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה שֶׁל שְׁבִיעִית:


עד העצרת – for until Atzeret/Shavuot, it is auspicious for the produce and not for more than that. And from Atzeret and onward, it appears that he is preparing for the needs of the seventh year. But in a bright, shade-less field (vegetable or grain field) where there is no tree, until Passover. But this Mishnah is the “earliest collection of Mishnah/משנה ראשונה and is not the Halakha, for Rabban Gamaliel and his Jewish court counted on these two periods of time, which are Passover for a bright shade-less field and Atzeret/Shavuot for a cultivated field in which trees grow, and permitted them [to work the fields beyond these established times] [so that] it is permitted to plough until Rosh Hashanah of the seventh year.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:2:1)


**לְבֵית סְאָה.** קַרְקַע שֶׁהִיא חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה עַל חֲמִשִּׁים אַמָּה:


לבית סאה – land which is fifty cubits by fifty cubits (i.e., a cubit is a measure equal to the distance from the elbow to the tip of the middle-finger).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:2:2)


**כִּכַּר דְּבֵלָה.** הַתְּאֵנִים הַיְבֵשִׁים עֲשׂוּיִין בְּמַעֲגֵּלָה כְּעֵין כִּכַּר לֶחֶם קְרוּיִם כִּכַּר דְּבֵלָה:


ככר דבלה – the dry figs are made by with a press similar to a loaf of bread which are called a cake of pressed figs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:2:3)


**בָּאִיטַלְקִי.** בְּמָנֶה שֶׁל אִיטַלְיָא שֶׁל יָוָן, וּלְפִי שֶׁהָיָה כָּךְ בִּימֵי מֹשֶׁה מְשַׁעֲרִים בּוֹ, וְהַמָּנֶה מֵאָה דִינָרִים וְהַדִּינָר שֵׁשׁ מָעוֹת וּמִשְׁקַל כָּל מָעָה שֵׁשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שְׂעוֹרוֹת בֵּינוֹנִיּוֹת:


באיטלקי – in the Italian Maneh (= 25 Selah) of Greece and since it was such in the days of Moses, we estimate by it and Maneh is one-hundred Denarim and the Denar is six Maot and the weight of each M’ah is sixteen intermediate-sized hairs/panacles.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:2:4)


**פָּחוֹת מִכָּאן.** מִכִּכָּר שֶׁל שִׁשִּׁים מָנֶה אֵין חוֹרְשִׁים כָּל בֵּית סְאָה, אֶלָּא תַּחַת הָאִילָן לְבָד וְחוּצָה לוֹ:


פחות מכאן – from a cake of sixty Maneh we do not plow the entire field requiring one Seah of seed other than under the tree alone, or outside of it, as much as its light and its basket that collects figs is called “its light” (??chaff) like one who harvests dates and cuts grapes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:2:5)


**כִּמְלֹא אוֹרֶה וְסַלּוֹ.** הַמְּלַקֵּט תְּאֵנִים קָרוּי אוֹרֶה כְּמוֹ גּוֹדֵר בִּתְמָרִים וּבוֹצֵר בַּעֲנָבִים. וְסַלּוֹ, הַסַּל שֶׁנּוֹתֵן בּוֹ מַה שֶּׁהוּא לוֹקֵט. וּשְׁאָר הַשָּׂדֶה חָשׁוּב שְׂדֵה הַלָּבָן וְאֵין חוֹרְשִׁין אוֹתוֹ אֶלָּא עַד הַפֶּסַח:


וסלו – the basket into which he places what he picks and the rest of the field is considered a bright, shade-less field and we don’t plow it other than until Passover.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:3:1)


**אֶחָד אִילַן סְרָק.** שֶׁאֵינָהּ עוֹשָׂה פֵּרוֹת וּנְטוּעִין שְׁלֹשָׁה לְבֵית סְאָה:


אחד אילן סרק – that does not bear fruit and they are planted three to a Bet Seah (50 cubits by fifty cubits – see the previous Mishnah).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:3:2)


**אִם רְאוּיִן לַעֲשׂוֹת.** רוֹאִין כָּל אִילָן וְאִילָן בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ אִם אִילָן שֶׁל תְּאֵנָה כַּיּוֹצֵא בוֹ הָיָה עוֹשֶׂה כִּכַּר דְּבֵלָה. וְלָמָּה שִׁעֲרוּ בִּתְאֵנָה, עַל שֶׁפֵּרוֹתֶיהָ גַּסִּין וְעוֹשָׂה הַרְבֵּה:


אם ראויין לעשות – we view each and every tree on its own – if it is a fig tree and things similar to it, it would make a loaf of pressed figs and for what reason did they estimate with a fig on its huge fruit and that it makes a lot.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:3:3)


**לְצָרְכָּן.** מְלֹא אוֹרֶה וְסַלּוֹ חוּצָה לוֹ:


לצרכן – the area that is occupied by the gatherer when his basket is placed behind him.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:4:1)


**עַד שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה וְעַד תִּשְׁעָה.** כְּלוֹמַר דִּין זֶה שֶׁאָמַרְנוּ הַיְנוּ מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה וְעַד תִּשְׁעָה, אֲבָל אִם הָיוּ עֲשָׂרָה אוֹ יוֹתֵר אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָן עוֹשִׂין חוֹרְשִׁין:


עד שיהיו מג' ועד ט' – meaning to say, this law that we stated, meaning from three until nine [trees], but if there were ten [trees] or more, even though they don’t produce [fruit], we plough [the entire Bet Seah].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:4:2)


**שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בֶּחָרִישׁ וּבַקָּצִיר תִּשְׁבֹּת.** אַרֵישָׁא קָאֵי דִּתְנַן אֵין חוֹרְשִׁין לָהֶן אֶלָּא לְצָרְכָּן. וּמִנַּיִן שֶׁאֵין חוֹרְשִׁין כָּל הַבֵּית סְאָה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בֶּחָרִישׁ וּבַקָּצִיר תִּשְׁבֹּת, וְאִם אֵינוֹ עִנְיָן לְשַׁבָּת בְּרֵאשִׁית שֶׁכְּבָר נֶאֱמַר (שמות כ) שֵׁשֶׁת יָמִים תַּעֲבֹד וְעָשִׂיתָ כָּל מְלַאכְתֶּךָ, וְלֹא לִשְׁמִטָּה שֶׁכְּבָר נֶאֱמַר (ויקרא כה) שֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים תִּזְרַע שָׂדֶךָ וְשֵׁשׁ שָׁנִים תִּזְמֹר כַּרְמֶךָ, תְּנֵהוּ עִנְיָן לֶאֱסֹר שְׁנֵי פְרָקִים שֶׁל עֶרֶב שְׁבִיעִית, מִפֶּסַח לִשְׂדֵה לָבָן, וּמֵעֲצֶרֶת לִשְׂדֵה אִילָן. וְהָכִי קָאָמַר קְרָא, בֶּחָרִישׁ שֶׁקְּצִירוֹ אָסוּר וְאֵיזֶה זֶה חָרִישׁ שֶׁל עֶרֶב שְׁבִיעִית שֶׁנִּכְנָס לַשְּׁבִיעִית, תִּשְׁבֹּת. וּבַקָּצִיר שֶׁחֲרִישׁוֹ אָסוּר וְאֵיזֶה זֶה קָצִיר שֶׁל שְׁבִיעִית שֶׁיּוֹצֵא לְמוֹצָאֵי שְׁבִיעִית, נַמִּי תִּשְׁבֹּת, שֶׁסְּפִיחִים שֶׁגָּדְלוּ בַּשְּׁבִיעִית אוֹ שֶׁעָבַר וְזָרַע בַּשְּׁבִיעִית אָסוּר לְקָצְרָם בַּשְּׁמִינִית:


שנאמר בחריש ובקציר תשבות – it refers to the first clause, as it is taught [at the conclusion of Mishnah 3 of this chapter): “We plow them only according to their need.” And from where do we learn that we don’t plough the entire Bet Seah, as it states (Exodus 34:21): “ you shall cease from labor even at plowing time and harvest time,” and if it has no connection with the Sabbath of Creation, as it is already stated (Exodus 20:9): “Six days you shall labor and do all your work,” and not for Shemittah/the Sabbatical Year , for it already states (Leviticus 25:3): “Six years you may sow your field and six years you may prune your vineyard [and gather in the yield],” teach it in connection with the two periods of the Eve of the Seventh (i.e., Sabbatical) year – from Passover for a grain or vegetable field/a bright field without shade, and from Shavuot for an orchard (see Tractate Sheviit, Chapter 2, Mishnah 1). And this is what the Biblical verse said: “at plowing time” when its harvesting is forbidden. And which is this? The plowing of the Eve of the Seventh year [which enters into the Seventh year] you should cease/rest, and in the harvesting, where plowing is forbidden. And which is this? This is the harvesting of the Seventh year that extends into the year following the Sabbatical year, you should alsorest/cease, and the after-growth/spontaneous growth (see Tractate Sheviit, Chapter 9, Mishnah 1) that grew during the Sabbatical year or that if he transgressed and sowed on the Sabbatical year is forbidden to harvest them in the eighth year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:4:3)


**רַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל אוֹמֵר.** לְעוֹלָם בְּשַׁבָּת מִשְׁתָּעֵי קְרָא כְּפַשְׁטֵיהּ, וַאֲתָא לְאַשְׁמְעִינַן מֶה חָרִישׁ רְשׁוּת, שֶׁאֵין לְךָ חָרִישׁ שֶׁל מִצְוָה, אַף קָצִיר דְּאָמַר רַחֲמָנָא תִּשְׁבֹּת בְּקָצִיר שֶׁל רְשׁוּת, יָצָא קְצִיר הָעֹמֶר שֶׁהוּא מִצְוָה דְּבָעִינַן קְצִירָה לִשְׁמָהּ, שֶׁזֶּה דּוֹחֶה אֶת הַשַּׁבָּת:


ר' ישמעאל אומר – the Biblical verse always talks about the Sabbath as its plain meaning, and it comes to inform us: just as plowing [which] is a voluntary act [is prohibited on the Sabbath], for you do not have a commanded form of plowing, so [only] harvesting, which the All-Merciful stated “you shall rest/cease,” [which likewise] is voluntary [is prohibited on the Sabbath] but excludes the harvesting of the Omer/the first sheaf [and is therefore permitted on the Sabbath – see Tractate Menahot, Chapter 10, Mishnah 9], that is a Mitzvah and that we require harvesting for its sake which supersedes the [observance of] the Sabbath.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:5:1)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ מִצְטָרְפִין.** וְנֶחְשָׁבִין כְּאִלּוּ הֵן שֶׁל אִישׁ אֶחָד וְחוֹרְשִׁין כָּל בֵּית סְאָה בִּשְׁבִילָן:


הרי אלו מצטרפין – and they are considered as if they (i.e., the trees join together to form a single orchard) belong to one person, and they plow the entire Bet Seah for them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:5:2)


**וְכַמָּה יְהֵא בֵינֵיהֶן.** בֵּין אִילָן לְאִילָן:


וכמה יהא ביניהן – between one tree and another.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:5:3)


**כִּמְלֹא בָקָר וְכֵלָיו.** צֶמֶד בָּקָר עִם הָעֹל, וְהַיְנוּ אַרְבַּע אַמּוֹת. אֲבָל פָּחוֹת מִכָּאן לְמֵעֲקָר קַיְמֵי וְאֵין חוֹרְשִׁין בִּשְׁבִילָן:


כמלא בקר וכליו - the yoke of an ox with the burden, and this is four cubits, but less than this, they are established as detached and they don’t plow from them.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:6:1)


**נְטִיעוֹת.** יְלָדוֹת, וְלִזְקֵנוֹת קָרֵי אִילָן, וְהָכָא קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּאַף עַל גַּב דִּשְׁלֹשָׁה אִילָנוֹת לְבֵית סְאָה קָרוּי שְׂדֵה אִילָן וְחוֹרְשִׁין כָּל בֵּית סְאָה בִּשְׁבִילָן, בִּנְטִיעוֹת יְלָדוֹת אֵין חוֹרְשִׁין עַד שֶׁיִּהְיוּ עֲשָׂרָה:


נטיעות (young trees) – young; and the old ones are called a tree, and here it comes to tell us that een though that three trees in a Bet Seah (i.e., 50 cubits by 50 cubits) is called an orchard and we plow the entire Bet Seat for them, with young trees, we don’t plow until there will be ten of them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:6:2)


**שׁוּרוֹת.** שֶׁנְּטוּעוֹת בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד כְּחוֹמָה וְאֵינָן מְפֻזָּרוֹת:


שורה – that are planted in one place like a wall and are not spread out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:6:3)


**אֵין חוֹרְשִׁין אֶלָּא לְצָרְכָּן.** מִשּׁוּם דִּכְשֶׁמְּפֻזָּרוֹת נִרְאֶה כְּחוֹרֵשׁ לְצֹרֶךְ הָאִילָן, אֲבָל הֵיכָא דְקַיְמֵי בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד נִרְאֶה כִּמְתַקֵּן אֶת הָאָרֶץ לְצֹרֶךְ שְׁבִיעִית וְלֹא לְצֹרֶךְ הָאִילָנוֹת:


שורה – The Aramaic translation of “wall” is a row.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:6:4)


**שׁוּרָה.** תַּרְגּוּם חוֹמָה שׁוּרָה:


עטרה – a fence around them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:6:5)


**עֲטָרָה.** גָּדֵר סָבִיב:


אין חורשין אלא לצרכן – because when they are spread out, they appear like one is plowing for the needs of the tree, but where they exist in one place, it appears like they are improving the land for the needs of the Seventh Year, and not for the needs of the trees.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:7:1)


**הַנְּטִיעוֹת וְהַדְּלוּעִים מִצְטָרְפִין לְבֵית סְאָה.** בִּדְלַעַת יְוָנִית דַּוְקָא אַיְרֵי בְּמַתְנִיתִין שֶׁהִיא גְדוֹלָה כְּאִילָן וְלֹא בִשְׁאָר דְּלוּעִין. וְצָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּהְיוּ הַנְּטִיעוֹת רַבּוֹת עַל הַדְּלוּעִים, כְּגוֹן שֶׁהָיוּ הַנְּטִיעוֹת שִׁשָּׁה וְהַדְּלוּעִים אַרְבָּעָה, מִצְטָרְפִין לַעֲשָׂרָה וְחוֹרְשִׁין כָּל בֵּית סְאָה בִּשְׁבִילָן:


הנטיעות והדלועים מצטרפין לבית סאה – with a Greek gourd, exactly is what our Mishnah is speaking which is large like a tree, but not with the rest of the gourds, and it is necessary that the young trees will be greater in number than the gourds as for example, when there are six young trees and four gourds, they combine to make ten and they plow the entire Bet Seah for their sake.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:7:2)


**רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר.** אֲפִלּוּ הָיוּ כָּל הָעֲשָׂרָה דְּלוּעִין חוֹרְשִׁין כָּל בֵּית סְאָה בִּשְׁבִילָן, לְפִי שֶׁהֵן כִּנְטִיעוֹת. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


רשב"ג אומר – even if all of the ten were gourds, they plow the entire Bet Seah for their sake, because they are like young trees. And the Halakha is according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:8:1)


**עַד שֶׁיָּחֹלּוּ.** עַד שֶׁיֵּצְאוּ לְחֻלִּין בַּשָּׁנָה הָרְבִיעִית עַל יְדֵי פִּדְיוֹן, וְאִם לֹא פְדָאָן בַּשָּׁנָה הָרְבִיעִית, עַד שֶׁיֵּעָשׂוּ חֻלִּין מֵאֲלֵיהֶן בַּשָּׁנָה הַחֲמִשִּׁית:


עד שיחולו – until they go out to be unconsecrated produce in the fourth year through redemption, and if he did not redeem them in the fourth year until they became unconsecrated produce on their own in the fifth year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:8:2)


**נְטִיעָה כִּשְׁמָהּ.** בַּת שְׁנָתָהּ קְרוּיָה נְטִיעָה וְתוּ לֹא. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר כִּשְׁמָהּ כָּל זְמַן שֶׁבְּנֵי אָדָם קוֹרְאִין לָהּ נְטִיעָה וְאֵין קוֹרְאִין לָהּ אִילָן. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


נטיעה בשמה – one year old it is called a young tree, and not further. A nother explanation: [The word] "כשמה"/according to its [common] meaning – all the while that people call it a young tree, they don’t call it "אילן"/a tree. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:8:3)


**שֶׁנִּגְמַם.** שֶׁנֶּחְתַּךְ:


שנגמם – cut off


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:8:4)


**חֲלִיפִין.** גִּדּוּלִים, לְשׁוֹן גִּזְעוֹ מַחֲלִיף:


חליפין (young shoots coming out of a stump) – growth , from the language of their stump grows new shoots/regenerates.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 1:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/1:8:5)


**כִּנְטִיעָה.** בֵּין לְעִנְיַן חֲרִישָׁה בֵּין לְעִנְיַן עָרְלָה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


כנטיעה – whether regarding the matter of plowing or the matter of Orlah. An the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon.



## Chapter 2


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:1:1)


**עַד אֵימָתַי, שְׂדֵה הַלָּבָן.** שָׂדֶה שֶׁל תְּבוּאָה וְקִטְנִית שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ אִילָן:


עד אימתי. שדה הלבן – a field of grain and pulse/beans where there is no tree in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:1:2)


**הַלֵּחָה.** לַחְלוּחִית הַקַּרְקַע מֵחֲמַת הַגְּשָׁמִים:


הליחה – moisture of the ground on account of the rains.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:1:3)


**מִקְשָׁאוֹת וּמִדְלָעוֹת.** קִשּׁוּאִים וּדְלוּעִים. אֲבָל מִשָּׁם וְאֵילֵךְ מֵחֲזֵי כִּמְתַקֵּן שָׂדֵהוּ לְצֹרֶךְ שְׁבִיעִית:


מקשאות ומדלעות (late vegetables or fruits) – cucumbers and gourds, but from then and onward, it appears like repairing his field for the needs of the Seventh year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:1:4)


**נָתַתָּ תּוֹרַת כָּל אֶחָד בְּיָדוֹ.** זֶה אוֹמֵר כָּלְתָה לֵחָה בְּתוֹךְ שֶׁלִּי וְזֶה אוֹמֵר לֹא כָלְתָה לֵחָה בְּתוֹךְ שֶׁלִּי:


נתת תורת כל אחד בידו – this one says that the moisten was used up within my own, and that one says that the moistness was not used up within my own.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:1:5)


**אֶלָּא בִּשְׂדֵה לָבָן.** שֶׁעָתִיד לְזָרְעָהּ אַחַר הַחֲרִישָׁה וְצָרִיךְ שֶׁתְּהֵא רֹב לֵחָה קַיֶּמֶת, אֵין חוֹרְשִׁים אֶלָּא עַד הַפֶּסַח:


אלא בשדה לבן – for he would ultimately sow it after the plowing and he needs to have most of its moistness existing , they do not plow other than until Passover.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:1:6)


**וּשְׂדֵה אִילָן.** שֶׁהִיא נְטוּעָה כְּבָר אֵין צָרִיךְ שֶׁתְּהֵא רֹב לֵחָה קַיֶּמֶת שֶׁאֵין חוֹרְשִׁין אוֹתָהּ אֶלָּא כְּדֵי שֶׁיֵּרְדוּ הַגְּשָׁמִים בְּעֹמֶק הַקַּרְקַע, הִלְכָּךְ חוֹרְשִׁים בָּהּ עַד הָעֲצֶרֶת. וְכָל הַמִּשְׁנָיוֹת הַלָּלוּ דְּחוּיוֹת הֵן כִּדְאָמְרִינַן לְעֵיל בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא שֶׁרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל וּבֵית דִּינוֹ נִמְנוּ עַל שְׁנֵי פְרָקִים הַלָּלוּ שֶׁהֵם פֶּסַח וַעֲצֶרֶת וּבִטְּלוּם, וּמֻתָּר לַחֲרֹשׁ עַד רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה שֶׁל שְׁבִיעִית:


ושדה אילן – which is planted already and there is no need that it most if of its moistness exists, for they don’t plow it other than in order that the rains will fall in the depths of the bground, therefore, they plow it until Shavuot/Atzeret. But all of these Mishnayot are superseded as we stated above in the first chapter, for Rabban Gamaliel and his court resolved about these two periods which are Passover and Atzeret/Shavuot and nullified them, and it is permitted to plow until Rosh Hashanah of the Seventh Year.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:1)


**מְזַבְּלִין.** מַכְנִיסִין לָהֶם זֶבֶל:


מזבלין – they bring in to them manure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:2)


**מְעַדְּרִים.** חוֹפְרִים בְּעִקְּרֵי הָאִילָנוֹת, שֶׁכָּל דָּבָר שֶׁהוּא לְצֹרֶךְ פֵּרוֹת שֶׁל שִׁשִּׁית מֻתָּר בְּתוֹסֶפֶת שְׁבִיעִית, וְדָבָר שֶׁאֵינוֹ אֶלָּא לְצֹרֶךְ תִּקּוּן הָאִילָן אָסוּר, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן הוּא דָּבָר שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ בַּשְּׁבִיעִית עַצְמָהּ אֵין אִסּוּרוֹ אֶלָּא מִדִּבְרֵי סוֹפְרִים, וּבְתוֹסֶפֶת שְׁבִיעִית לֹא גָזוּר:


מעדרים (hoe) – they dig at the roots of the trees, for every thing that is for the needs of the fruit of the Sixth Year, is permissible with the time added to (I.e., the supplement) to the Seventh Year, and something that is not other than for the need of repair of the tree is prohibited, unless it is something that even in the Seventh Year itself that ists prohibition is ot other than from the words of the Scribes, but in the supplement/time added to the Seventh Year, they did not make a decree.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:3)


**בֵּית הַשְּׁלָחִין.** שֶׁאֵין דַּי לָהּ בְּמֵי גְשָׁמִים וְצָרִיךְ לְהַשְׁקוֹתָהּ, תַּרְגּוּם וְהוּא עָיֵף (בראשית כה) וְהוּא מְשַׁלְהֵי:


בית השלחין (a field which needs irrigation) – the rain water is not enough for it and one must water it. The Aramaic Targum/translation of (Genesis 25:29): “[Esau came in from the open,] famished (i.e., tired), he is tired (in Aramaic).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:4)


**מְיַבְּלִין.** חוֹתְכִין הַיַּבֶּלֶת, מוּמִין הַנּוֹלָדִין בָּאִילָן:


מיבלין – they cut the withered twigs, warts that appear on the tree.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:5)


**מְפָרְקִין.** הֶעָלִין מֵעַל הָאִילָן לְהָקֵל מֵעָלָיו:


מפרקין – release [the too- luxuriant growth of] leaves from the tree in order to lighten it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:6)


**מְאַבְּקִין.** שָׁרָשִׁים הַמְּגֻלִּים מְכַסִּים אוֹתָן בְּאָבָק:


מאבקין – roots that are revealed, they cover them with dust.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:7)


**מְעַשְּׁנִין.** תַּחַת הָאִילָן לְהָמִית הַתּוֹלָעִים הַגְּדֵלִים בּוֹ:


מעשנין (fumigate) – underneath the tree to kill the worms growing on it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:2:8)


**אַף נוֹטֵל אֶת הֶעָלִין.** בַּשְּׁבִיעִית עַצְמָהּ, וְתַנָּא קַמָּא לֹא אָמַר אֶלָּא מְפָרְקִין אֶת הֶעָלִין בְּתוֹסֶפֶת שְׁבִיעִית לְבָד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


אף נוטל את העלין – on the Seventh Year itself, but the First Tanna/teacher did not say other than releasing the [overly luxirant growth of] leaves in the time added to/the supplement to the Seventh Year alone. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:3:1)


**מְסַקְּלִין.** מְסִירִין אֶת הָאֲבָנִים:


מסלקין – they remove the stones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:3:2)


**מְקַרְסְמִין.** כְּמוֹ מְכַרְסְמִין בְּכַ"ף, לְשׁוֹן יְכַרְסְמֶנָּה חֲזִיר מִיָּעַר (תהלים פ), כְּלוֹמַר כּוֹרְתִים וְחוֹתְכִין הָעֲנָפִים הַיְבֵשִׁים מִן הָאִילָן:


מקרסמין (trim trees) – like מכרסמין with a KAF (trimming) from the language of (Psalms 80:14): “Wild boars gnaw at it [and creatures of the field feed on it],” meaning to say, they sever and cut the dry branches from the tree.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:3:3)


**מְזָרְדִין.** הָעֲנָפִים הַלַּחִים כְּשֶׁהֵם מְרֻבִּין רְגִילִים לְקָצְצָן וּמַנִּיחִין מִקְצָת מֵהֶן, וְזֶהוּ זֵרוּד:


מזרדין (trim, nip shoots off) – themoist branches when they are many, it is customary to cu them and leave some of them and that is trimming/cutting shoots off/thinning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:3:4)


**מְפַסְּלִין.** שֶׁנּוֹטֵל אֶת הַפְּסֹלֶת. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים מְפַסְּלִין לְשׁוֹן פְּסָל לְךָ (שמות לד), שֶׁחוֹתְכִים כָּל הָעֲנָפִים שֶׁבָּאִילָן כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְעַבֶּה:


מפסלין (cut off dry dwigs/trim)- that he takes the refuse, and there are those who interpret [the word] מפסלין as the language of (Exodus 34:1): “Carve [two tablets of stone like the first,]” that they cut all the branches that are on the tree in order that they would become thick.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:3:5)


**כְּזֵרוּדָהּ וּכְפִסּוּלָהּ שֶׁל חֲמִשִּׁית וְכוּ'.** תַּנָּא קַמָּא לֹא שָׁרֵי אֶלָּא עַד רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה שֶׁל שְׁבִיעִית. וְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ סָבַר כִּשְׁנַת חֲמִשִּׁית כֵּן שִׁשִּׁית, מַה חֲמִשִּׁית נִכְנֶסֶת לַשִּׁשִּׁית כָּךְ שִׁשִּׁית נִכְנֶסֶת לַשְּׁבִיעִית, וּכְמוֹ שֶׁמְּזָרְדִין שָׁל חֲמִשִּׁית בַּשִּׁשִּׁית כָּךְ מְזָרְדִין שֶׁל שִׁשִּׁית בַּשְּׁבִיעִית.


כזירודה וכפיסולה של חמישית וכו' – the first Tanna/teacher did not permit it other than until Rosh Hashanah of the Seventh Yeaer. But Rabbi Yehoshua held that the Fifth Year is like the Sixth [Year]; just as the Fifth Year enters into the Sixth [Year], so, the Sixth [Year] enters into the Seventh [Year], and just as they trim/nip shoos off of the Fifth [Year] in the Sixth [Year], so they trim/nip shoots off of the Sixth [Year] in the Seventh [Year]


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:3:6)


**וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר.** מֵעֲצֶרֶת וְאֵילָךְ שֶׁהוּא נֶאֱסַר בַּעֲבוֹדַת הָאִילָן נֶאֱסַר נַמִּי בְּזֵרוּד וּפִסּוּל. וְשָׁלֹשׁ מַחְלוֹקוֹת בַּדָּבָר, וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא:


ור' שמעון אומר – From Atzeret/Shavuot and onwards, working of (the Land0 is prohibited [the Tree] is prohibited also in trimming and cutting off dry twigs, and there are three disputes in the matter, and the Halakha is according to the first Tanna/teacher.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:4:1)


**מְזַהֲמִין אֶת הַנְּטִיעוֹת.** כְּשֶׁקְּלִפַּת הָאִילָן נוֹשֶׁרֶת מַדְבִּיקִים שָׁם זֶבֶל לְהַבְרִיאוֹ כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יָמוּת הָאִילָן. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים מְזַהֲמִין שֶׁמּוֹשְׁחִים הָאִילָנוֹת בְּדָבָר שֶׁרֵיחוֹ רַע כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּבְרְחוּ הַתּוֹלָעִים מִן הָרֵיחַ אוֹ שֶׁיָּמוּתוּ:


מזהמין את הנטיעות (smear plants with rancid oil for keeping off vermin – or – covring a wound in a tree with dung and tie it up) – when the husk/skin of a tree falls off, they attach there manure to heal it in order that the tree won’t die. And there are those who explain [the word] מזהמין – that they smear/anoint the trees with something that its odor is bad in order that the worms will be chased away from the odor or that they would die.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:4:2)


**וְכוֹרְכִים.** יֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים שֶׁכּוֹרְכִים דָּבָר סְבִיב הָאִילָן בַּקַּיִץ מִפְּנֵי הַחַמָּה וּבַחֹרֶף מִפְּנֵי הַצִּנָּה. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים כּוֹרְכִים הָעֲנָפִים זוֹ עִם זוֹ וְקוֹשְׁרִין אוֹתָם לְמַעְלָה בְּרָאשֵׁיהֶם שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ נְטוּשִׁים עַל הָאָרֶץ:


וכורכין – there are those who explain it that they wrap something around the tee in the summer because of the heat and in the winter because of the cold. And there are those who explain it that they wrap the branches one with the other and tie them above at their tops so that they will not be sink to the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:4:3)


**וְקוֹטְמִין אוֹתָן.** יֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים לָשִׁים בְּשָׁרְשֵׁיהֶם אֵפֶר, תַּרְגּוּם אֵפֶר קִטְמָא. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים שׁוֹבֵר הָרָאשִׁים, כְּמוֹ נִקְטַם רֹאשׁוֹ [סֻכָּה כט:]:


וקוטמין אותן (cover them with powder) – there are those who explain to put powder on their roots, and the Aramaic Targum/translation of אפר is ashes/powder. And there are those who explain that they break their heads, like (Sukkah 29b): “their heads are lopped off/broken.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:4:4)


**וְעוֹשִׂים לָהֶם בָּתִּים.** רְגִילִים לַעֲשׂוֹת לָאִילָן גָּדֵר אַמָּה סְבִיבוֹתָיו וּמְמַלְּאִים אוֹתוֹ עָפָר. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים שֶׁעוֹשִׂים לוֹ סֻכָּה מִלְּמַעְלָה לְהָגֵן עָלָיו מִן הַחַמָּה אוֹ מִן הַצִּנָּה:


ועושים להם בתלים – it is customary to make for the tree a fence a cubit around it and they will it with dust. And there are those who explain that they make for it a Sukkah from above to protect it from the heat or from the cold.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:4:5)


**עַל הַנּוֹף.** שֶׁל הָאִילָן, אֲבָל לֹא עַל הָעִקָּר, שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲשֶׂה בַּשְּׁבִיעִית כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה בִּשְׁאָר הַשָּׁנִים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בְּרַבִּי צָדוֹק, אֶלָּא אָסוּר לְהַשְׁקוֹת הָאִילָן בַּשְּׁבִיעִית בֵּין עַל הַנּוֹף בֵּין עַל הָעִקָּר:


על הנוף (on the boughs of the tree) – of the tree, but not upon the root so that he should not do in the Seventh Year in the manner that he does in the rest of the years. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer B’Rabbi Tzadok, but rather it is forbidden to water the tree in the Seventh Year, whether on the boughs or on the root.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:5:1)


**סָכִין אֶת הַפַּגִּים.** פֵּרוֹת שֶׁלֹּא נִגְמַר בִּשּׁוּלָן סָכִין אוֹתָן בְּשֶׁמֶן כְּשֶׁהֵן מְחֻבָּרִים לָאִילָן לְמַהֵר בִּשּׁוּלָן. וּפְעָמִים שֶׁמְּנַקְּבִים אוֹתָן וּמְשִׂימִים שֶׁמֶן בְּתוֹךְ הַנֶּקֶב, אִי נַמִּי מְנַקְּבִים הַפַּגִּים כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּכָּנְסוּ בָהֶם גְּשָׁמִים וְיִתְבַּשְּׁלוּ מְהֵרָה:


סכין את הפגים (pour oil on unripe figs) – fruit which did not complete their ripening, we pour oil on them when they are attached to the tree to hurry their ripening. But sometimes they perforate them and place oil inside the hole; alternatively, they perforate the unripe figs in order that rain can enter into them and they will ripen more quickly.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:5:2)


**פַּגֵּי עֶרֶב שְׁבִיעִית.** פֵּרוֹת שֶׁאֵין מִתְבַּשְּׁלִים עַד תִּשְׁרֵי שֶׁל שְׁבִיעִית, אֵין סָכִין וְאֵין מְנַקְּבִין אוֹתָן בַּשִּׁשִּׁית, מֵאַחַר שֶׁאֵין נִגְמַר בִּשּׁוּלָן עַד שֶׁתִּכָּנֵס שְׁבִיעִית. וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ שֶׁלֹּא לָסוּךְ אָסַר תַּנָּא קַמָּא. וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה מַפְלִיג בֵּין מָקוֹם שֶׁנָּהֲגוּ לָסוּךְ לְמָקוֹם שֶׁלֹּא נָהֲגוּ לָסוּךְ, דִּבְמָקוֹם שֶׁלֹּא נָהֲגוּ לָסוּךְ לֹא מְחַזְיָא סִיכָה עֲבוֹדָה וְשָׁרֵי:


פגי ערב שביעית – fruit that do not ripen until Tishrei of the Seventh Year, and we don’t pour oil on them and we don’t perforate them on the Sixth Year from after which their ripening was not completed until the Seventh Year entered, and even in a place where thy had the practice not to pour oil, the first Tanna/teacher forbade it. But Rabbi Yehuda distinguishes between a place where they had the practice to pour oil and a place where they did not have the practice to pour oil, for in a place where they did not have the practice to pour oil, anointing is not seen as work and it is permitted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:5:3)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מַתִּיר בָּאִילָן.** שֶׁהוּא מָלֵא פֵּרוֹת שְׁבִיעִית, לַעֲשׂוֹת בּוֹ מְלָאכָה בְּמוֹצָאֵי שְׁבִיעִית, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא רַשַּׁאי בַּעֲבוֹדַת הָאִילָן אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַפֵּרוֹת קְדוֹשִׁים בִּקְדֻשַּׁת שְׁבִיעִית. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא:


ר' שמעון מתיר באילן – which is filled with Seventh Yeaer fruit to perform on work on the aftermath of the Seventh Year, because it is permitted to work on the tree, even though the fruit are sanctified with the holiness of the Seventh Year. But the Halakha is according to the first Tanna/teacher.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:6:1)


**מַבְרִיכִין.** כּוֹפְפִים זְמוֹרַת הַגֶּפֶן בָּאָרֶץ וּמְכַסִּים אוֹתָהּ בְּעָפָר:


מבריכין (bending a vine by drawing it into the ground and make it grow forth as an independent plant) – bend the vine-shoot of the vine in the ground and covering it with dust.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:6:2)


**מַרְכִּיבִים.** מְבִיאִים עָנָף שֶׁל אִילָן וּמַרְכִּיבִין אוֹתוֹ עַל גַּב אִילָן אַחֵר שֶׁהוּא מִמִּינוֹ:


מרכיבים – bringing a branch of a tree and engrafting it upon another tree that is of its species.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:6:3)


**פָּחוֹת מִשְּׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם.** בַּגְּמָרָא בְּמַסֶּכֶת רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה מוֹכִיחַ דַּהֲנָךְ שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם דִּקְלִיטָה לְתַנָּא קַמָּא, וּשְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה, וּשְׁתֵּי שַׁבָּתוֹת דְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן, צְרִיכִין קֹדֶם שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם דְּתוֹסֶפֶת שְׁבִיעִית, דְּבָעִינַן שֶׁתִּקְלֹט הַנְּטִיעָה קֹדֶם תּוֹסֶפֶת שְׁבִיעִית, וְכוּלְהוּ סְבִירָא לְהוּ תּוֹסֶפֶת שְׁבִיעִית שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם, הִלְכָּךְ לְתַנָּא קַמָּא דְּאָמַר אֵין קְלִיטָה פְּחוּתָה מִשְּׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם צָרִיךְ שְׁלֹשָׁה דִּקְלִיטָה וּשְׁלֹשִׁים דְּתוֹסֶפֶת, לְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה דְּאָמַר בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים הֲוֵי קְלִיטָה צָרִיךְ שְׁלֹשִׁים דִּקְלִיטָה וּשְׁלֹשִׁים דְּתוֹסֶפֶת, לְדִבְרֵי רַבִּי יוֹסֵי וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דְּאָמְרֵי שְׁתֵּי שַׁבָּתוֹת צָרִיךְ שְׁתֵּי שַׁבָּתוֹת לִקְלִיטָה וּשְׁלֹשִׁים לְתוֹסֶפֶת. וּפֵרוּשׁ קְלִיטָה שֶׁתְּהֵא הַנְּטִיעָה נִקְלֶטֶת וְנֶאֱחֶזֶת בְּשָׁרָשֶׁיהָ בָּאָרֶץ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


פחות ל' יום – In the Gemara of Tractate Rosh Hashanah [10b] , it proves that these thirty days of [a graft] taking root, according to the first Tanna/teacher and three days, according to Rabbi Yehuda, and two weeks according to Rabbi Yossi and Rabbi Shimon, that we need prior to thirty days of the addition/supplement of the Seventh Year, that we require that the sapling should take root prior to the supplement/addition to the Seventh Year, and all of them hold that the supplement/addition to the Seventh Year is thirty days, therefore, according to the first Tanna/teacher who stated that there is no taking root less than thirty days, it needs thirty days of taking root and thirty [days] of supplement/addition; according to Rabbi Yehuda who stated that taking root is three days, one needs three days of taking root and another thirty of supplement; to the words of Rabbi Yossi and Rabbi Shimon who stated two weeks, one requires two weeks for taking root and thirty for supplement. And the explanation of taking root is that the sapling takes root and its roots take hold in the ground. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi and Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:7:1)


**הָאֹרֶז.** בְּלַעַ"ז רי"ש:


האורז – in the foreign tongue, RICE


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:7:2)


**וְדֹחַן.** מילי"ו:


והדוחן (a species of millet) – MILLIEV.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:7:3)


**וְהַפְּרָגִין.** כְּעֵין רִמּוֹן מָלֵא זֶרַע וְהַזֶּרַע שֶׁלּוֹ מְקַשְׁקֵשׁ בְּתוֹכוֹ, וְקוֹרִין לוֹ בַּעֲרָבִי כשכ"ש וּבְלַעַ"ז פפאוור"ו:


והפרגין – a kind of pomegranate filled with seeds and its seed shakes within it, and we call it KASHBASH in Arabic and in the foreign language POPOORU.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:7:4)


**וְהַשֻּׁמְשְׁמִין.** כְּמִין זֶרַע אָרֹךְ, וּבְאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל מִמֶּנּוּ הַרְבֵּה, וְקוֹרִין לוֹ בַּעֲרָבִי סומס"ם:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:7:5)


**מִתְעַשְּׂרִים לְשֶׁעָבַר.** כְּמַעֲשַׂר שָׁנָה שֶׁעָבְרָה, אִם הָיְתָה שָׁנָה רִאשׁוֹנָה וּשְׁנִיָּה שֶׁל שְׁמִטָּה, מַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי, וְאִם שְׁלִישִׁית מַעֲשַׂר עָנִי:


מתעשרין לשעבר – with the tithe of the previous year. Of ot was the first year or the second of Shemitah, Second Tithe, and if [it was] the third year, the Poor Person’s Tithe.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:7:6)


**וּמֻתָּרִין בַּשְּׁבִיעִית.** כֵּיוָן שֶׁהִשְׁרִישׁוּ בְּשָׁנָה שִׁשִּׁית לִפְנֵי רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה שֶׁל שְׁבִיעִית אֵין בָּהֶם קְדֻשַּׁת שְׁבִיעִית:


ומותרין בשביעית – since it had taken root in the Sixth Year before Rosh Hashanah of the Seventh Year, they do not have the holiness of Seventh Year [produce].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:7:7)


**וּמִתְעַשְּׂרִין לְשָׁנָה הַבָּאָה.** כִּשְׁנַת לְקִיטָתָן, כֵּיוָן דִּבְאוֹתָהּ שָׁנָה הִשְׁרִישׁוּ. וְאִם הִיא שָׁנָה שְׁבִיעִית אֵין מִתְעַשְּׂרִין כְּלָל וְיֵשׁ לָהֶן קְדֻשַּׁת שְׁבִיעִית. אַף עַל גַּב דִּבְאִילָן אָזְלִינַן בָּתַר חֲנָטָה, וּבְיָרָק בָּתַר לְקִיטָה, וּבִתְבוּאָה וְזֵיתִים בָּתַר שְׁלִישׁ, כְּלוֹמַר כְּשֶׁיָּבִיאוּ שְׁלִישׁ בִּשּׁוּלָן אָז נִתְחַיְּבוּ בְּמַעַשְׂרוֹת, מִכָּל מָקוֹם בְּאֹרֶז וְדֹחַן וּפְרָגִין וְשֻׁמְשְׁמִין דְּאֵין לוֹקְטִים אוֹתָם כְּאֶחָד וְאֵין מְבִיאִים שְׁלִישׁ בִּשּׁוּלָם כְּאֶחָד, אֵין הוֹלְכִים בָּהֶם אֶלָּא אַחַר הַשְׁרָשָׁה, דִּבְשָׁנָה אַחַת מַשְׁרֶשֶׁת כָּל הַשָּׂדֶה, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּבַת אַחַת זוֹרְעִים אוֹתָם:


ומתעשרין לשנה הבאה – like the year of its harvesting since that in that year, it had taken root. And if it is the Seventh Year, they are not tithed at all and they have the holiness of the Seventh Year. Even though with a tree, we follow after the formation of fruits/leaves, and with vegetables after their harvesting, and with grain and olives after one-third [growth], meaning to say, when they would bring forth one-third of their ripening, then they become liable for tithes; nevertheless, with rice and millet and pomegranates and sesame, which we don’t harvest them as one, we don’t bring one-third of their ripening as one, we don’t follow after them other than following taking root, for in one year, the entire field takes root and we sew it at one time.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:8:1)


**פּוֹל הַמִּצְרִי.** פאשול"י בְּלַעַ"ז:


פול מצרי – in the foreign tongute, PASHULI


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:8:2)


**שֶׁזְּרָעוֹ לְזֶרַע.** כְּדֵי לְהוֹצִיא מִמֶּנּוּ זֶרַע לִזְרֹעַ וְלֹא לַאֲכִילָה:


שזרעו לזרע – in order to remove from it seed for sowing but not for eating.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:8:3)


**כַּיּוֹצֵא בָהֶם.** בָּתַר הַשְׁרָשָׁה, כְּאֹרֶז וְדֹחַן, בֵּין לְעִנְיַן מַעַשְׂרוֹת בֵּין לְעִנְיַן שְׁבִיעִית. וְדַוְקָא שֶׁזְּרָעוֹ לְזֶרַע, אֲבָל זְרָעוֹ לְיָרָק אַזְלִינַן בָּתַר לְקִיטָה כְּיָרָק:


כיוצא בהם – after taking root, like rice and millet, whether for the matter of tithes whether for the matter of the Seventh Year. And exactly when it was own for its seed, but if it was sown for vegetation, we follow after harvesting like vegetation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:8:4)


**הַגַּמְלוֹנִין.** הַגַּסִּים:


הגמלונין – large-sized [bean].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:8:5)


**מִשֶׁתִּרְמְלוּ.** שֶׁהֻקְשׁוּ וְנַעֲשׂוּ כְּמִין כִּיס. וְדֻגְמָתוֹ בְּתַרְמִילוֹ [שַׁבָּת לא.] שֶׁהוּא כִּיס שֶׁל רוֹעִים:


משתרמלו (when they have begun to form pods) – when they have hardened and became like a pocket, and its example, with his traveling bag (Talmud Shabbat 31a – the mean proselyte who came with nothing but his staff and travelling bag – I being without merit or ancestry), which is the pocket of shepherds.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:9:1)


**הַבְּצָלִים הַסָּרִיסִים.** שֶׁאֵין עוֹשִׂים זֶרַע כִּשְׁאָר בְּצָלִים:


הבצלים והסריסים – which do not make seed like other onions.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:9:2)


**וּפוֹל הַמִּצְרִי.** שֶׁזְּרָעוֹ לְיָרָק, דְּאִי זְרָעוֹ לְזֶרַע הָא תְּנַן לְעֵיל דְּאַזְלִינַן בֵּיהּ בָּתַר הַשְׁרָשָׁה אֲפִלּוּ לֹא מָנַע מֵהֶן מַיִם:


ופול המצרי – that was sown for herbs, for if it were seed, that which is taught in the Mishnah above (i.e., Mishnah 8), that we follow it after taking root, even if we didn’t prevent water from them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:9:3)


**שֶׁמָּנַע מֵהֶן מַיִם שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם.** יָצְאוּ מִתּוֹרַת יְרָקוֹת הַגְּדֵלוֹת עַל כָּל מַיִם וְנַעֲשׂוּ כִּשְׂדֵה בֵית הַבַּעַל שֶׁמִּסְתַּפֶּקֶת בְּמֵי גְשָׁמִים, הִלְכָּךְ מִתְעַשְּׂרִין לְשֶׁעָבַר:


שמנע מהם מים שלשים יום – they have departed from the law regarding vegetables that are grown on all water, and have been made like a field sufficiently watered by rain and requiring no artificial irrigation, that suffices with rain water, therefore, we tithe it as according to the previous year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:9:4)


**וְשֶׁל בַּעַל.** שָׂדֶה שֶׁהִיא בָּעֵמֶק וּמְלֻחְלַחַת וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לְהַשְׁקוֹתָהּ, וּמִכָּל מָקוֹם לְיָרָק צְרִיכָה הַשְׁקָאָה, מִשּׁוּם הָכִי קָתָנֵי שֶׁמָּנַע מֵהֶם מַיִם שְׁתֵּי עוֹנוֹת, זְמַן הַשְׁקָאָתָהּ שֶׁרְגִילִין לְהַשְׁקוֹתָהּ קָרֵי עוֹנָה. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


ושל בעל – a field which is in the valley and it is moist and there is no need to water it. But nevertheless, for vegetation, it requires watering, because of that which is taught that he deprived water from them for two seasons/periods of watering , the time of watering that is customary to water it is called an עונה/period, season, and the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:10:1)


**הִקְשׁוּ.** נִתְיַבְּשׁוּ וְנַעֲשׂוּ קָשִׁין:


הוקשו – they dried up and became hard.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:10:2)


**אָסוּר לְקַיְּמָן בַּשְּׁבִיעִית.** שֶׁפֵּרוֹת שְׁבִיעִית אָסוּר לְהַנִּיחָן לְזֶרַע:


אסור לקיימן בשביעית – for the fruit of the Seventh Year is forbidden to leave them for sowing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:10:3)


**הַתְּמָרוֹת.** הַצִּיץ קֹדֶם שֶׁיִּפָּתַח דּוֹמֶה לִתְמָרָה, וְכָמוֹהוּ נִקְטַם רֹאשׁוֹ וְעָלְתָה בוֹ תְמָרָה בְּפֶרֶק לוּלָב הַגָּזוּל [סֻכָּה לג.]. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר תְּמָרוֹת כְּעֵין לוּלָבֵי גְפָנִים:


התמרות (seed-capsules) – the blossom prior that its opening is similar to a seed-capsule, and like him, its head was lopped off and there ascended upon it a seed-capsule in the chapter “The Stolen Lulav” (Tractate Sukkah 33a); another explanation: the seed capsules are similar to sprouts of vines.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:10:4)


**מַרְבִּיצִין בְּעָפָר לָבָן.** כְּלוֹמַר מַשְׁקִים שְׂדֵה לָבָן. וְכֵן הֲלָכָה שֶׁמַּשְׁקִים שְׂדֵה לָבָן עֶרֶב שְׁבִיעִית כְּדֵי שֶׁיֵּצְאוּ יְרָקוֹת בַּשְּׁבִיעִית, וְלֹא עוֹד אֶלָּא שֶׁמַּשְׁקִים אוֹתוֹ בַּשְּׁבִיעִית כְּדֵי שֶׁיֵּצְאוּ יְרָקוֹת לְמוֹצָאֵי שְׁבִיעִית:


מרביצים בעפר לבן – meaning to say, we water the bright field lacking shade (vegetable or grain). And this is the Halakha that we water a bright field lacking shade on the Eve of the Seventh Year in order that the vegetables will come forth in the Seventh Year, and not only this but we water tit on the Seventh Year in order that vegetables came come forth in the aftermath of the Seventh Year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:10:5)


**מְמָרְסִין בָּאֹרֶז.** מַשְׁקִין עֲפַר הָאֹרֶז וּמְעָרְבִין אוֹתוֹ בְּמַיִם, כְּמוֹ נְתָנוֹ לַמְּמָרֵס בְּיוֹמָא [פֶּרֶק ד'‏ מִשְׁנָה ג']:


ממרסין באורז (stir the ground of a rice-field with water – to make it dough-like) – we water the dust of the rice and mix it with water, like (Tractate Yoma 43b) – they placed it in the mixer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 2:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/2:10:6)


**אֲבָל לֹא מְכַסְּחִין.** שֶׁאֵין חוֹתְכִים הֶעָלִין שֶׁל הָאֹרֶז, תַּרְגּוּם לֹא תִזְמֹר לֹא תְכַסַּח. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


אבל לא מכסחין (you may not trim rice plants) – we don’t cut the leaves of the rice, it is the Aramaic Targum/translation of לא תזמור/”or prune your vineyard” (Leviticus 25:4)/you shall not trim, and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon.



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:1:1)


**מֵאֵימָתַי מוֹצִיאִים זְבָלִים לְאַשְׁפּוֹת.** דֶּרֶךְ עוֹבְדֵי אֲדָמָה לְכַנֵּס הַזֶּבֶל לְמָקוֹם אֶחָד בַּשָּׂדֶה וְעוֹשֶׂה שָׁם אַשְׁפָּה גְדוֹלָה עַד שֶׁהוּא מְפַזְּרוֹ בַּשָּׂדֶה לְזַבְּלָהּ:


מאימתי מוציאיןם זבלים לאשפות – it is the manner of thos who work the land to bring in the manure to one place in the field and make there a large dung-heap until he disperses It in the field to manure it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:1:2)


**מִשֶׁיִּפְסְקוּ עוֹבְרֵי עֲבֵרָה.** לַעֲבֹד אֶת הָאָרֶץ בַּשָּׁנָה הַשְּׁבִיעִית, וְקֹדֶם שֶׁיִּפְסְקוּ עוֹבְרֵי עֲבֵרָה אָסוּר, שֶׁלֹּא יֹאמְרוּ מֵעוֹבְרֵי עֲבֵרָה הוּא וּלְזַבֵּל שָׂדֵהוּ הוּא מוֹצִיא. וְיֵשׁ סְפָרִים שֶׁכָּתוּב בָּהֶם עוֹבְדֵי עֲבוֹדָה, וְהִיא הִיא:


משיפסקו עוברי עבירה – to work the land in the Seventh Year and prior to that those who transgress [the laws of the Seventh Year] cease, it is forbidden so that they do not say that this is from those who transgress [the Seventh Year] and that he removes it to manure his field. And there are books in wich it is written them, that those “who perform work” and this is it (i.e., the correct reading).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:1:3)


**מִשֶּׁיִּיבַשׁ הַמָּתוֹק.** מִשֶּׁיִּהְיֶה יָבֵשׁ הַזֶּבֶל שֶׁנּוֹתֵן מְתִיקָה לַפֵּרוֹת:


משייבש המתוק – that the manure will be dried up as it gives a sweetness to the fruits/produce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:1:4)


**מִשֶּׁיִּקָּשֵׁר.** כְּשֶׁמַּתְחִיל לְהִתְיַבֵּשׁ מִתְעַבֶּה וְנַעֲשֶׂה קְשָׁרִים, וּקְרוֹבִים דִּבְרֵיהֶם לִהְיוֹת שָׁוִים. וּבַיְרוּשַׁלְמִי מְפָרֵשׁ מִשֶּׁיִּיבַשׁ הַמָּתוֹק סְדָקִין שֶׁבַּבִּקְעָה כְּשֶׁהַמָּטָר יוֹרֵד מִתְמַלְּאִים מַיִם וְאֵינָם מִתְיַבְּשִׁים אֶלָּא לְאַחַר זְמַן, וְנִקְרָאִים מָתוֹק עַל שֵׁם מָתְקוּ לוֹ רִגְבֵי נָחַל (איוב כא):


משיקשר – when it begins to dry up, it thickens and makes clumps/becomes knotty excrescences, and their words are close to being equivalent. And in the Jerusalem Talmud, it explains that when the sweetness dries out, fissures in the group of fields/valley when the rain falls fill up with water and do not dry up other than after a while, and they are called מתוק /sweet on account of (Job 21:33): “The clods of the wadi are sweet to him, [everyone follows ehind hi, innumerable are those who precede him].”



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:2:1)


**מַשְׁפֵּלוֹת.** קֻפּוֹת שֶׁמּוֹצִיאִין בָּהֶם זְבָלִים, שֶׁכָּל מַשְׁפֵּל מַחֲזִיק לֶתֶךְ שֶׁהוּא מַשְּׂאוֹי שֶׁל חֲמוֹר, וְהַלֶּתֶךְ חֲצִי כּוֹר, חֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה סְאִין:


משפלות (wicker baskets carried on a pole over the shoulder) - baskets that they carry out in them manure and each wicker bashed holds a LETEKH (15 SEAH of dung) which is the burden of the donkey, and the LETEKH is one-half of a KOR which is fifteen SEAH.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:2:2)


**מוֹסִיפִין עַל הַמַּשְׁפֵּלוֹת.** נוֹתֵן בְּאַשְׁפָּה אַחַת מַשְׁפֵּלוֹת כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא רוֹצֶה:


מוסיפין על המשפלות – he places in one dunk heap wicker baskets like he wishes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:2:3)


**וְאֵין מוֹסִיפִין עַל הָאַשְׁפּוֹת.** לַעֲשׂוֹת יוֹתֵר מִשָּׁלֹשׁ אַשְׁפּוֹת בְּבֵית סְאָה, שֶׁלֹּא יֵרָאֶה כִּמְזַבֵּל אֶת הַשָּׂדֶה:


ואין מוסיפין על האשפות – to make more than three dung-haps in a Bet Seah (50 cubits by 50 cubits) , so that he will not appear as if he is manuring the field.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:2:4)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר אַף עַל הָאַשְׁפּוֹת.** מוֹסִיפִין, כֵּיוָן שֶׁיֵּשׁ צִבּוּר גָּדוֹל בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד לֹא מֵחֲזֵי כִּמְזַבֵּל. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ר' שמעון אומר אף על האשפות – they may add [to the dung heaps] since there is a large community in one place and it does not appear like manuring. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:3:1)


**יָתֵר מִכָּאן.** מִשָּׁלֹשׁ [אַשְׁפּוֹת] מֻתָּר. וְהַיְנוּ דְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דְּרֵישָׁא, וְהַשְׁתָּא אָתָא לְאַשְׁמְעִינַן דְּשָׁרֵי רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אֲפִלּוּ אֵין עֶשֶׂר מַשְׁפֵּלוֹת, וּבִלְבָד שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיֶה בָהּ פָּחוֹת מִשָּׁלֹשׁ, דְּאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֵּשׁ מְעַט זֶבֶל בָּאַשְׁפָּה וְסַלְקָא דַעְתָּךְ אֲמֵינָא דְּמוֹדֶה רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דְּאִם מוֹסִיף הָאַשְׁפּוֹת נִרְאֶה כִּמְזַבֵּל, קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּלֹא מֵחֲזֵי כִּמְזַבֵּל הוֹאִיל וְאֵינוֹ מְפַזְּרוֹ:


יתר מכאן – from three [dung-heaps] it is permitted, which is Rabbi Shimon’s [opinion] in the opening clause, and not it comes to inform us that Rabbi Shimon permits it, even if there aren’t ten wicker baskets, as long as there will not be in it less than three, and even though there is a little manure in the dung-heap, and you might think that I would say that Rabbi Shimon admits that if he adds dung-heaps, it appears like manuring, it comes to tell us that it does not appear like manuring since he does not disperse it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:3:2)


**עַד שֶׁיַּעֲמִיק שְׁלֹשָׁה.** שֶׁיַּעֲמִיק מְקוֹם הָאַשְׁפָּה אוֹ יַגְבִּיהַּ מִשְּׁאָר הַקַּרְקַע שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים, שֶׁיִּהְיֶה נִכָּר דְּלִכְנֹס הַזֶּבֶל לְמָקוֹם אֶחָד הוּא עוֹשֶׂה וְלֹא לְזַבֵּל אֶת הַשָּׂדֶה:


עד שיעמיק שלשה – that he will deepening the place of the manure or raise it up from the rest of the land b three handbreadths, so that it will be recognized that he is doing this in order to bring in the manure into one place, and not to manure the field.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:3:3)


**עוֹשֶׂה אָדָם אֶת זִבְלוֹ אוֹצָר.** אוֹתָם שָׁלֹשׁ אַשְׁפּוֹת שֶׁל שְׁלֹשִׁים מַשְׁפֵּלוֹת יָכוֹל לַעֲשׂוֹת אוֹתָם אַשְׁפָּה אַחַת וּלְהוֹסִיף עֲלֵיהֶם כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּרְצֶה, דְּלֹא תֵימָא כִּי הֵיכִי דְאָסוּר יוֹתֵר מִשָּׁלֹשׁ אַשְׁפּוֹת לְבֵית סְאָה הָכִי נַמִּי אָסוּר יוֹתֵר מִשִּׁעוּר שָׁלֹשׁ אַשְׁפּוֹת בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד:


עושה אדם את זבלו אוצר – those three dung-heaps of thirty wicker backets, he can make of them one dung-heap and can add to them as he wishes, and that is not to say that just as it is forbidden to have more than three dung-heaps for a Bet Seah (i.e., fifty cubits by fifty cubits), that is also forbidden to have more than the measurement of three dung-heaps in one place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:3:4)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹסֵר.** בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי מוּכָח דִּבְיוֹתֵר מִכַּשִּׁעוּר בִּלְבָד אָסַר רַבִּי מֵאִיר. אֲבָל בְּכַשִּׁעוּר, הַשְׁתָּא בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה מְקוֹמוֹת מֻתָּר, בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד לֹא כָּל שֶׁכֵּן. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


ר"מ אוסר – in the Jerusalem Talmud it is found that Rabbi Meir forbade more than the measurement alone, but with the merasurement, now that there are three places, it is it is permitted in one place, is it not obvious? But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:3:5)


**הָיָה לוֹ דָּבָר מֻעָט.** שֶׁאֵין לוֹ לְהוֹצִיא בְּפַעַם אַחַת כַּשִּׁעוּר הַמְפֹרָשׁ לְעֵיל, מוֹצִיא אוֹתוֹ מְעַט וּמוֹסִיף עָלָיו וְהוֹלֵךְ:


היה לו דבר מועט – he doesn’t have to remove at one time according to the explained measurement of above, but removes a bit of it an/or adds to it and continues.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:3:6)


**רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר בֶּן עֲזַרְיָה אוֹסֵר.** שֶׁמָּא לֹא יִהְיֶה לוֹ זֶבֶל יוֹתֵר וְנִרְאֶה כִּמְזַבֵּל שָׂדֵהוּ, עַד שֶׁיּוֹצִיא עֶשֶׂר מַשְׁפֵּלוֹת כְּאַחַת:


ר"א בן עזריה אוסר – lest he will not have more manure and it appears like he is manuring his field until he removes ten wicker baskets at once, or,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:3:7)


**אוֹ עַד שֶׁיַּעֲמִיק שְׁלֹשָׁה אוֹ יַגְבִּיהַּ שְׁלֹשָׁה.** שֶׁכֵּיוָן שֶׁאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם מְשֻׁנֶּה מִשְּׁאָר הַשָּׂדֶה לֹא מֵחֲזֵי כִּמְזַבֵּל, וְכֵן אִם נְתָנוֹ עַל הַסֶּלַע שֶׁאֵינוֹ רָאוּי לִזְרִיעָה:


עד שיעמיק ג' או יגביה ג' – for since he same place is different from the rest of the field, it does not appear like manuring, and similarly, if he placed it on a rocky place which is not appropriate for sowing.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:4:1)


**הַמְדַיֵּר שָׂדֵהוּ.** לְשׁוֹן דִּיר שֶׁל בְּהֵמוֹת, שֶׁאֵין לוֹ מָקוֹם פָּנוּי לְהַעֲמִיד בֶּהֱמוֹתָיו אֶלָּא שָׁם, וְאֵינוֹ מִתְכַּוֵּן לְזַבֵּל שָׂדֵהוּ בְּעִנְיָן זֶה, וְדַעְתּוֹ לְהַכְנִיס הַזֶּבֶל לָאַשְׁפּוֹת אַחַר כָּךְ:


המדיר את שדהו (let cattle changes folds within a field/collect mnaure in a field, by letting cattle live on it) – it is the language of a shed for cattle , and he has no free place to place his cattle other than there, and he doesn’t intend to manure his field in this manner and it is intention to bring in the manure to dung-heaps afterwards.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:4:2)


**סַהַר.** כְּמוֹ סַחַר, הֶקֵּף שֶׁל מְחִצָּה סְבִיב הַצֹּאן:


סהר (enclosure for cattle near a dwelling/stable) – like an enclosure, a circumference of a partition around the sheep.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:4:3)


**עוֹקֵר שָׁלֹשׁ רוּחוֹת.** כְּשֶׁנִּתְמַלְּאָה הַסַּהַר זֶבֶל עוֹקֵר מִן הַסַּחַר שָׁלֹשׁ רוּחוֹת וְזוֹקְפָן לְצַד [אַחֵר] שֶׁל אֶמְצָעִית וּמְדַיֵּר שָׁם כְּמוֹ כֵן בֵּית סָאתַיִם, וְעוֹשֶׂה אַשְׁפּוֹת בְּתוֹךְ שָׂדֵהוּ שָׁלֹשׁ אַשְׁפּוֹת לְבֵית סְאָה כִּדְאָמְרָן:


עוקר ג' רוחות – when the enclosure for cattle is filled with manure, he ruproots from the enclosure from three sides and restores on the [other] side of them middle and changes folds there like that for two Bet Seah, and makes dung-heaps within his field , three dung-heaps for a Bet Seah as we have stated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:4:4)


**נִמְצָא מְדַיֵּר בֵּית אַרְבַּעַת סְאִים.** סָאתַיִם מִצַּד זֶה שֶׁל מְחִצָּה אֶמְצָעִית וְסָאתַיִם מִצַּד אַחֵר. וְאַשְׁמְעִינַן מַתְנִיתִין דְּשָׁרֵי לְדַיֵּר בֵּית אַרְבַּעַת סְאִין קֹדֶם שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂה מִמֶּנָּה אַשְׁפּוֹת, אֲבָל טְפֵי לֹא, שֶׁלֹּא יֹאמְרוּ לְדַיֵּר שָׂדֵהוּ מִתְכַּוֵּן:


נמצא מדייר בית ארבעה סתים – Two Seah from this side of the middle partition and two from the [other] side, and our Mishnah teaches us that it permits a cattle fold of four Seah prior to his making from it dung-heaps, but not more than that, so that they should not say that he intended to make folds in his fields [for cattle to live in them].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:4:5)


**מִפְּנֵי מַרְאִית הָעַיִן.** שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא נִרְאֶה כְּמִתְכַּוֵּן לְזַבֵּל שָׂדֵהוּ אִם עוֹשֶׂה אֶת כֻּלָּהּ סַחַר:


מפני מראית העין – so that it would not appear like he intends to manure his field if he makes all of it an enclosure for cattle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:4:6)


**כְּדֶרֶךְ הַמְּזַבְּלִין.** שָׁלֹשׁ אַשְׁפּוֹת לְכָל בֵּית סְאָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


כדרך המזבלין – three dung-heaps for each Bet Seah, but the Halakha is not according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:5:1)


**מַחְצֵב.** מָקוֹם בְּתוֹךְ שָׂדֵהוּ שֶׁחוֹצְבִין מִמֶּנּוּ אֲבָנִים לְבִנְיָן. אִם הָיָה מְכֻסֶּה עָפָר וְלֹא הָיָה נִרְאֶה אֵין פּוֹתְחִין אוֹתוֹ בַּתְּחִלָּה בַּשְּׁבִיעִית, שֶׁהָרוֹאֶה אוֹמֵר לְתַקֵּן שָׂדֵהוּ הוּא עוֹשֶׂה כְּדֵי לִזְרֹעַ בְּתוֹכוֹ וְלֹא לְהוֹצִיא הָאֲבָנִים לְבִנְיָן:


מחצב (quarry) – a place within his field that he hews/chisels stones for building. If it was covered with dirt, and it would not be visible, they don’t open it at the beginning of Seventh Year, for one who sees it says that he is doing it to repair his field in order to sow within it and not to remove the stones for building.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:5:2)


**עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא בוֹ שָׁלֹשׁ מוּרְבִּיּוֹת.** שֶׁיִּהְיוּ נִגְלִים וְנִרְאִים מֵאוֹתוֹ מַחֲצֵב קֹדֶם שְׁבִיעִית שָׁלֹשׁ שׁוּרוֹת שֶׁל אֲבָנִים, שֶׁכָּל שׁוּרָה מֵהֶם שָׁלֹשׁ אַמּוֹת אֹרֶךְ וְשָׁלֹשׁ אַמּוֹת רֹחַב בְּרוּם שָׁלֹשׁ אַמּוֹת, שֶׁנִּמְצְאוּ שָׁם תֵּשַׁע אֲבָנִים שֶׁכָּל אֶבֶן הוּא אַמָּה עַל אַמָּה בְּרוּם שָׁלֹשׁ, וְכֵן בְּכָל שׁוּרָה וְשׁוּרָה, הֲרֵי עֶשְׂרִים וְשֶׁבַע אֲבָנִים לְשָׁלֹשׁ שׁוּרוֹת, וּבְהָכִי נִכָּר שֶׁלְּצֹרֶךְ אֲבָנִים לְבִנְיָן הוּא מוֹצִיאָם וְלֹא לְתַקֵּן שָׂדֵהוּ:


עד שיהא בו שלש מורביות (piles of hewn stonesa0 – that there would revealed and appear from that quarry prior to the Seventh Year three rows of stones, that every row from there is three cubits long and three cubits wide at the height of three cubits , that there would be found there nine stones that each stone is one cubit by one cubit at a height of three [cubits], and similarly in each and every row , there are twenty-seven stones for the three rows, and in this manner, it is recognized that for the need of stones for building he finds them and not to repair his field.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:6:1)


**שֶׁל מַשְּׂאוֹי שְׁנַיִם שְׁנַיִם.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּכָל אַחַת מֵהֶן מַשְּׂאוֹי שְׁנֵי בְנֵי אָדָם:


של משאוי שנים שנים – that there is with each one of them a burden for two people.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:6:2)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ יִנָּטֵלוּ.** כֻּלָּן וַאֲפִלּוּ הַקְּטַנּוֹת שֶׁבָּהֶן, וְלֹא מִתְחֲזֵי כִּמְפַנֶּה הַגָּדֵר לַעֲשׂוֹת מְקוֹמוֹ שָׂדֶה:


הרי אלו ינטלו (they may be removed) – all of them and even the smallest of them and it does not appear like removing the wall to make a field in its place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:6:3)


**פָּחוֹת מִכָּאן מְחַצֵּב.** וְאֵינוֹ נוֹטְלוֹ כֻּלּוֹ אֶלָּא גוֹמְמוֹ וּמַנִּיחַ מִן הַסֶּלַע בָּאָרֶץ טֶפַח כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא רָאוּי לִזְרִיעָה:


פחות מכאן מחצב – and he doesn’t take all of it, but rather razes the rock even with the ground and leaves from the rock a handbreadth in the land in order that it would not be appropriate for sowing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:6:4)


**בַּמֶּה דְבָרִים אֲמוּרִים.** הַאי דְלֹא יִפְתַּח מַחְצֵב וְהַאי דְגוֹמְמוֹ:


במה דברים אמורים – that which he should not begin with hewing/chiseling and that in which he razes the rock.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:6:5)


**מַה שֶּׁהוּא רוֹצֶה יִטֹּל.** דְּלֹא מֵחֲזֵי כִּמְתַקֵּן שָׂדֶה כֵּיוָן דְּלָאו דִּידֵיהּ הוּא, וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּשֶׁלּוֹ נַמִּי לֹא מֵחֲזֵי כִּמְתַקֵּן שָׂדֶה אִם הִתְחִיל לִטֹּל מֵעֶרֶב שְׁבִיעִית:


מה שהוא רוצה יטול – that it doesn’t appear like repairing the field if he began to take [the stones] from the Eve of the Seventh Year.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:7:1)


**שֶׁזִּעְזְעָתַן הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה.** וַאֲפִלּוּ לֹא זִעְזְעָתַן אֶלָּא שֶׁעֲתִידָה לְזַעְזְעָן הֲוֵי כְּאִלּוּ זִעְזְעָתַן:


שזעזעתן המחרישה – and even if he did not loosen/move them but rather that he will eventually remove them, it is as if he moved/loosened them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:7:2)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ יִנָּטֵלוּ.** כֻּלָּן אַגַּב אוֹתָם שְׁתַּיִם:


הרי אלו ינטלו – all of them on account of these two [people who are required to move to stones].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:7:3)


**הַמְסַקֵּל שָׂדֵהוּ.** שֶׁמְּסַלֵּק הָאֲבָנִים מִן הַשָּׂדֶה, לְשׁוֹן סַקְּלוּ מֵאֶבֶן (ישעיה סב):


המסלק שדהו – that he removes stones from the field, the language of (Isaiah 62:10): “Remove the rocks!”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:7:4)


**וּמַנִּיחַ אֶת הַתַּחְתּוֹנוֹת הַנּוֹגְעוֹת בָּאָרֶץ.** דְּבִכְהַאי גַוְנָא לֹא מֵחֲזֵי כִּמְתַקֵּן שָׂדֶה:


ומניח את התחתונות הנוגעות בארץ – that in that manner, it does not appear like repairing/improving the field.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:7:5)


**גַּרְגֵּר שֶׁל צְרוֹרוֹת.** גַּל שֶׁל אֲבָנִים קְטַנּוֹת:


גרגיר של צרורות וגל של אבנים – small.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:7:6)


**אִם יֵשׁ תַּחְתֵּיהֶן סֶלַע אוֹ קַשׁ.** לֹא מֵחֲזֵי כִּמְתַקֵּן כֵּיוָן דְּאֵין הַמָּקוֹם רָאוּי לִזְרִיעָה:


אם יש תחתיהן סלע או קש – it does not appear like repairing since the place is not appropriate for sowing.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:8:1)


**אֵין בּוֹנִין מַדְרֵגוֹת.** מַעֲלוֹת עַל פִּי הַגֵּאָיוֹת, שֶׁלֹּא יַחֲלִיקוּ בִּירִידָתָן לָקַחַת הַמַּיִם שֶׁנִּתְקַבְּצוּ בָּעֵמֶק בְּאוֹתָן הַגֻּמּוֹת, דְּנִרְאֶה כִּמְתַקְּנָן לְהַשְׁקוֹת בָּהֶן שְׂדוֹתָיו בַּשְּׁבִיעִית:


אין בונין מדרגות – steps leading to ravines (for carrying up the water for irrigation), that they not slip in their descent to take the water that had gathered in the valley in those holes/indentations, because it appears like one is improving/repairing them [= the ravines/holes] to water through them his fields in the Seventh Year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:8:2)


**לֹא יִסְמֹךְ בְּעָפָר.** אִם בָּא לִהְיוֹת סוֹכֵר מְקוֹם יְצִיאַת הַמַּיִם בַּאֲבָנִים, לֹא יִתֵּן עָפָר וְטִיט בֵּין הָאֲבָנִים, מִשּׁוּם דְמֵחֲזֵי טְפֵי שֶׁהוּא מִתְכַּוֵּן לְהַשְׁקוֹת מֵהֶן שְׂדוֹתָיו:


לא יסמוך בעפר – if he comes to dam up the place where the waters go out on the rocks, he should not put dust or plaster between the stones because it looks like more like he intends to water from them his fields.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:8:3)


**אֲבָל עוֹשֶׂה הוּא חַיִץ.** אֲבָנִים סְדוּרוֹת זוֹ עַל זוֹ כְּעֵין גָּדֵר בְּלֹא עָפָר וְטִיט:


אבל עושה הוא חייץ (he may make a loose embankment/ a pile of loose and uneven material) – stones arranged this one on top of tha tone, similar to a fence without dust and/or plaster.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:8:4)


**כָּל אֶבֶן.** שֶׁבְּשָׂדֵהוּ אֲפִלּוּ קְטַנָּה, אִם כְּשֶׁהוּא בּוֹנֶה הַגָּדֵר אוֹ הַבִּנְיָן יָכוֹל לִפְשֹׁט יָדוֹ וְלִטְּלָהּ וְלִבְנוֹת בָּהּ:


כל אבן – [every stone] that is in his field, even a small one, if when he builds the fence or a building, he can stretch out his hand and take it and to build it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:8:5)


**הֲרֵי זוֹ תִּנָּטֵל.** שֶׁהַגָּדֵר מוֹכַחַת עָלָיו דְּלִבְנוֹת גִּדְרוֹ הוּא נוֹטְלָהּ וְלֹא לְתַקֵּן הַשָּׂדֶה לִזְרִיעָה:


הרי זו תנטל – that the fence proves for him that he takes it to build his fence but not to improve/repair his field for sowing.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:9:1)


**בָּאוֹת מִכָּל מָקוֹם.** אֲפִלּוּ מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁלּוֹ, דְּמֵידַע יְדִיעַ דִּלְבִנְיָן קָבָעֵי לְהוּ וְלֹא לְתַקֵּן אֶת הַשָּׂדֶה:


באות מכל מקום – even within his own [field] for he surely knows that it was established for building and not to improve/repair the field.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:9:2)


**וְהַקַּבְּלָן.** שֶׁקִּבֵּל שָׂדֶה בַּאֲרִיסוּת מֵבִיא מִכָּל מָקוֹם, וַאֲפִלּוּ מֵאוֹתָהּ שָׂדֶה שֶׁהוּא אָרִיס בָּהּ, אֲפִלּוּ אֲבָנִים קְטַנּוֹת, שֶׁאֵין [שָׂדֶה זוֹ חֲשׁוּבָה כְּשֶׁלּוֹ. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים וְהַקַּבְּלָן שֶׁדַּרְכּוֹ לִבְנוֹת בִּנְיָנִים בְּקִבּוֹלֶת, שֶׁאֵין] כַּוָּנָתוֹ אֶלָּא לְקַבֵּץ אֲבָנִים, מֵבִיא [מִכָּל מָקוֹם וַאֲפִלּוּ מִשָּׂדֶה] שֶׁלּוֹ:


והקבלן (and the contractor) – who received the field as a tenant (who tills the wowner’s ground for a certain share in the produce). מביא מכל מקום – and even from the same field that he is a tenant in it, even small stones, for [this field] is not [considered as his own, and there are those who explain that the contractor that is his manner to build buildings through his contract-labor as it is not] his intention other than to gather/collect stones he brings [nevertheless and even from] his own [field].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:9:3)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ר' יוסי אומר כו' – and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:10:1)


**לְהַעֲמִיק עַד הַסֶּלַע.** וְלֹא חַיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא מִמְלָּךְ וְזָרַע לֵיהּ, שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם לִזְרֹעַ סָמוּךְ לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים. אֲבָל בֵּינוֹ לְבֵין חֲבֵרוֹ חַיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא מִמְלָּךְ וְזָרַע לֵיהּ, וְאָסוּר:


להעמיק עד הסלע – and we are not troubled that perhaps he changes his mind and sows it, for it is not the manner for people to sow close to the public domain. But between him and his friend, we are troubled tht perhaps he will change his mind and sow it and it is forbidden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:10:2)


**צוֹבְרוֹ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים.** וְאַחַר כָּךְ נוֹטְלוֹ מִשָּׁם וּמְתַקֵּן אֶת רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה:
