צוברו ברה"ר (pile it up in the public domain) - and afterwards, he will take it from there and repair the public domain as it was.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:10:3)


**כְּשֵׁם שֶׁאֵין מְקַלְקְלִים רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים כָּךְ לֹא יְתַקֵּן.** כְּלוֹמַר לֹא מִבַּעְיָא לִצְבֹּר עֲפָרוֹ לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים שֶׁהוּא אָסוּר אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיְּסִירֶנּוּ אַחַר כָּךְ, שֶׁהֲרֵי מְקַלְקֵל הוּא לְשָׁעָה, אֶלָּא אֲפִלּוּ לְתַקֵּן אָסוּר, כְּגוֹן אִם הָיוּ אֲבָנִים אוֹ עָפָר מְפֻזָּרִים בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לֹא יִצְבְּרֵם בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד, אֶלָּא יְסַלְּקָם לְגַמְרֵי מֵרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וְהַיְנוּ דְקָאָמַר כְּשֵׁם שֶׁאֵין מְקַלְקְלִין רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לִצְבֹּר עֲפָרוֹ לִרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁדַּעְתּוֹ לְסַלְּקוֹ מִיָּד, כָּךְ לֹא יְתַקֵּן לִצְבֹּר אֲבָנִים אוֹ עָפָר הַמְפֻזָּרִים בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וְלָשׂוּם אוֹתָם בְּצִדֵּי רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים לְפִי שָׁעָה, אֶלָּא יְסַלְּקָם מִיָּד לְגַמְרֵי מִכָּל רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים וְיוֹצִיאֵם לַיָּם אוֹ לַנָּהָר:


כשם שאין מקלקלים רה"ר כך לא יתקן - meaning to say, it is not necessary to pile up his dust in the public domain which is prohibited, even though he would remove it afterwards for he does damage by the hour, but even to repair is forbidden, as for example, if the stones or the dust were scattered in the public domain, he should not pile them up in one place, but rather remove them completely from the public domain, and that is what he said: just as we don’t do damage in the public domain, he should not pile up his dust , even though that it was his intention to remove it immediately, so also he should not repair to pile up stones or dust that is scattered in the public domain and bring them out to the sea or the river.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:10:4)


**מַה יַּעֲשֶׂה בֶּעָפָר.** אַלִּיבָּא דְרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


מה יעשה בעפר – according to Rabbi Akiva.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:10:5)


**צוֹבְרוֹ בְּתוֹךְ שָׂדֵהוּ כְּדֶרֶךְ הַמְזַבְּלִים.** שָׁלֹשׁ אַשְׁפּוֹת לְבֵית סְאָה:


צוברו בתוך שדהו כדרך המזבלים- three dung heaps for a Bet Seah (fifty cubits by fifty cubits).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 3:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/3:10:6)


**וְכֵן הַחוֹפֵר בּוֹר שִׁיחַ וּמְעָרָה.** בִּשְׁאָר יְמוֹת הַשָּׁנָה, פְּלִיגֵי בָהּ רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ וְרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא. לְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ צוֹבְרוֹ בִּרְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים, וּבִלְבָד שֶׁיְּסַלְּקֶנּוּ אַחַר כָּךְ וִיתַקֵּן רְשׁוּת הָרַבִּים כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה, וּלְרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁדַּעְתּוֹ לְתַקֵּן אָסוּר, אֶלָּא צוֹבְרוֹ בִּרְשׁוּתוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


וכן החופר בור שיח מערה – like the rest of the days of the yeaer, Rabbi Yehoshua and Rabbi Akiva disagree regarding this. According to Rabbi Yehoshua, we pile it up in th public domain, as long as he doesn’t remove it afterwards and improve/repair the public domain as it was, and for Rabbi Akiva, even though it is his intention to repair/improve, it is forbidden, other than to pile it up in his domain. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Akiva.



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:1:1)


**בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה.** כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁהוּא מְלַקֵּט מֵחֲבֵרוֹ אֶת הַגַּס הַגַּס. הָכִי מְפָרְשָׁא מַתְנִיתִין בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי, בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה הָיוּ אוֹמְרִים מְלַקֵּט אָדָם בְּתוֹךְ שֶׁלּוֹ אֶת הַגַּס הַגַּס כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁמְּלַקֵּט בְּשֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ, בֵּין גַּסִּין בֵּין דַּקִּין, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּעֵצִים וַעֲשָׂבִים כְּשֶׁמְּלַקְּטִים אוֹתָן מִתּוֹךְ הַשָּׂדֶה מְתַקֵּן הַשָּׂדֶה לִזְרִיעָה, כְּשֶׁמְּלַקֵּט הַגַּס כְּלוֹמַר הָעֵצִים וְהָעֲשָׂבִים הַגַּסִּים וּמַנִּיחַ אֶת הַדַּקִּין, תָּלִינַן דְּצָרִיךְ לָעֵצִים הוּא וְלָאו לְתַקֵּן אֶת הַקַּרְקַע קָעָבֵיד, כְּדֶרֶךְ דְּתָלִינַן בִּמְלַקֵּט בִּשְׂדֵה חֲבֵרוֹ בֵּין דַּקִּין וּבֵין גַּסִּין, דְּלֹא עָבֵיד אִינָשׁ לְתַקֵּן שְׂדֵה חֲבֵרוֹ וּמֵידַע יְדִיעַ דְּלָעֵצִים הוּא צָרִיךְ:


בראשונה. כדרך שהוא מלקט מחברו את הגס הגס – this Mishnah is explained in the Jerusalem [Talmud]. – at first they would say that a person could gather within his own [field] the large ones in the manner that he gathers that of his neighbor, whether large whether small, and even though that wood and grasses when we collect them from within the field, he is clearing the field for sowing, when he gathers the large ones, meaning to say, the wood and the large grasses and leaves the small ones, we rely that he needs the wood and not to clear/repair the land he is doing so, in the manner that we rrely with someone who gathers in the field of his neighbor whether small or whether large, for a person doesn’ act to clear/repair the field of his neighbor and he certainly knows that it is for the wood tha the needs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:1:2)


**מִשֶּׁרַבּוּ עוֹבְרֵי עֲבֵרָה.** שֶׁהָיוּ מְלַקְּטִין בִּשְׂדוֹתֵיהֶן בֵּין דַּקִּים בֵּין גַּסִּין וְהֵן אוֹמְרִים בַּגַּסִּים לִקַּטְנוּ:


משרבו עוברי עבירה – that they would gather in the fields whether small whether large, and they say hat we gathered large ones.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:1:3)


**שֶׁלֹּא בְטוֹבָה.** שֶׁאֵין חֲבֵרוֹ מַחֲזִיק לוֹ טוֹבָה, דְּהַשְׁתָּא וַדַּאי לֹא אָתֵי לְמִלְקָט הַדַּקִּין כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵין חֲבֵרוֹ מַחֲזִיק לוֹ טוֹבָה עַל זֶה:


שלא בטובה – that his neighbor does not attach merit, for now, he certainly does not come to gather the small ones, since his friend does not attach merit to this for now, he certainly doesn’t come to gather the small ones, since his neighbor does not attach merit on this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:1:4)


**וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר שֶׁיְּקַצֵּץ לָהֶם מְזוֹנוֹת.** שֶׁיֹּאמַר לוֹ לְקֹט מִשָּׂדִי הַיּוֹם וְאֶתֵּן לְךָ מְזוֹנוֹתֶיךָ, דִּפְשִׁיטָא דְאָסוּר:


אין צריך לומר שיקצץ להם מזונות – that he would say to him, gather from my field today and I will give you your meals, for that is clearly prohibited.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:2:1)


**שֶׁנִּתְקַוְּצָה.** שֶׁנִּטְּלוּ קוֹצֶיהָ בַּשְּׁבִיעִית:


שנתקווצה – that took its thorns out in the Seventh Year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:2:2)


**שֶׁנִּטַּיְּבָה.** שֶׁנֶּחְרְשָׁה הֵיטֵב, שֶׁכָּל הָעוֹלָם חוֹרְשִׁים פַּעַם אַחַת וְהוּא חָרַשׁ שְׁתֵּי פְּעָמִים. וּמַתְנִיתִין אַיְרֵי בִּשְׁעַת הַסַּכָּנָה שֶׁהָיְתָה הַמַּלְכוּת אוֹנֶסֶת עַל הַמַּס הַמּוּטָל עַל הַקַּרְקָעוֹת וְהִתִּירוּ לַחֲרֹשׁ בַּשְּׁבִיעִית כְּדֵי לִתֵּן הַמַּס הַקָּצוּב מִתְּבוּאָתָהּ לַמֶּלֶךְ, וְאִם חָרַשׁ שְׁתֵּי פְּעָמִים קְנָסוּהוּ רַבָּנָן, דַּחֲרִישָׁה אַחַת הִתִּירוּ וְלֹא שְׁתֵּי חֲרִישׁוֹת:


שנטייבה – that was well plowed for the entire world plows one time and he plowed twice. But our Mishnah is speaking about a time of danger when the Kingdom (Rome) takes by force the tax that is placed upon the land masses and they permitted plowing in the Seventh Year in order to give the determined tax from its grain to the king, and if he plowed twice, the Rabbis fined him, for one plowing they permitted but not two plowings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:2:3)


**אוֹ שֶׁנִּדַּיְּרָה.** שֶׁעָשׂוּ מִמֶּנָּה דִּיר לַבְּהֵמוֹת שֶׁעָשָׂה בָהּ סַהַר כִּדְאָמְרִינַן בְּפִרְקִין דִּלְעֵיל, וְלֹא עָשָׂה מִמֶּנּוּ אַשְׁפּוֹת אֶלָּא הִנִּיחוֹ כְּדֵי לְזַבֵּל בּוֹ הַשָּׂדֶה:


או שנדיירה – lthat they made from it a pen for the cattle that made with it an enclosure for cattle as we stated in the chapter above (Tractate Sheviit, Chapter 3, Mishnah 4), and di dnot make from it dung-heaps but rather left it in order to manure the field with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:2:4)


**לֹא תִזָּרַע לְמוֹצָאֵי שְׁבִיעִית.** דְּקָנְסִינַן לֵיהּ בַּהֲנֵי יוֹתֵר מִבְּנִתְקַוְּצָה:


לא תזרע למוצאי שביעית – for we fine him in that which is greater than removing the thorns.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:2:5)


**בְּטוֹבָה.** אָסוּר, דְּאֵין מַחֲזִיקִין טוֹבָה לַבְּעָלִים, דְּרַחֲמָנָא אַפְקְרִינְהוּ:


בטובה – it is forbidden we don’t attach merit to it to the owners for the All-Merciful made them ownerless.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:2:6)


**חִלּוּף הַדְּבָרִים.** דְּבֵית שַׁמַּאי לְקֻלָּא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


חלוף הדברים – that the School of Shammai is lenient, But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:3:1)


**חוֹכְרִין נִירִין.** יָכוֹל יִשְׂרָאֵל לְקַבֵּל עָלָיו בְּכָךְ וְכָךְ כּוֹרִים לְשָׁנָה שְׂדֵה הַנָּכְרִי שֶׁחֲרָשָׁהּ בַּשְּׁבִיעִית לְזָרְעָהּ לְמוֹצָאֵי שְׁבִיעִית, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁגּוֹרֵם לַנָּכְרִי לַחֲרֹשׁ בַּשְּׁבִיעִית. נִירִין לְשׁוֹן נִירוּ לָכֶם נִיר (ירמיה ד):


חוכרין נירין – an Israelite can receive for it for such and such Korim for a year the field of a heathen that was plowed in the Seventh Year to be sown in the aftermath of the Seventh Year, and even though it causes the heathen to plow in the Seventh Year. [The word] ניר/freshly, newly plowed field , is the language of (Jeremiah 4:3): “Break up the untilled ground, [and do not ow among thorns].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:3:2)


**וּמַחֲזִיקִים יְדֵי נָכְרִים.** אִם מְצָאוֹ חוֹרֵשׁ יָכוֹל לוֹמַר לוֹ יִישַׁר כֹּחֲךָ וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָזֶה:


ומחזיקים ידי נכרים – if they found hi plowing, he can say to him, “May your strength (health) be firm,” and things of a similar manner.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:3:3)


**וְשׁוֹאֲלִים בִּשְׁלוֹמָן.** וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּיוֹם חַגָּם:


ושואלים בשלומן – and even on the day of their idolatrous festival.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:4:1)


**הַמֵּדֵל בַּזֵּיתִים.** כְּשֶׁהַזֵּיתִים מְקוֹרָבִים זֶה לָזֶה יוֹתֵר מִדַּאי נוֹטֵל אֶחָד וּמַנִּיחַ שְׁנַיִם אוֹ נוֹטֵל שְׁנַיִם וּמַנִּיחַ אֶחָד כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּגְדַּל וְיִתְעַבֶּה. מֵדֵל לְשׁוֹן שְׁלִיפָה וַהֲסָרָה, וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים לְשׁוֹן דִּלְדּוּל וְדַלּוּת, שֶׁמְּדַלְדֵּל שָׂדֵהוּ מִן הַזֵּיתִים:


המדל בזיתים – when the olive [trees] become too close to each other, he takes one [tree] and leaves two or he takes two [trees] and leaves one, in order that it should grow and thicken. [The word] מדל/thins out is the language of slipping off and removal, but there are those who explain it as the language of something torn in shreds and poverty when he reduces/weakens his field from the olives.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:4:2)


**יָגוֹם.** גּוֹמֵם וּמְקַצֵּץ הָאִילָן וּמַשְׁאִיר הַשֹּׁרֶשׁ בָּאָרֶץ, וְלֹא יְשָׁרֵשׁ לְגַמְרֵי, שֶׁלֹּא יֵרָאֶה כְּעוֹבֵד אֶת הָאֲדָמָה:


יגום – he razes and cuts down the tree and leaves the root in the ground, but he does not uproot it completely so that it will not appear like he is working the land.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:4:3)


**וּמוֹדִים בְּמַחֲלִיק.** כְּגוֹן אִם נוֹטֵל שְׁלֹשָׁה זֵיתִים זֶה בְּצַד זֶה, שֶׁמַּחֲלִיק פְּנֵי הַקַּרְקַע יוֹתֵר מִדַּאי, יֹאמְרוּ לַעֲבוֹדַת קַרְקַע הוּא מִתְכַּוֵּן וְאָסוּר:


ומודים במחליק – as for example, if he takes three olive [trees], one on the side of the other, that he smoothens the face of the field too much, they (i.e., people) will say that the intended to work the field, and that is prohibited.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:4:4)


**מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ אַף הַמַּחֲלִיק יְשָׁרֵשׁ.** דְּלֹא עָבֵיד אִינָשׁ שֶׁיְּתַקֵּן קַרְקַע חֲבֵרוֹ, וְיָדוּעַ דְּלָעֵצִים הוּא מְכַוֵּן:


מתוך של חבירו כף המחליק ישרש- that a person doesn’t act that he clears/repairs the field of his neighbor, for it is known that intends only [to remove] the wood.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:5:1)


**לֹא יְחַפֶּה.** הַבִּקּוּעַ בְּעָפָר, שֶׁהֲרֵי הוּא מְתַקֵּן בְּכָךְ, שֶׁהֶעָפָר נַעֲשֶׂה טִיט וּמַשְׁבִּיחַ הָאִילָן:


לא יחפה – that is split in the dirt, for he would be clearing/improving [the land] through this, for the dust becomes plaster and improves the tree.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:5:2)


**בַּאֲבָנִים וּבְקַשׁ.** דַּהֲוֵי כְּמוֹשִׁיב שׁוֹמֵר שֶׁלֹּא יִיבַשׁ:


באבנים ובקש – for it is like restoring a guard that it should not dry out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:5:3)


**בְּתוּלַת שִׁקְמָה.** שֶׁלֹּא נִקְצְצָה מֵעוֹלָם:


בתולת שקמה – [a virgin tree] that was never cut down.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:5:4)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהִיא עֲבוֹדָה.** שֶׁהִיא מֻשְׁבַּחַת בְּכָךְ:


מפני שהיא עבוה – and it improves it with this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:5:5)


**כְּדַרְכָּהּ אָסוּר.** שֶׁהִיא מִתְגַּדֶּלֶת וּמִתְעַבָּה בְּכָךְ וַהֲוֵי כְּעֵין זוֹמֵר לַגְּפָנִים:


כדרכה אסור – that it grows and becomes thicker with this, and it is like pruning the vines.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:5:6)


**אֶלָּא מַגְבִּיהַּ עֲשָׂרָה.** שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ לְעוֹלָם לְכָרְתָהּ אֶלָּא לְמַטָּה מֵעֲשָׂרָה:


אלא מגביה עשרה – for it is not the manner ever to cut it other than below ten [handbreadths from the ground]


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:5:7)


**אוֹ גוֹמֵם מֵעַל הָאָרֶץ.** שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ לִכְרֹת אוֹתָהּ כָּךְ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


או גומם מעל הארץ – for it is not the manner to cut it as such. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:6:1)


**הַמְזַנֵּב בִּגְפָנִים.** מְקַטֵּעַ זַנְבוֹת הַגְּפָנִים כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּעַבֶּה הַגֶּזַע וְיִגְדַּל וְיִגְבַּר כֹּחוֹ, וְכֵן הַקּוֹצֵץ קָנִים כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְעַבּוּ וְיוֹסִיף כֹּחָן:


המזנב גפנים – cut/lop off the extreme branches of the vine, on order that what grows from the trunk will grow and thicken and increase its strength, and similarly, he cuts reeds in order that they thicken and add to them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:6:2)


**יַרְחִיק טֶפַח.** מִן הַקַּרְקַע, וְשׁוּב לֹא מֵחֲזֵי כַּעֲבוֹדָה:


ירחיק טפח – [it should be distanced one handbreadth] from the ground and further it will not appear as work.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:6:3)


**שֶׁנִּפְשַׁח.** נִסְדַּק, כְּמוֹ (איכה ג) וַיְפַשְּׁחֵנִי:


שנפשה – that was split, like (Lamentations 3:11): “[He has forced me off my way] and mangled me.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:6:4)


**לֹא שֶׁיַּעֲלֶה.** שֶׁיִּתְחַבְּרוּ סְדָקָיו:


לא שיעלה – that its split parts will not become reattached.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:6:5)


**אֶלָּא שֶׁלֹּא יוֹסִיף.** לִסָּדֵק:


אלא שלא יוסיף – to split further.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:7:1)


**מֵאֵימָתַי אוֹכְלִים פֵּרוֹת הָאִילָן בַּשְּׁבִיעִית.** מִשּׁוּם דְּדָרְשִׁינַן לְאָכְלָה וְלֹא לְהֶפְסֵד, וְאִם אוֹכְלָן קֹדֶם בִּשּׁוּלָן הַיְנוּ הֶפְסֵד:


מאימתי אוכלים פירות האילן בשביעית – because we have expounded, “to eat it” and not “to become lost” and if they eat them before they ripen, that is loss.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:7:2)


**הַפַּגִּים.** פַּגֵּי תְאֵנָה כְּדִכְתִיב (שיר השירים ב) הַתְּאֵנָה חָנְטָה פַגֶּיהָ:


הפגים – unripe dates, as it is written (Song of Songs 2:13): “The green figs form on the fig tree, [the vines in blossom give off fragrance].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:7:3)


**מִשֶּׁהִזְרִיחוּ.** הֶאֱדִימוּ, וְזֶהוּ הַתְחָלַת בִּשּׁוּלָן:


משהזריחו – they redden and this is the beginning of their ripening.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:7:4)


**אוֹכֵל בָּהֶם פִּתּוֹ בַּשָּׂדֶה.** אֲבָל לְלַקֵּט וּלְהַכְנִיס לַבַּיִת אֵינוֹ רַשַּׁאי עַד שֶׁיִּגָּמְרוּ:


אוכל בהם פתו בשדה – but to gather and to bring them into his house, he is not allowed until they should complete [their ripening].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:7:5)


**בִּחֲלוּ.** שֶׁגָּדַל הַרְבֵּה, וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ וְגַם נַפְשָׁם בָּחֲלָה בִי (זכריה יא). וּבַיְרוּשַׁלְמִי יָלִיף לַהּ מִדִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כה) תִּהְיֶה כָל תְּבוּאָתָהּ לֶאֱכֹל וּכְתִיב (שם) מִן הַשָּׂדֶה תֹּאכְלוּ אֶת תְּבוּאָתָהּ, בִּשְׁתֵּי תְבוּאוֹת הַכָּתוּב מְדַבֵּר, אַחַת מִן הַבַּיִת וְאַחַת מִן הַשָּׂדֶה:


בחלו – that it grew a great deal, and similar to it (Zechariah 11:8): “and they in turn were disgusted with me.” But in the Jerusalem [Talmud] it derives it from (Leviticus 25:7): “[and your cattle and the beasts in your land] may eat all its yield,” and it is written (Leviticus 25:12): “you may only eat the growth directly from the field,” with two grains the Biblical verse speaks – one from the house and the other from the field.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:8:1)


**מִשֶּׁהֵבִיא מַיִם.** שֶׁסּוֹחֲטוֹ וְיוֹצֵא מִמֶּנּוּ מַשְׁקֶה:


משהביא מים – when he presses the fruit/wrings them out, and the liquid comes out from them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:8:2)


**מִשֶּׁהִבְאִישׁוּ.** מִשֶּׁיִּקָּרְאוּ בָּאְשָׁה, וְהַיְנוּ כְּשֶׁיַּתְחִילוּ לְהִתְבַּשֵּׁל וְנִתְבַּשְּׁלוּ עַד שֶׁהַחַרְצַנִּים שֶׁבִּפְנִים נִרְאִים מִבַּחוּץ מִתּוֹךְ הַקְּלִפָּה:


משהבאישו – when they are called in the early stage of ripening, and that is when they begin to ripen and they have ripened until the shells of the grapes that are inside appear from the outside from within the shell.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:9:1)


**רְבִיעִית לִסְאָה.** שֶׁמִּסְּאָה זֵיתִים יָכוֹל לְהוֹצִיא רְבִיעִית הַלֹּג שֶׁמֶן:


רביעית לסאה – that from a Seah of olives, he is able to produce/bring forth a quarter of a Log of oil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:9:2)


**פּוֹצֵעַ.** דֶּרֶךְ הַזֵּיתִים לְפָצְעָן כְּדֵי לְמַתֵּק מְרִירוּתָן:


פוצע – it is the manner of olives to burst them open in order to sweeten their bitterness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:9:3)


**וְאוֹכֵל בַּשָּׂדֶה.** דְּדָבָר שֶׁדַּרְכּוֹ לֶאֱכֹל הוּא. אֲבָל לָסוּךְ לֹא חָזוּ, וְאֵין סָכִין אֶלָּא בְּדָבָר שֶׁדַּרְכּוֹ לָסוּךְ:


ואוכל בשדה – for it is a thing whose manner is to eat it, but to pour oil, it was not fit, and they don’t pour oil other than from something whose manner is to pour oil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:9:4)


**הִכְנִיסוּ חֲצִי לֹג.** שֶׁנִּתְבַּשְּׁלוּ עַד שֶׁהוּא יָכוֹל לְהוֹצִיא חֲצִי לֹג מִסְּאָה:


הכניסו חצי לוג – when they became ripe until he is able to remove one-half a LOG from a Seah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:9:5)


**הִכְנִיסוּ שְׁלִישׁ.** שֶׁנִּתְגַּדְּלוּ שְׁלִישׁ מִמַּה שֶׁעֲתִידִים לְהִתְגַּדֵּל, אִי נַמִּי שֶׁמּוֹצִיא מֵהֶן עַכְשָׁיו שְׁלִישׁ מִמַּה שֶּׁמּוֹצִיא לִסְאָה כְּשֶׁנִּתְבַּשְּׁלוּ יָפֶה:


הכניסו שלשי – when they have grown one -third from what they will eventually grow; alternatiely, when he removes from them now one-third from what he he removes for a Seah when they have ripened well.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:9:6)


**כָּךְ עוֹנָתָן לַשְּׁבִיעִית.** אֵין אוֹכְלִין מֵהֶן בַּשְּׁבִיעִית עַד שֶׁיַּגִּיעוּ לְעוֹנַת מַעַשְׂרוֹת הַמְפֹרָשׁ בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא דְמַעַשְׂרוֹת, דְּדַוְקָא תְּאֵנִים וַעֲנָבִים וְזֵיתִים רְגִילִים לֶאֱכֹל קֹדֶם בִּשּׁוּלָן, לְפִיכָךְ הִתִּירוּ לֶאֱכֹל מֵהֶם בַּשָּׂדֶה בַּשְּׁבִיעִית קֹדֶם שֶׁיַּגִּיעוּ לְעוֹנַת הַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת, אֲבָל לֹא בִּשְׁאָר פֵּרוֹת:


כך עונתן לשביעית – one does not eat from them in the Seventh Year until they arrive at the season for tithing that is explained in the first chapter of [Tractate] Maaserot (see especially Mishnah 2), for especially figs and grapes and olives that are one eats customarily prior to their ripening, therefore, they permitted to eat of them in the field in the Seventh Year prior to their reaching the season of tithing, but ot with other fruit/produce.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:1)


**אֵין קוֹצְצִין אֶת הָאִילָן בַּשְּׁבִיעִית.** מִשּׁוּם דִּלְאָכְלָה אָמַר רַחֲמָנָא וְלֹא לְהֶפְסֵד. וַאֲנִי שָׁמַעְתִּי כֵּיוָן דְּאַפְקְרִינְהוּ רַחֲמָנָא לַפֵּרוֹת, אִם יִקְצְצֵם הֲוֵי גּוֹזֵל אֶת הָרַבִּים:


אין קוצצין את האילן בשביעית – because the All-Merciful said, to eat them, but not to loss them, and I heard since the All-Merciful made them ownerless, for fruit, if he cut them down, he would rob the many.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:2)


**מִשֶּׁיּוֹצִיא.** תְּחִלַּת הֶעָלִין בִּימֵי נִיסָן:


משיוציא – the beginning of the leaves in the days of Nisan.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:3)


**מִשֶּׁיְּשַׁלְשֵׁלוּ.** מִשֶּׁיַּתְחִילוּ לְהַכְבִּיד וְלִתְלוֹת כְּעֵין שַׁלְשֶׁלֶת:


משישלשלו (let them down) -when they begin to become heaven and to hang down like a chain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:4)


**מִשֶּׁיְּגָרְעוּ.** מִשֶּׁיַּעֲשׂוּ גַרְעִינִים. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, הַבֹּסֶר כְּשֶׁיַּגְדִּילוּ הָעֲנָבִים וְנַעֲשׂוּ כְּפוֹל הַלָּבָן נִקְרָא גָּרוּעַ:


משיגרעו – when they make kernels of fruit/stones; another explanation: the half-ripe grapes when [the grapes] grow and they become like a white bean that is called inferior.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:5)


**מִשֶּׁיָּנֵצוּ.** מִשֶּׁיִּגְדַּל עֲלֵיהֶן הַנֵּץ, כְּמוֹ הַנִּצָּנִים נִרְאוּ בָאָרֶץ (שיר השירים ב), כְּשֶׁיַּגִּיעוּ לַשִּׁעוּרִים הַלָּלוּ אָסוּר לְקָצְצָן בַּשְּׁבִיעִית:


משינצו – when it grows a blossom, like (Song of Songs 2:12): “The blossoms have appeared in the land,” when they have reached these measurements it is forbidden to cut them in the Seventh Year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:6)


**מֻתָּר לְקָצְצוֹ.** וְאֵין כָּאן מִשּׁוּם הֶפְסֵד הַפְּרִי, אוֹ מִשּׁוּם גָּזֵל לְאִידָךְ לִישְׁנָא, שֶׁכְּבָר הַפְּרִי רָאוּי לַאֲכִילָה. וּמִשּׁוּם קוֹצֵץ אִילָנוֹת טוֹבִים לֵיכָּא אִם הוּא מְעֻלֶּה בְּדָמִים, שֶׁדָּמָיו יְקָרִים לְעֵצִים יוֹתֵר מִלְּאִילָן:


מותר לקצצו – and there is nothing here because of loss of the fruit, or because of theft, to the other language, for the fruit is already appropriate for eating, and because he cuts down good trees, it is not if it of great monetary worth, for its worth is expensive for wood more than for a tree.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:7)


**וְכַמָּה יְהֵא בַזַּיִת.** לָאו אַשְּׁבִיעִית קָאֵי, אֶלָּא שֶׁיְּהֵא אָסוּר לְקָצְצוֹ מִשּׁוּם קוֹצֵץ אִילָנוֹת טוֹבִים, כְּדִכְתִיב (דברים כ) כִּי מִמֶּנּוּ תֹאכֵל וְאוֹתוֹ לֹא תִכְרֹת:


וכמה יהא בזית – It does not refer to the Seventh Year, but rather that it would be forbidden to cut it because cutting good trees, as it is written (Deuteronomy 20:19): “you may eat of them, but you must not cut them down.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 4:10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/4:10:8)


**רֹבַע.** הַקַּב, מִשּׁוּם דְּחָשִׁיב יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁאָר אִילָנוֹת, דְּאִלּוּ בְּדֶקֶל מֻתָּר לְקָצְצוֹ עַד דְּטָעִין קַבָּא:


רובע –[one quarter] of a Kab, because it is considered greater from other trees, whereas, with the palm-tree, it is permitted to cut it until It is laden with a Kab.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:1:1)


**בְּנוֹת שׁוּחַ.** תְּאֵנִים לְבָנוֹת הַמְגַדְּלוֹת מִשָּׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים לְשָׁלֹשׁ שָׁנִים:


בנות שוח (a species of white figs) – white figs that grow from every three years to another three years.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:1:2)


**שְׁבִיעִית שֶׁלָּהֶם שָׁנָה שְׁנִיָּה.** שֶׁל שְׁמִטָּה, דְפֵרוֹת הַחוֹנְטִים בַּשְּׁבִיעִית אֵין נִגְמַר בִּשּׁוּלָן עַד שָׁנָה שְׁנִיָּה שֶׁל שְׁמִטָּה שֶׁהִיא שְׁלִישִׁית לַחֲנִיטָה, וְאָז נוֹהֵג בָּהֶן דִּין שְׁבִיעִית, דְּבָתַר חֲנָטָה אָזְלִינַן:


שביעית שלהם שנה שניה – of the Shemitah cycle for fruit that assume the shape of fruits in the Seventh Yar, their ripening is not completed until the second year of Shemiah which is three years from their formation of fruit and then the law of the Seventh Year applies to them, for we go after the formation of fruit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:1:3)


**פַּרְסִיּוֹת.** מִין תְּאֵנִים שֶׁעוֹשִׂים מִשְּׁתֵּי שָׁנִים לִשְׁתֵּי שָׁנִים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


פרסיות – a species of figs that produce from two years to another two years. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:2:1)


**הַטּוֹמֵן אֶת הַלּוּף.** מִין מִמִּינֵי הַבְּצָלִים שֶׁרְגִילִים לְטָמְנָן בָּאָרֶץ:


הטומן את הלוף (he who stores LOF/ a plant with edible leaves and root, and bearing beans – classified with onions and garlic) – a kind from the species of onions that are customarily stored in the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:2:2)


**לֹא יִפְחֹת מִסָּאתַיִם.** מִשּׁוּם דְּמֵיחֲזֵי כְּזוֹרֵעַ, עַד שֶׁיִּטְמֹן מֵהֶם סָאתַיִם בַּחֲפִירָה אַחַת עַל גֹּבַהּ שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים, שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ מְפֻזָּרִים אֶלָּא יִהְיֶה גֹּבַהּ הַכְּרִי וְהַצִּבּוּר שְׁלֹשָׁה טְפָחִים, וְטֶפַח עָפָר עַל גַּבֵּי הַכְּרִי:


לא יפחתו מסאתים – because it appears like sowing, until he stores from them two Seah in one digging at the height of three handbreadths, so that they will not be scattered, but rather it will be the height of a pile and gathered three handbreadths and a handbreadth of dirt on top of the pile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:2:3)


**וְטוֹמְנוֹ בִּמְקוֹם דְּרִיסַת הָאָדָם.** כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִצְמַח. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וטומנו במקום דריסת האדם – in order that it would not grow. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:3:1)


**לוּף שֶׁעָבְרָה עָלָיו שְׁבִיעִית.** וְנִכְנְסָה עָלָיו שְׁמִינִית, שֶׁהוּא מִתְקַיֵּם זְמַן מְרֻבֶּה בָּאָרֶץ בִּמְחֻבָּר:


לוף שערבה עליו שביעית – and entered upon it the Eighth Year when it endures a long time attached in the ground.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:3:2)


**אִם לָקְטוּ עֲנִיִּים אֶת עָלָיו לָקָטוּ.** דַּעֲלֵי הַלּוּף יֵשׁ לָהֶן בִּעוּר וְעִקָּרוֹ אֵין לוֹ בִּעוּר כְּדִתְנַן לְקַמָּן בְּפֶרֶק ז', הִלְכָּךְ אִם לָקְטוּ עֲנִיִּים אֶת עָלָיו בַּשְּׁבִיעִית לָקָטוּ, וְאִם לָאו וְנִכְנְסוּ לַשְּׁמִינִית וְגָדְלוּ הֶעָלִין, נִמְצְאוּ גִּדּוּלֵי שְׁבִיעִית וּשְׁמִינִית מְעֹרָבִין יַחַד:


אם לקטו ענבים את עליו לקטו – as the leaves of he LOF/a plant with edible leaves and root, and bearing beans (of the onion species) they have removal (in the third and sixth years of the Sabbatical cycle) but if they were uprooted , they don’t have removal as is taught in the Mishnah further on in Chapter 7 (Mishnayot 1-2), therefore, if the poor gathered their leaves in the Seventh Yeaer, they gathered them, and if not, and they entered into the Eighth Year and the leaves grew, it was found that the growth of the Seventh Year and the Eighth Year were combined together.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:3:3)


**יַעֲשֶׂה חֶשְׁבּוֹן.** וִישַׁעֵר כַּמָּה גָּדַל מֵהֶן בַּשְּׁבִיעִית, וְיִתֵּן לָעֲנִיִּים, דְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר סָבַר כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה דְּאָמַר לְקַמָּן עֲנִיִּים אוֹכְלִים אַחַר בִּעוּר וְלֹא עֲשִׁירִים:


יעשה חשבון – and he should estimate how much of them grew in the Seventh Year and give it to the poor, as Rabbi Eliezer holds like Rabbi Yehuda who staed further on that the poor eat after emoval and not the rich.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:3:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר אֵין לָעֲנִיִּים עִמּוֹ חֶשְׁבּוֹן.** דְּסָבַר כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי דְּאָמַר לְקַמָּן אֶחָד עֲנִיִּים וְאֶחָד עֲשִׁירִים אוֹכְלִים אַחַר הַבִּעוּר. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ:


רבי יהושע אומר אין לעניים עמו חשבון – that he (i.e., Rabbi Yehoshua) held like Rabbi Yossi who stated further on (Tractate Sheviit, Chapter 9, Mishnah 8) that both rich and poor eat the produce after the time of the removal. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehoshua.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:4:1)


**לוּף שֶׁל עֶרֶב שְׁבִיעִית.** שֶׁנִּשְׁלַם מֵעֶרֶב שְׁבִיעִית וְלֹא הוֹסִיף צְמָחִים בַּשְּׁבִיעִית, שֶׁאִם הוֹסִיף צְמָחִים בַּשְּׁבִיעִית אָסוּר לְעָקְרוֹ מִשּׁוּם הֶפְסֵד פְּרִי:


לוף של ערב שביעית – that was completed from the eve of the Seventh Year and it didn’t add growth in the Seventh Year, for it he added growth in the Seventh Year it is forbidden to uproot it because of loss of fruit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:4:2)


**הַקֵּיצוֹנִים.** יֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים שֶׁנִּזְרְעוּ בַּקַּיִץ, וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים מְיֻחָדוֹת לַקַּיִץ שֶׁהֵן יְבֵשׁוֹת מְאֹד:


הקיצונים – there are those who interpret that they were sown in the summer, and there are those who interpret that theya re unique to the summer in that they are very dry.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:4:3)


**פּוּאָה שֶׁל עִדִּית.** הַגְּדֵלָה בְּשָׂדֶה שְׁמֵנָה. פּוּאָה, צֶבַע אָדֹם שֶׁקּוֹרִין רויי"א בְּלַעַ"ז:


פואה (dyer’s madder growing on choice land) – that grows in a rich field; פואה/dyer’s madder , the red color that we call in the foreign language ROYAH.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:4:4)


**בְּמַאֲרוּפוֹת שֶׁל עֵץ.** בְּמָרָה שֶׁחוֹפְרִים בּוֹ אֶת הַקַּרְקַע. שֶׁל עֵץ וְלֹא שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל, שֶׁלֹּא יֵרָאֶה כְּעוֹבֵד אֶת הָאָרֶץ:


במארופות של עץ (with a wooden shovel/rake) – with a hoe that they dig with it the ground, of wood, and not of iron, so that it would not appear as working the land.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:4:5)


**בְּקַרְדֻּמּוֹת שֶׁל מַתֶּכֶת.** וְלֹא חַיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא מֵחֲזֵי כַּעֲבוֹדָה:


בקרדומות של מתכת – and we aren’t concerned that perhaps that it looks like work.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:4:6)


**בְּפוּאָה שֶׁל צְלָעוֹת.** כְּמוֹ סְלָעוֹת בְּסָמֶ"ךְ, פּוּאָה הַגְּדֵלָה בֵּין הַסְּלָעִים, כֵּיוָן דְּאֵין דֶּרֶךְ לִזְרֹעַ שָׁם לֹא מֵחֲזֵי כְּעוֹבֵד אֶת הָאָרֶץ. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר צְלָעוֹת כְּמוֹ וּלְצֶלַע הַמִּשְׁכָּן (שמות כו), כְּלוֹמַר צִדֵּי הַשָּׂדֶה שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ לִזְרֹעַ שָׁם:


בפואה של צלעות – like סלעות/stony-rocky areas with the letter “SAMEKH,” madder’s dye that grows between the rocks since there is no path to sow there , it does not look like working the land. Another explanation: צלעות – like (Exodus 26:20): “And for the other side wall of the Tabernacle,” meaning to say, the sides of the field, where it is not the practice to sow there.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:5:1)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר מִיָּד.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּבִשְׁאָר יְרָקוֹת מוֹדֶה רַבִּי יְהוּדָה דְּאָסוּר עַד שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂה כַּיּוֹצֵא בוֹ, בְּלוּף הוֹאִיל וְצָרִיךְ לְעָקְרוֹ לְבֵית שַׁמַּאי בְּמַאֲרוּפוֹת שֶׁל עֵץ וּלְבֵית הִלֵּל בְּקַרְדֻּמּוֹת שֶׁל מַתָּכוֹת, וְאֵין דֶּרֶךְ לְעָקְרוֹ בַּשְּׁבִיעִית אֶלָּא עַל יְדֵי שִׁנּוּי, הִלְכָּךְ בְּמוֹצָאֵי שְׁבִיעִית לֹא שְׁכִיחַ דְּאִסּוּרָא וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי מֻתָּר מִיָּד, אֲבָל יָרָק שֶׁלֹּא גָזְרוּ שֶׁלֹּא לְתָלְשׁוֹ כִּי אוֹרְחֵיהּ שְׁכִיחַ נַמִּי דְּאִסּוּרָא בַּשְּׁמִינִית, לְפִיכָךְ אָסוּר עַד שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂה כַּיּוֹצֵא בוֹ:


רבי יהודה אומר מיד – and even though that with the rest of the vegetables, Rabbi Yehuda admits that it is prohibited until he makes something in a similar manner; the LOF (plant with edible leaves and root of the onion species) since he must uproot it, according to the School of Shammai with a wooden rake, and according to the School of Hillel with metal spade , there is no way to uproot it in the Seventh Year other than through a change of form, therefore, on the aftermath of the Seventh Year, there is no prohibition found on hand, and because of this, it is permitted immediately, but regarding vegetation where they did not decree that they should not detach it because its path is found at hand, it is also forbidden during the Eighth Year, therefore, it is prohibited until he does something in a similar manner.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:5:2)


**מִשֶּׁיִּרְבֶּה הֶחָדָשׁ.** בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי מְפָרֵשׁ דְּהַיְנוּ מִן הַפֶּסַח וְאֵילָךְ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


מירבה (בו) החדש – in the Jerusalem [Talmud] it explains that this is from Passover onwards. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:6:1)


**שֶׁאֵין הָאֻמָּן רַשַּׁאי לְמָכְרָן בַּשְּׁבִיעִית.** לְמִי שֶׁהוּא חָשׁוּד עַל הַשְּׁבִיעִית, אֲבָל לְמִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ חָשׁוּד שָׁרֵי, דְּאֵינוֹ קוֹנֶה אֶלָּא לְהַצְנִיעַ עַד [אַחַר] שְׁבִיעִית:


שאין האומן רשאי למכרן בשביעית – for someone who is suspected [of violating] on the Sabbatical/Seventh Year , but for someone who is not suspected, he perfmitted for he doesn’t acquire but rather stores it away/hides it until [after] the Seventh Year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:6:2)


**מִזְרֶה.** שֶׁזּוֹרִין בּוֹ הַתְּבוּאָה בַּגֹּרֶן:


מזרה (winnowing fan) – that scatters/winnows the grain in the granary/threshing floor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:6:3)


**דָּקָר.** כְּמִין יָתֵד שֶׁל בַּרְזֶל שֶׁקּוֹרִין קולטרו בְּלַעַ"ז, וְהוּא עָשׂוּי לַחְפֹּר בּוֹ אֶת הַקַּרְקַע, לְשׁוֹן וַיִּדְקֹר אֶת שְׁנֵיהֶם (במדבר כה):


דקר (pronged tool/mattock) – a kind of iron handle of a tool/peg is called KOLTERO in the foreign language, and it is made to dig the ground with it, such as the language (Numbers 25:8): “[he (Phinehas) followed the Israelite into the chamber] stabbed both of them [the Israelite and the woman, through the belly].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:6:4)


**מַגַּל יָד וּמַגַּל קָצִיר וַעֲגָלָה.** מֻתָּרִין, שֶׁמָּא הוּא [רוֹצֶה] לִקְצֹר מִן הַהֶפְקֵר וּלְהָבִיא בַּעֲגָלָה לְמַאֲכַל בֵּיתוֹ, וְאֵין אָסוּר אֶלָּא לְהָבִיא הַרְבֵּה לַעֲשׂוֹת אוֹצָר:


מגל יד ומגל קציר ועגלה – they are permitted [to be sold] lest he [wishes] to reap from that which is ownerless and to bring [it] in a wagon for the food of his household, but it is not prohibited other than to bring a lot to make a storehouse.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:7:1)


**הַיּוֹצֵר.** אֻמָּן הָעוֹשֶׂה כְּלֵי חֶרֶס מוֹכֵר אֲפִלּוּ לְחָשׁוּד עַל הַשְּׁבִיעִית חָמֵשׁ כַּדֵּי שֶׁמֶן וַחֲמֵשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה כַּדֵּי יַיִן, וְלֵיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ שֶׁמָּא רוֹצֶה כֻּלָּם לְשֶׁמֶן אוֹ כֻּלָּם לְיַיִן, שֶׁנִּכָּרִים הֵן אוֹתָם שֶׁל יַיִן מֵאוֹתָם שֶׁל שֶׁמֶן, שֶׁהֶעָפָר שֶׁעוֹשִׂים מִמֶּנּוּ כַּדֵּי יַיִן אֵינוֹ דוֹמֶה לֶעָפָר שֶׁעוֹשִׂים מִמֶּנּוּ כַּדֵּי שֶׁמֶן:


היוצר – an artisan who makes earthenware vessels sells even to be suspected [of violating] the Seventh Year five pitchers of oil and fifteen pitchers of wine, and there is nothing to be anxious about lest perhaps he wants all of them for oil or all of them for wine, for they are recognized – those of wine from those of oil, that the dust that the wine pitchers make is not similar to the dust that the pitchers of oil makes from them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:7:2)


**וְאִם הֵבִיא יוֹתֵר מִכָּאן מֻתָּר.** וְלֹא חַיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא מִן הַמְשֻׁמָּר דִּשְׁבִיעִית הֵבִיא:


ואם הביא יותר מכאן מותר – and we don’t concern ourselves lest from that he brought that which is preserved from the Seventh Year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:7:3)


**וּמוֹכֵר לְנָכְרִים בָּאָרֶץ.** וְלֹא חַיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא יַחֲזֹר הַנָּכְרִי וְיִמְכְּרֵם לְיִשְׂרָאֵל הֶחָשׁוּד:


ומוכר לגוים בארץ – and we don’t concern ourselves less the heathen will go back and sell them to an Israelites that is suspected [of violating the Sabbatical year].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:7:4)


**וּלְיִשְׂרָאֵל בְּחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ.** וְלֹא חַיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא יוֹלִיכֵם בָּאָרֶץ, אִי נַמִּי שֶׁמָּא יַכְנִיס לְתוֹכָן יַיִן וְשֶׁמֶן שֶׁל שְׁבִיעִית שֶׁהֵבִיא מֵאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, דְּלֹא הֶחְמִירוּ חֲכָמִים כָּל כָּךְ:


ולישראל בחוצה לארץ – and we don’t concern ourselves lest he bring them into the Land [of Israel]; alternatively, lest he place within them wine or oil of the Seventh Yar that he brought from the Land of Israel, for the Sages were not that stringent.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:8:1)


**שֶׁהוּא יָכוֹל לְשָׁחֲטָהּ.** וּבְכָל דְּהוּא דְּמָצִינַן לְמִתְלֵי תָּלִינַן. וּבֵית שַׁמַּאי סָבְרֵי דְּאֵין דֶּרֶךְ כְּלָל לִשְׁחֹט פָּרָה הָעוֹמֶדֶת לַחֲרִישָׁה:


שהוא יכול לשחטה – and in whatever that we are able to leave in suspense, we do so. But the School of Shammai holds that it is not the practice at all to slaughter a cow that is about to engage in plowing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:8:2)


**אֲפִלּוּ בִּשְׁעַת הַזֶּרַע.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן וַדַּאי לִזְרִיעָה הוּא לוֹקֵחַ, דְּתָלִינַן לַאֲכִילָה הוּא רוֹצֶה:


אפילו בשעת הזרע – and we don’t say that definitely he purchases it for sowing for he wants that we assign it for eating.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:8:3)


**שֶׁיֶּשׁ לוֹ גֹּרֶן.** וְלֹא חַיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא מוֹדֵד לְהַכְנִיס לָאוֹצָר, דְּאִיכָּא לְמִתְלֵי דְּלִטְחִינָה הוּא מוֹדֵד:


שיש לו גורן – and we are not concerned lest he measures it to bring into the store house, for one can designate it for grinding it that he measures it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:8:4)


**וּפוֹרֵט לוֹ מָעוֹת.** מַחֲלִיף לוֹ מָעוֹת בִּפְרוּטוֹת:


ופורט לו מעות – he exchanges money with pennies.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:8:5)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁיֶּשׁ לוֹ פּוֹעֲלִים.** וְנִמְצָא מְסַיֵּעַ יְדֵי עוֹבְרֵי עֲבֵרָה, דְּאָמְרִינַן שֶׁמָּא לִשְׁאָר צְרָכָיו הוּא צָרִיךְ:


אע"פ שהוא יודע שיש לו פועלים – and it is found that he is assisting in transgressing [working the fields on the Sabbatical Year] , that we say lest for the rest of his needs he requires it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:8:6)


**וְכֻלָּם.** אִם פֵּרֵשׁ שֶׁלְּדָבָר אָסוּר רוֹצֶה אוֹתָם, אָסוּר:


וכולם – if they explained that for a forbidden matter he wants them, it is forbidden.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:1)


**הַחֲשׁוּדָה עַל הַשְּׁבִיעִית.** לֶאֱכֹל פֵּרוֹת שְׁבִיעִית אַחַר הַבִּעוּר בְּלֹא בִעוּר:


החשודה על השביעית – to eat the fruit/produce of the Seventh Year after the removal [of the fruits of the third and sixth years of the Sabbatical cycle – see also Deuteronomy 26:12-14).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:2)


**נָפָה.** שֶׁאֲנִי אוֹמֵר לִסְפֹּר בָּהּ מָעוֹת הִיא לוֹקַחַת:


נפה (sieve/winnow) – that I say to count in it money and she purchases it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:3)


**כְּבָרָה.** שֶׁאֲנִי אוֹמֵר לִכְבֹּר בָּהּ אֶת הַחֹל:


כברה (basket used as a sieve) – I say [to her] to sift the sand in it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:4)


**רֵחַיִם.** דְּשֶׁמָּא לִטְחֹן בָּהֶם סַמָּנִין:


רחיים (millstone) – for perhaps to grind them ingredients of frankincense.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:5)


**תַּנּוּר.** שֶׁמָּא לְיַבֵּשׁ בּוֹ אוּנִין שֶׁל פִּשְׁתָּן:


תנור – lest to dry in it bundles of wet flax.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:6)


**וְלֹא תִטְחַן עִמָּהּ.** שֶׁאָסוּר לְסַיֵּעַ יְדֵי עוֹבְרֵי עֲבֵרָה:


ולא תטחן עמה – for it is prohibited to assist the hands of those who transgress [the law of not working the land in the Seventh Year].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:7)


**וּבוֹרֶרֶת וְטוֹחֶנֶת עִמָּהּ.** וְלֹא אָסְרוּ הָכָא בְּאֵשֶׁת עַם הָאָרֶץ הַחֲשׁוּדָה עַל הַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת כִּדְאָסְרוּ בַּחֲשׁוּדָה עַל הַשְּׁבִיעִית, מִשּׁוּם דְּרֹב עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ מְעַשְּׂרִים הֵן:


ובוררת וטוחנת עמה – they did not prohibit here regarding a woman who is illiterate who is suspected illiterate people tithe.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:8)


**אֲבָל מִשֶּׁתַּטִּיל הַמַּיִם.** בָּעִסָּה:


אבל משתטיל המים –with started dough.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:9)


**לֹא תִגַּע עִמָּהּ.** לְפִי שֶׁמִּשֶּׁגִּלְגְּלָה הֻטְבְּלָה לְחַלָּה וְקָא מִטַּמְּאָה מֵחֲמַת כֵּלִים טְמֵאִים, שֶׁהֲרֵי הֻכְשְׁרָה לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, וְזוֹ מְסַיַּעְתָּהּ לְגַלְגֵּל, וְאָסוּר לִגְרֹם טֻמְאָה לְחַלָּה:


לא תגע עמה – because once she [formed the dough by] rolling, it was immersed for Hallah, and she has deiled it on account of impure utensils because they became susceptible to receive Levitical impurity, and this one helps her to roll, and it is prohibited to cause ritual impurity to Hallah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:10)


**וְכֻלָּן לֹא אָמְרוּ.** לֹא הִתִּירוּ לְהַשְׁאִילָן כֵּלִים וּלְסַיְּעָן בְּלֹא שְׁעַת עֲבֵרָה עַצְמָהּ אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם דַּרְכֵי שָׁלוֹם:


וכולן לא אמרו – they did not permit to loan them utensils and to assist them not at the time of the transgression itself but rather because of the ways of peace.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 5:9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/5:9:11)


**מַחֲזִיקִין יְדֵי נָכְרִים.** אַגַּב גְּרָרָא דְדַרְכֵי שָׁלוֹם תָּנָא לֵיהּ הָכָא, וּמְפֹרָשׁ לְעֵיל בְּפֶרֶק ד':


מחזיקין ידי נכרים – incidentally the Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah] taught the ways of peace here, and it is explained above in Chapter 4 (Mishnah 3 of Tractate Sheviit).



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:1:1)


**שָׁלֹשׁ אֲרָצוֹת לַשְּׁבִיעִית.** חֲלוּקוֹת לְעִנְיַן דִּין שְׁבִיעִית:


שלש ארצות לשביעית – they are divided for the matter of the law of the Seventh Year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:1:2)


**כָּל שֶׁהֶחֱזִיקוּ עוֹלֵי בָבֶל.** עֶזְרָא וְסִיעָתוֹ כְּשֶׁעָלוּ מִבָּבֶל:


כל שהחזיקו עולי בבל (all-of the land- that was occupied by those who returned from Babylonia)- Ezra and his followers when they came up from Babylonia.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:1:3)


**לֹא נֶאֱכָל וְלֹא נֶעֱבָד.** שֶׁעֲבוֹדַת הָאָרֶץ הַהִיא אֲסוּרָה בַּשְּׁבִיעִית, וְאִם נֶעֶבְדָה וְגִדְּלָה אָסוּר לֶאֱכֹל מֵאוֹתָן גִּדּוּלִים. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר וְעִקָּר, לֹא נֶאֱכָל לְאַחַר שְׁבִיעִית בְּלֹא בִעוּר, וְלֹא נֶעֱבָד בַּשְּׁבִיעִית:


לא נאכל ולא נעבד (may not be eaten, nor the land cultivated) – for the working of this land is forbidden in the Seventh Year, and if it is worked and it grows, it is prohibited to eat from those things that grow. Another explanation, and this is the essence, it is not eaten after the Seventh Year without removal and is not cultivated in the Seventh Year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:1:4)


**מִכְּזִיב וְעַד הַנָּהָר וְעַד אַמְנוֹם.** כְּזִיב מָשׁוּךְ כְּלַפֵּי הַצָּפוֹן לְצַד מִזְרָח, וּרְצוּעָה יוֹצֵאת מֵעַכּוֹ שֶׁיּוֹשֶׁבֶת בְּרִבּוּעָהּ שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּמִקְצוֹעַ צְפוֹנִית מִזְרָחִית, וְאוֹתָהּ רְצוּעָה מְשׁוּכָה כְּלַפֵּי צָפוֹן וְהוֹלֶכֶת עַד כְּזִיב, וּרְצוּעָה אַחֶרֶת מְשׁוּכָה מֵעַכּוֹ כְּלַפֵּי מִזְרָח וּבָהּ הַנָּהָר הָאָמוּר כָּאן, וְאֵלּוּ הַשְּׁתֵּי רְצוּעוֹת כְּבָשׁוּם עוֹלֵי מִצְרַיִם וְלֹא כְבָשׁוּם עוֹלֵי בָבֶל:


מכזיב ועד הנהר ועד אננום – Kezib is in a continuous line/stretched towards the north to the side of the east, and a strip goes out from Acre/Akko that sits/dwells on a square of the Land of Israel on the northeastern angle and that strip is drawn/stretched towards the north and goes until Kezib, and another strip is stretched from Acre/Akko towards the east and in it is the river mentioned here, and these two strips were conquered by those who came up from Egypt, and were not conquered by those who came up from Babylonia (following the first Exile in 586-516 BCE).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:1:5)


**וְעַד אַמְנוֹם.** הוּא הֹר הָהָר הַכָּתוּב בִּגְבוּלוֹת הָאָרֶץ בְּפָרָשַׁת אֵלֶּה מַסְעֵי, תְּתָאוּ לָכֶם הֹר הָהָר מְתַרְגְּמִינַן בַּיְרוּשַׁלְמִי טוּרֵי אַמְנוֹם, וְהוּא בִּצְפוֹנָהּ שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְצַד מַעֲרָב אֵצֶל הַיָּם הַגָּדוֹל, וְגַם זוֹ לֹא כָבְשׁוּ עוֹלֵי בָבֶל, הִלְכָּךְ נֶאֱכָל לְאַחַר הַבִּעוּר, וּלְאִידָךְ לִישְׁנָא אִם נֶעֱבָד בַּשְּׁבִיעִית נֶאֱכָל, דִּקְדֻשָּׁה רִאשׁוֹנָה קָדְשָׁה לִשְׁעָתָהּ וְלֹא קָדְשָׁה לֶעָתִיד לָבֹא, וְלֹא נֶעֱבָד, דִּלְעִנְיַן עֲבוֹדָה הֶחְמִירוּ לֶאֱסֹר מִדְּרַבָּנָן:


ועד אמנום – this is Mount Hor that is written in the boundaries of the Land [of Israel] in the Torah portion אלה מסעי/”These were the marches [of the Israelites],” (Numbers 34:8): “From Mount Hor draw a line [to Lebo-Hamath, and let the boundary reach Zedad],” we translate in Aramaic in the Jerusalem [Talmud] the Mount of Amamum, and it is in the north of Land of Israel to the western side near the Great Sea (i.e., the Mediterranean Sea), and this also, those who came up from Babylonia did not conquer, therefore, it is eaten after the removal, and regarding the other language, if it (i.e., that land) was cultivated in the Seventh Year, it can be eaten, for the first holiness was sanctified only for that particular time, but was not sanctified for the future [days] to come , and is not worked, and regarding working [the land]/cultivation, they were more stringent to prohibit than the Rabbis.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:1:6)


**מִנָּהָר וּמֵאַמְנוֹם וְלִפְנִים.** כָּךְ כָּתוּב בְּכָל הַסְּפָרִים, וּטְפֵי הֲוָה נִיחָא אִי הֲוָה גָרְסִינַן וְלַחוּץ. וְאֶפְשָׁר לְפָרֵשׁ בְּדֹחַק מֵהַנָּהָר וְלִפְנִים מִתְּחִלַּת הַנָּהָר וְלִפְנִים לְצַד הַנָּהָר חוּצָה לָאָרֶץ, וְכֵן מִתְּחִלַּת טוּרֵי אַמְנוֹם וְלִפְנִים לְצַד הָהָר קְרוּיָה לִפְנִים:


מנהר ומאמנום ולפנים – such is written in all the books, and more, would be pleasant if we had had the reading "ולחוץ"/gong to outside [the Land]/ But it is possible to interpret in a forced manner “from the river and beyond – to the side of the river outward to the Land, and similarly, from the beginning of Mount Amanum and beyond to the side of the Mount is called “beyond/inside.”



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:2:1)


**סוּרְיָא.** אֲרַם נַהֲרַיִם וַאֲרַם צוֹבָה שֶׁכָּבַשׁ דָּוִד, וְאֵינָהּ כְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְכָל דָּבָר, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁכְּבָשָׁהּ קֹדֶם שֶׁכָּבַשׁ אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל כֻּלָּהּ, וּבִקְצַת דִּינִים עֲשָׂאוּהָ כְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל וּבִקְצָתָן כְּחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ:


עושים בתלוש – even with those who are suspected [of violating the Seventh Year by working the land], whereas in the Land of Israel, it is taught in the Mishnah (Tractate Sheviit, Chapter 5, Mishnah 9), that “she may not sift or grind [flour] with her [since the grain was gathered in violation of the law].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:2:2)


**עוֹשִׂים בְּתָלוּשׁ.** אֲפִלּוּ אֵצֶל הַחֲשׁוּדִים, וְאִלּוּ בְאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל תְּנַן לֹא תָבוֹר וְלֹא תִטְחַן עִמָּהּ:


סוריא – Aram Naharayim and Aram Tzovah that David conquered, and it is not like the Land of Israel for all matters because it was conquered before he (i.e., David) conquered the entire land of Israel, and in some of the laws, it was made to be like the Land of Israel and in some of them, like the Diaspora/outside the Land of Israel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:2:3)


**אֲבָל לֹא בַמְחֻבָּר.** כְּגוֹן מְסִיקָה קְצִירָה וּבְצִירָה, וְדַוְקָא בַּשָּׁמוּר, אֲבָל בַּמֻּפְקָר אֲפִלּוּ בָּאָרֶץ מֻתָּר, דְּתַנְיָא בְּתוֹרַת כֹּהֲנִים מִן הַשָּׁמוּר אִי אַתָּה בּוֹצֵר אֲבָל אַתָּה בּוֹצֵר מִן הַמֻּפְקָר. וְטַעֲמָא דְגָזוּר בְּסוּרְיָא בִּמְחֻבָּר כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ מַנִּיחִים אֶת אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל וְהוֹלְכִים וּמִשְׁתַּקְּעִים בְּסוּרְיָא, וּבְתָלוּשׁ הִתִּירוּ, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּמְצְאוּ הָעֲנִיִּים שֶׁבְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל קְצַת רֶוַח בְּסוּרְיָא שֶׁהִיא קְרוֹבָה לָהֶם, וְלֹא יִצְטָרְכוּ לָצֵאת מֵאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ:


אבל לא במחובר – as for example, harvesting olives, harvesting [grain] and harvesting grapes, and especially that which is guarded, but that which is declared ownerless, even in the Land [of Israel], it is permitted, as it is taught in Torat Kohanim (the Halakhic Midrash on the Book of Leviticus), from that which is guarded/preserved, you cannot harvest grapes, but you may harvest grapes from that which is declared ownerless, and the reason that they decreed in Syria regarding that which is attached, in order that they should not leave the Land of Israel and reside in Syria, and regarding that which is detached, they permitted in order that the poor people that are in the land of Israel will find a bit of room/space in Syria which is close by to them, and they won’t have to leave from the Land of Israel to the Diaspora/outside the Land of Israel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:2:4)


**כָּל שֶׁכַּיּוֹצֵא בוֹ מֻתָּר בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל.** מִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא, כְּגוֹן תָּלוּשׁ, אַף עַל גַּב דְּמִדְּרַבָּנָן אָסוּר בְּלֹא שִׁנּוּי, עוֹשִׂין אוֹתוֹ בְּסוּרְיָא כִּי אוֹרְחֵיהּ וְאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ לְשַׁנּוֹת. אִי נַמִּי הָכִי קָאָמַר, כָּל שֶׁכַּיּוֹצֵא בוֹ מֻתָּר בָּאָרֶץ בַּמֻּפְקָר עוֹשִׂים אוֹתוֹ בְּסוּרְיָא אֲפִלּוּ בַּשָּׁמוּר, אֲבָל בִּמְחֻבָּר אָסוּר מִדְּרַבָּנָן בָּאָרֶץ אֲפִלּוּ בַּמֻּפְקָר:


כל שכיוצא בו מותר בא"י – according to the Torah, such as for example, something detached, even though from the Rabbis, it is prohibited without a change of form we perform it in Syria just as we normally do and we don’t need to change anything. Alternatively, this is what he said: All that is in a similar manner is permitted in the Land [of Israel] with something declared ownerless, they perform it in Syria, even with something guarded/preserved, but that which is attached is prohibited from the Rabbis in the Land [of Israel] even when it is declared ownerless.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:3:1)


**בְּצָלִים שֶׁיָּרְדוּ עֲלֵיהֶם גְּשָׁמִים.** בְּצָלִים שֶׁל שִׁשִּׁית שֶׁנִּכְנְסוּ לַשְּׁבִיעִית, וְיָרְדוּ עֲלֵיהֶם גְּשָׁמִים וְצָמְחוּ, אִם הִשְׁחִירוּ הֶעָלִין בְּיָדוּעַ שֶׁגָּדְלוּ וְהוֹסִיפוּ, וַאֲתוּ גִּדּוּלֵי שְׁבִיעִית וּמְבַטְּלִים הָעִקָּר אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא מְרֻבֶּה. אֲבָל אִם הוֹרִיקוּ הֶעָלִין אָז הוֹלְכִים וּכְמֵשִׁין וְלָאו מֵאַרְעָא קָא רָבוּ:


בצלים שירדו עליהם גשמים – onions of the Sixth [Year] which entered into the Seventh [Year, and rain fell upon them and they grew, if the leaves blackened with the knowledge that they grew and added, and the growth of the Seventh Year came and nullified the essence, even though it is a lot. But if the leaves became green, they we go and they wither and it is not from the Land [of Israel] that they increased.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:3:2)


**אִם יְכוֹלִין לִיתָּלֵשׁ בֶּעָלִין.** דֶּרֶךְ בְּצָלִים כְּשֶׁנִּגְמָרִים יוֹצְאִין מֵעַצְמָן מִן הָאָרֶץ וְנוֹחִין לִתָּלֵשׁ, וְאִם הֶעָלִין חֲזָקִים וּכְשֶׁאוֹחֵז בָּהֶן יָכוֹל לִתְלֹשׁ הַבְּצָלִים מִן הָאָרֶץ, אָז וַדַּאי גָּדְלוּ בַשְּׁבִיעִית:


אם יכולין ליתלש בעלין – it is the manner of onions when they are compee that leave on their own from the land and are easy to detach, and if the leaves are strong, and when you grab hold of them, you are able to detach the onions from the ground, then they have certainly grown during the Seventh Year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:3:3)


**כְּנֶגֶד כֵּן לְמוֹצָאֵי שְׁבִיעִית מֻתָּרִים.** לָאו בִּבְצָלִים שֶׁל שְׁבִיעִית שֶׁיָּצְאוּ לְמוֹצָאֵי שְׁבִיעִית מַיְרֵי, דְּלֹא מְקִילִינַן בְּהוּ כֻּלֵּי הַאי שֶׁגִּדּוּלֵי הֶתֵּר כָּל שֶׁהוּ יַעֲלוּ אֶת הָאִסּוּר, אֶלָּא סֵיפָא נַמִּי מַיְרֵי בִּבְצָלִים שֶׁל שִׁשִּׁית שֶׁעֲקָרָן בַּשִּׁשִּׁית וְחָזַר וּנְטָעָן בַּשְּׁבִיעִית, וּלְאַחַר שֶׁגָּדְלוּ מְעַט בַּשְּׁבִיעִית עֲקָרָן וְחָזַר וּנְטָעָן בַּשְּׁמִינִית, וּמִתּוֹךְ שֶׁכְּבָר גָּדְלוּ בַּשִּׁשִּׁית לֹא הוֹסִיפוּ כִּי אִם מְעַט בַּשְּׁבִיעִית וְנֶאֶסְרוּ, וּכְשֶׁחָזְרוּ וְהוֹסִיפוּ מְעַט בַּשְּׁמִינִית חָזַר אוֹתוֹ הֶתֵּר וּבִטֵּל אִסּוּר הַמֻּעָט, שֶׁגַּם שֶׁל שִׁשִּׁית מְסַיֵּעַ לְבַטְּלוֹ אַחַר שֶׁסּוֹף גִּדּוּלוֹ הֶתֵּר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי חֲנַנְיָא בֶן אַנְטִיגְנוֹס:


כנגד כן למוצאי שביעית מותרים – we are not speaking about onions of the Seventh Year that went out to the aftermath of the Seventh Year, fo we ae not lenient with them all that much, for the permitted growth of a little bit will raise the prohibition, but the ending clause [of the Mishnah] also speaks regarding onions of the Sixth Year that were uprooted in the Sixth Year and then return and were replanted in the Seventh Year, and after they grew a little bit in the Seventh Year, he uprooted them and replanted them in the Eighth Year, and because they had grown already in the Sixth Year, they did n’t add even a little bit in the Seventh Year, and they were prohibited, as when they returned and added a little bit in the Eighth Year, he retracted that permission and nullified the minute prohibition, for also that of the Sixth Year assisted to nullify it the end of permitted growh. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Hanina ben Antigonos.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:4:1)


**מִשֶּׁיַּעֲשֶׂה כַיּוֹצֵא בוֹ.** מִמַּה שֶׁנִּזְרַע בְּמוֹצָאֵי שְׁבִיעִית יֵשׁ גִּדּוּל כַּיּוֹצֵא בָזֶה דְתָלִינַן בֵּיהּ. אִי נַמִּי מִפְּנֵי שֶׁרָבָה הַהֶתֵּר עַל הָאִסּוּר וְנִתְבַּטֵּל:


משיעשה כיוצא בו – from what is sown on the aftermath of the Seventh Year there is growth in a similar manner that we suspend concerning it; alternatively, because the permit is greaterthan the prohibition and it was abolished.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:4:2)


**הַבַּכִּיר.** מָקוֹם שֶׁמְּמַהֵר לְבַכֵּר פֵּרוֹתָיו יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁאָר מְקוֹמוֹת:


הבכיר – a place that its fruit ripens more quickly than in the rest of the places.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:4:3)


**הֻתַּר הָאָפִיל.** דְתָלִינַן שֶׁמִּמְּקוֹם הַבַּכִּיר הֱבִיאוּם:


הותר האפל – for we assign it that he brought them from the place of those that ripen early.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:4:4)


**מוֹצָאֵי שְׁבִיעִית מִיָּד.** לְפִי שֶׁהָיוּ מְבִיאִים יָרָק מִחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ וְהָיָה רָבֶה עַל יָרָק שֶׁבְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל. וַאֲנִי שָׁמַעְתִּי הַטַּעַם לְפִי שֶׁבִּשְׁנַיִם אוֹ שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים עוֹשֶׂה כַּיּוֹצֵא בוֹ:


מוצאי שביעית מיד – because they would thing vegetables from outside the Land [of Israel] and it was greater than the vegetation of the Land of Israel. But I heard the reason since that in two or three days, [the new crop] of the same vegetable has become ripe.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:5:1)


**שֶׁמֶן שְׂרֵפָה.** שֶׁמֶן שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה טְמֵאָה, מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁלִּשְׂרֵפָה עוֹמֵד קָרֵי לֵיהּ שֶׁמֶן שְׂרֵפָה, וּמַעֲלָה עָשׂוּ לִשְׂרֵפָה בָּאָרֶץ, כִּדְאַשְׁכְּחָן בְּקָדָשִׁים פְּסוּלִים שֶׁהֵן נִשְׂרָפִים בִּמְקוֹמָן, וְכֵן פֵּרוֹת שְׁבִיעִית צְרִיכִים בִּעוּר בָּאָרֶץ, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כה) בְּאַרְצֶךָ תִּהְיֶה כָל תְּבוּאָתָהּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


שמן שריפה – impure oil of heave-offering since it is fit for burning, they call it “burning oil,” and hey made an preference in the Land [of Israel] for burning, as we find in the ritually unfit holy things that are burned in their places, and similarly, the fruit/produce of the Seventh Year requires removal in the Land [of Israel] as it is written (Leviticus 25:7): “[and your cattle and the bests] in your land may eat all its yield. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/6:6:1)


**אֵין מְבִיאִין תְּרוּמָה מִחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ לָאָרֶץ.** לְפִי שֶׁהַתְּרוּמָה כֹּהֲנִים מְחַזְּרִין אַחֲרֶיהָ לְבֵית הַגְּרָנוֹת, חַיְשֵׁי רַבָּנָן שֶׁמָּא יִהְיוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים מְרַדְּפִים אַחֲרֶיהָ וְיֵצְאוּ חוּצָה לָאָרֶץ לְהָבִיא אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


אין מביאין תרומה מחוצה לארץ לארץ – since the heave-offering/Terumah, which Kohanim search after it to the granaries/threshing floors, the Rabis wee concerned that the Kohanim would pursue after it and they would go outside the Land [of Israel] to bring the Terumah. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:1)


**כְּלָל גָּדוֹל.** וּלְדָמָיו שְׁבִיעִית. אִם מְכָרָן וְקִבֵּל הַדָּמִים שֶׁלָּהֶם, יֵשׁ בִּדְמֵיהֶן קְדֻשַּׁת שְׁבִיעִית:


כלל גדול. ולדמיו שביעית – if he sold them and received their value in payment, their monetary value has the holiness of Seventh Year [produce].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:2)


**יֶשׁ לוֹ בִּעוּר.** כְּשֶׁכָּלָה לַחַיָּה חַיָּב לְבַעֵר:


יש לו ביעור – when it is consumed by the wildlife/beasts of chase, he is obligated in its removal [from the house].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:3)


**עֲלֵה לוּף שׁוֹטֶה.** אִצְטָרִיךְ לְמִתְנֵי לוּף שׁוֹטֶה מִשּׁוּם דִּתְנַן בְּפֶרֶק בַּתְרָא דְּעֻקְּצִים עֲלֵה לוּף שׁוֹטֶה אֵין מִטַּמֵּא טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִים, סַלְקָא דַעְתָּךְ אֲמֵינָא הָכִי נַמִּי לֹא לֵחוֹל עֲלַיְהוּ קְדֻשַּׁת שְׁבִיעִית, קָמַשְׁמַע לָן:


עלה לוף שוטה (leaves of the wild LOF – species of onion) – it is necessary to teach the leaves of the wild LOF, because it is taught in the concluding chapter of [Tractate] Uktzin (Chapter 3, Mishnah 4), that they do not defile with ritual uncleanness as food [until they are sweetened], you might think that I would say that the holiness of the Seventh Year do not take effect, it comes to tell us [that this is not the case].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:4)


**הַדַּנְדַּנָּה.** בַּעֲרָבִי נענ"ע וּבְלַעַ"ז מינטו"א:


הדנדנה (dandana/mint) – in Arabic NAANAH, and in the foreign language MINTO (i.e., mint).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:5)


**הָעֻלְשִׁין.** תְּרֵי גַוְנֵי עֻלְשִׁין הֵן, עֻלְשֵׁי גִנָּה וְעֻלְשֵׁי שָׂדֶה, וּבִשְׁאָר שָׁנִים דִּשְׁכִיחֵי עֻלְשֵׁי גִנָּה, עֻלְשֵׁי שָׂדֶה לֹא חֲשִׁיבֵי אֹכֶל וְאֵין מִטַּמֵּא טֻמְאַת אָכֳלִים, וְהָכָא קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּבַשְּׁבִיעִית דְּאֵין עֻלְשֵׁי גִנָּה מְצוּיִין, עֻלְשֵׁי שָׂדֶה חֲשִׁיבֵי וּמִטַּמְּאִין טֻמְאַת אָכֳלִים וּשְׁבִיעִית נוֹהֶגֶת בָּהֶן:


העולשין (endives) – there are two kinds of endives: garden endives and field endives, and in the rest of the years where garden endives are available, field endives are not considered as food and do not defile with ritual uncleanness as food and here it comes to tell us that in the Seventh Year where garden endives are not found, field endives are considered [as food] and defile with ritual uncleanness as food and the [laws of] the Seventh Year are practiced with them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:6)


**וּכְרֵשִׁין.** נַמִּי תְּרֵי גַוְנֵי נִינְהוּ, כְּרֵשֵׁי גִנָּה וּכְרֵשֵׁי שָׂדֶה, וּכְרֵשֵׁי שָׂדֶה אִצְטָרִיךְ לְאַשְׁמוּעִינַן:


וכרישין (leeks) – also there are two kinds of leeks – garden leeks and field leeks, but field leeks it was necessary to inform us about.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:7)


**הָרְגִילָה.** הֵם חֲלוֹגְלוֹגוֹת וּבְלַעַ"ז בירדולגי"ש:


הרגילה – they are purslane, and in the foreign language, BIRDUGLISH.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:8)


**וְנֵץ הֶחָלָב.** פְּרָחִים לְבָנִים כְּחָלָב. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר עֵשֶׂב הוּא כְּשֶׁנֶּחְתָּךְ יוֹצֵא מִמֶּנּוּ חָלָב:


ונץ החלב – white flowers like milk. Another explanation: it is a grass that when it is cut milk comes out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:9)


**הַחוֹחִים וְהַדַּרְדָּרִים.** מִינֵי קוֹצִים הֵן:


החוחים והדרדרים (thorns and thistles/artichokes) – they are kinds of thorns.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:10)


**אִסְטִיס.** הוּא שֶׁצּוֹבְעִין בּוֹ כְּמִין תְּכֵלֶת, וְקוֹרִים לוֹ בַּעֲרָבִי ני"ל, וְקוֹצְרִים אוֹתוֹ וְהוּא צוֹמֵחַ וְדוֹמֶה לְסָפִיחַ, לְכָךְ קוֹרֵהוּ הַתַּנָּא סְפִיחֵי אִסְטִיס:


אסטיס (woad) – that they dye with it like Tekhelet/bluish dye – and it is called NEEL in Arabic, and they harvest it and it grows and it is similar to spontaneous after-growth; for this reason, the Tanna/Teacher calls them ספיחי אסטיס/aftergrowth of woad.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:11)


**וְקוֹצָה.** מִין מִמִּינֵי הַצְּבָעִים, יֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים שֶׁהוּא אלעצפו"ר בַּעֲרָבִי:


וקוצה (madder – a plant used in dyeing red) – one of a kind of dyes and there are those who say that it is ALATZPOR in Arabic.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:12)


**יֶשׁ לָהֶן שְׁבִיעִית.** קְדֻשַּׁת שְׁבִיעִית נוֹהֶגֶת בָּהֶן שֶׁלֹּא לְהַפְסִידָם וְשֶׁלֹּא לַעֲשׂוֹת בָּהֶם סְחוֹרָה:


יש להן שביעית – the holiness of Seventh Year [produce] is practiced with them, not to cause them loss and not to do business with them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:1:13)


**וְיֶשׁ לָהֶם בִּעוּר.** כְּשֶׁיַּגִּיעַ זְמַן הַבִּעוּר צָרִיךְ לְבַעֲרָם, וּלְקַמָּן בִּפְנִים מְפֹרָשִׁים דִּינֵי הַבִּעוּר:


ויש להם ביעור – when the time arrives for removal, one must remove them [from the house – in years three and six] as will be explained inside the laws of removal (Chapter 9, Mishnah 2).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:1)


**עִקָּר.** שֹׁרֶשׁ:


עיקר – root.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:2)


**עַרְקַבְנִין.** רַבּוֹתַי מְפָרְשִׁים עֵשֶׂב הַגָּדֵל אֵצֶל הַדֶּקֶל וְעוֹלֶה וְנִכְרָךְ סְבִיבוֹת הַדֶּקֶל. וַאֲנִי שָׁמַעְתִּי שֶׁהוּא עֵשֶׂב שֶׁעָלִין שֶׁלּוֹ דּוֹמוֹת לְעַקְרָב:


ערקבלין (palm-ivy/prickly creepers) – my Rabbis explain this as grass that grows near the palm tree and rises and is attached around the palm. But I heard that it is grass that its leaves are similar to a scorpion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:3)


**וְהַחַלְבִצִין.** הַשָּׁרָשִׁים שֶׁל נֵץ חָלָב הָאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה:


והחלבצין (bulb of Ornithogalum) – the roots of the Ornithogalum that was mentioned above (in Mishnah 1 of this chapter).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:4)


**הַבֻּכְרִיָּה.** קוֹרִין לוֹ בַּעֲרָבִי כנג"ר:


הבכרייה (hazelwort/spike-nard – an aromatic plant) – they call it KANGAR in Arabic.
