###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:5)


**הַפּוּאָה.** בַּעֲרָבִי פו"א וּבְלַעַ"ז זרויי"א:


הפואה (dyer’s madder) – in Arabic, PUAH, and in the foreign language, ZRUYAH.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:6)


**הָרִכְפָּה.** שָׁרָשִׁים שֶׁתַּחַת הַקַּרְקַע, וְשׁוֹלְקִין אוֹתָן וְשׁוֹתִין מֵימֵיהֶן, וּמִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ תּוֹלָעִים בְּבִטְנוֹ יְמִיתֵם:


הרכפה (tuberous-rooted plant used for dyeing/a kind of onion) – roots that are under the ground and they seethe it and drink from them their oil, and these are fit as an antidote to worms in the stomach.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:7)


**אֵין לָהֶם בִּעוּר.** לְפִי שֶׁמִּתְקַיְּמִים בָּאָרֶץ וְלֹא כָלֶה לַחַיָּה מִן הַשָּׂדֶה לְפִיכָךְ אֵין צָרִיךְ לְבַעֲרוֹ מִן הַבַּיִת:


אין להם ביעור – because they continue to grow in the ground (as perennials) and they aren’t consumed by the beasts of chase/wildlife in the field, therefore, there is no need to remove them from the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:8)


**דְּמֵיהֶם מִתְבַּעֲרִים עַד רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה.** הָכִי קָאָמַר דְּמֵיהֶם צָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּתְבַּעֲרוּ מִן הָעוֹלָם קֹדֶם רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה שֶׁל שְׁמִינִית. וְהוּא הַדִּין דַּהֲוֵי מָצֵי לְמִתְנֵי מִתְבַּעֲרִים אַחַר רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה, שֶׁאִם יֶשְׁנָן לְאוֹתָן דָּמִים בָּעוֹלָם אַחַר רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה שֶׁל שְׁמִינִית חַיָּב לְבַעֲרָם בִּקְדֻשַּׁת שְׁבִיעִית:


דמיהם מתבערים עד ר"ה – this is what he said: their monetary valu needs to be reoved from the world prior to Rosh Hashanah of the Eighth Year, and the same law applies that they had to teach in the Mishnah that they remove it it after Rosh Hashanah if there is anything of those funds in the world after Rosh Hashanah of the Eighth Year, one is obligated to remove them in the holiness of the Seventh Year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:2:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:2:9)


**קַל וָחֹמֶר לִדְמֵיהֶן.** וְרַבִּי מֵאִיר סָבַר מַחְמִיר אֲנִי בִּדְמֵיהֶן יוֹתֵר מִבָּהֶן עַצְמָן, דִּפְרִי עַצְמוֹ מִנְכָּר וְלֹא אָתֵי לְמֵעֲבֵד בֵּיהּ סְחוֹרָה, אֲבָל דָּמִים לֹא מִנְכְּרֵי וְאָתֵי לְמֵעֲבֵד בֵּיהּ סְחוֹרָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


ק"ו לדמיהן – But Rabbi Meir held: I am stringent regarding their monies (from the sale of the produce) more than the actual produce itself, for the produce/fruit is recognized that it would not be used for commerce, but money is not recognized and one might come to use it for commerce. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:1)


**וְהַנֵּץ שֶׁלּוֹ.** פֶּרַח שֶׁעַל הַפִּטְמָא:


והנץ שלו – the flower/blossom that is on the upper portion a frui/protuberance on the blossom-end of fruits (having the appearance of a pestle seated in a mortar).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:2)


**וּקְלִפֵּי אֱגוֹזִים.** כָּל הַנֵּי צוֹבְעִים בָּהֶן:


וקליפי אגוזים – all of these are one dyes with them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:3)


**וְהַגַּלְעִינִים.** גַּרְעִינֵי פֵרוֹת, כְּגוֹן הַגַּרְעִינִים שֶׁבַּזֵּיתִים שֶׁמּוֹצִיאִים מֵהֶן שֶׁמֶן, וְגַרְעִינֵי תְמָרִים שֶׁרְאוּיִן לְמַאֲכַל בְּהֵמָה:


והגלעינים (stone/kernel of a fruit) – stone/kernel of fruit, as for example, the stones that are in olives that we remove oil from them. And the stones of dates that are appropriate for food for cattle.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:4)


**צוֹבֵעַ לְעַצְמוֹ.** דִּכְתִיב לָכֶם, לְכָל צָרְכֵיכֶם:


צובע לעצמו – as it is written (Leviticus 25:6): “[But you may eat whatever the land during its sabbath will produce -] you, [your male and female slaves, the hired and bound laborers who live with you),” for all of your needs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:5)


**שֶׁאֵין עוֹשִׂים סְחוֹרָה בְּפֵרוֹת שְׁבִיעִית.** וּצְבִיעָה בְּשָׂכָר הַיְנוּ סְחוֹרָה:


שאין עושים סחורה בפירות שביעית – and dyeing for pay is business.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:6)


**לֹא בִבְכוֹרוֹת וְלֹא בִתְרוּמוֹת.** דִּלְמָא מַשְׁהֵי לְהוּ לְגַבֵּיהּ וְאָתֵי בְהוּ לִידֵי תַקָּלָה. וְדַוְקָא בִּבְכוֹר חַי גָּזְרוּ, אֲבָל שָׁחוּט שָׁרֵי לְמָכְרוֹ אֲפִלּוּ מִדְּרַבָּנָן, וּבִלְבָד שֶׁלֹּא יִמְכְּרֶנּוּ בְאִטְלִיז:


לא בבכורות ולא בתרומה – perhaps they will be detained with him and they will come to be a snare to him. And exactly with a first born living animal, they decreed this but if it is slaughtered, it is permitted t sell it, even according to the Rabbis, as long as they don’t sell it in a bazaa/a number of booths put up for a mercantile fair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:7)


**לֹא בִנְבֵלוֹת וְלֹא בִטְרֵפוֹת.** דְּמֵאַחַר דְּכָתַב רַחֲמָנָא טְמֵאִים הֵם, מַה צָּרִיךְ לוֹמַר תּוּ וּטְמֵאִים יִהְיוּ לָכֶם, אֶלָּא אֶחָד לְאִסּוּר אֲכִילָה וְאֶחָד לְאִסּוּר הֲנָאָה. וְאִם תֹּאמַר הֲרֵי חֲמוֹר הֲרֵי גָמָל וְסוּס וָפֶרֶד, שָׁאנֵי הַנֵּי דְּלִמְלַאכְתָּן הֵן גְּדֵלִים, וְלֹא אָסְרוּ לַעֲשׂוֹת סְחוֹרָה אֶלָּא בְדָבָר הָעוֹמֵד לַאֲכִילָה. וְכָל דָּבָר שֶׁאִסּוּרוֹ מִן הַתּוֹרָה אָסוּר לַעֲשׂוֹת בּוֹ סְחוֹרָה, אֲבָל דָּבָר שֶׁאִסּוּרוֹ מִדִּבְרֵיהֶם אֵין אָסוּר לַעֲשׂוֹת בּוֹ סְחוֹרָה אֶלָּא סְתַם יֵינָן, דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּאִסּוּרוֹ מִדִּבְרֵיהֶם אָסוּר לַעֲשׂוֹת בּוֹ סְחוֹרָה:


ולט בנבלות ואל בטרפות – for after the All-Merciful wrote that “they are unclean for you,” (Leviticus 11:27), why does it have to state further, “and they will be impure for you,” (Leviticus 11:35) but rather one is for the prohibition of eating and the other is for the prohibition of benefitting from it. But if you say – that the donkey, the camel, the horse and the mule, these are different for they are gown for their work and they are not forbidden to do business, but rather, with something that stands to be eating. And any thing that is forbidden from the Torah is prohibited to conduct business with, but a thing that from their words (i.e., the Sages) it was made forbidden, it is not forbidden to conduct business with it, but rather, wine of gentiles of which it is not known that it has been dedicated to an idol, that was made forbidden according to their words (I.e., of the Sages), it is forbidden to conduct business with it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:8)


**וְלֹא יְהֵא לוֹקֵחַ.** כְּלוֹמַר לוֹקֵט וּמוֹכֵר בַּשּׁוּק, דְּנִרְאֶה כִּסְחוֹרָה. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁבְּנוֹ מוֹכֵר עַל יָדוֹ, פֵּרוּשׁ בִּשְׁבִילוֹ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא לְקָטָן לְמָכְרָן לֹא מֵחֲזֵי כִּסְחוֹרָה:


ולא יהא לוקח – meaning to say, to gather and sell in the market as it appears like business, but when his son sells it by his hand, meaning, for him, even though he gathered them to sell them, it does not look like business.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:3:9)


**לָקַח לְעַצְמוֹ.** לָקַט לְעַצְמוֹ לֶאֱכֹל וְהוֹתִיר, מֻתָּר לְמָכְרָן אֲפִלּוּ הוּא עַצְמוֹ:


לקח לעצמו – gathered it for himself to eat and there was leftover, it is permitted to sell them, even he himself.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:4:1)


**לָקַח בְּכוֹר.** בַּעַל מוּם, דְּמֻתָּר לְזָרִים:


לקח בכור – [a firstling] having a blemish (which is unfit for consumption by Kohanim; see Tractate Bekhorot, Chapter 5, Mishnah 2) which is permitted to foreigners (i.e., non-Kohanim).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:4:2)


**מֻתָּר לְמָכְרוֹ.** וּבִלְבָד שֶׁלֹּא יִמְכְּרֶנּוּ אֶלָּא בַדָּמִים שֶׁקָּנָה אוֹתוֹ, שֶׁלֹּא יִשְׂתַּכֵּר בּוֹ:


מותר למכרו – as long as he does not sell it other than for the price that he purchased it for, that he should not profit from it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:4:3)


**מֻתָּרִין לְמָכְרָן.** דְּהָא כְּתִיב גַּבֵּי נְבֵלָה (דברים יד) אוֹ מָכֹר לְנָכְרִי, הָא לֹא אָסְרָה תּוֹרָה אֶלָּא לְקַיְּמָן לַעֲשׂוֹת בָּהֶן סְחוֹרָה, אֲבָל בְּנִזְדַּמֵּן מֻתָּר:


מותרין למכרן – because it is written regarding an animal that dies of itself (Deuteronomy 14:21): “[You shall not eat anything that has died a natural death; give it to the stranger in your community to eat,] or you may sell it to a foreigner,” abut the Torah didn’t prohibit other than to keep thema live to engage in business with them but if he accidentally caught unclean animals, it is permitted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:4:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר אַף מִי שֶׁנִּתְמַנָּה.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ צַיָּד אֶלָּא שֶׁמָּצָא עוֹפוֹת טְמֵאִים, כְּמוֹ כִּי יִקָּרֵא קַן צִפּוֹר (דברים כב). וּפָלֵיג אַתַּנָּא קַמָּא דְּאָמַר צַיָּדֵי דַּוְקָא:


ר"י אומר אף מי שנתמנה (if he incidentally encounters it) – even though that he does not hunt but rather that he found impure birds like (Deuteronomy 22:6): “If, along the road, you chance upon a bird’s nest.” But he disputes the first Tanna/teacher who stated that he especially was hunting.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:4:5)


**נִתְמַנָּה.** נִזְדַּמֵּן, לְשׁוֹן וַיְמַן הַמֶּלֶךְ (דניאל א):


נתמנה – incidentally encounters, from the language (Daniel 1:5): “The king allotted daily rations [to them from the king’s food and from the wine he drank[.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:4:6)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹסְרִים.** לְמִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ צַיָּד, וַאֲפִלּוּ אֵין אֻמָּנוּתוֹ בְּכָךְ, וְטַעֲמַיְהוּ דְרַבָּנָן לְפִי שֶׁהַצַּיָּדִים שֶׁצְּרִיכִים לִתֵּן מַס לַמֶּלֶךְ מֵאֻמָּנוּתָם הִתִּירוּ לָהֶם לִמְכֹּר חַיָּה עוֹפוֹת וְדָגִים טְמֵאִים כְּדֵי שֶׁיּוּכְלוּ לִפְרֹעַ לַמֶּלֶךְ הַמַּס שֶׁעֲלֵיהֶם, אֲבָל שְׁאָר כָּל אָדָם לֹא. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


וחכמים אוסרים – to someone who is does not hunt, and even has no skill in this, and the reason of the Rabbis is because the hunters that need to give a tax to the king from their trade, they (i.e., the Rabbis) permitted them to sell impure beasts, birds and fish in order that they are able to make payment to the king of the tax that is upon them, but not the rest of the people. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:5:1)


**לוּלְבֵי זְרָדִין.** לוּלְבֵי אִילָן שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ זַרְדָּתָא:


לולבי זרדים (young sprouts of the service-tree, the interior of which is eaten as a relish) – the branches of the tree whose name is “sorb.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:5:2)


**לוּלְבֵי הָאֵלָה.** לְשׁוֹן כָּאֵלָה וְכָאַלּוֹן (ישעיה ו) בַּעֲרָבִי באלו"ט וּבְלַעַ"ז גלנד"י:


לולבי האלה (branches of the terebinth) – the language of (Isaiah 6:13): “[It shall be ravaged] like the terebinth and the oak, [of which stumps are left even when they are felled: its stump shall be a holy seed],” BALLUT in Arabic and GALNADI in the froeign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:5:3)


**בָּטְנָה.** מִלְּשׁוֹן בָּטְנִים וּשְׁקֵדִים, בִּלְשׁוֹן עֲרָבִי פאסת"ק:


בטנה (pistachio) – from the language of (Genesis 43:11): “pistachio nuts and almonds”; in Arabic, POSTAK.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:5:4)


**וַאֲטָדִים.** מִין מִמִּינֵי הַקּוֹצִים שֶׁצּוֹמְחִים בּוֹ גַּרְעִינִים שְׁחוֹרִים קָשִׁים וְהֵן נֶאֱכָלִים:


והאטדים (white thorn)– a kind of the species of thorns that grow in it hard black stones/kernels and they are eaten.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:5:5)


**אֲבָל לֶעָלִים.** שֶׁל כָּל אֵלּוּ יֵשׁ בִּעוּר, דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּלוּלָבִים אֵין נוֹשְׁרִים וְאֵין אֲנִי קוֹרֵא בָהֶם וְלִבְהֶמְתֶּךָ וְלַחַיָּה, כָּלָה לַחַיָּה מִן הַשָּׂדֶה כַּלֶּה לִבְהֶמְתְּךָ מִן הַבַּיִת, מִכָּל מָקוֹם הֶעָלִים נוֹשְׁרִים הֵן מִמְּקוֹם חִבּוּרָן, וְלָעֲנָפִים שֶׁהֵם מְחֻבָּרִים בּוֹ קָרֵי אֲבִיהֶן, וְכֵיוָן שֶׁהֵם נוֹשְׁרִים יֵשׁ לָהֶם בִּעוּר:


אבל לעלים – of all of these there is removal, even though the branches do not fall, and I do not call them (Leviticus 25:7): “and your cattle and the beasts] in your land may eat all its yield],” they cease to beasts from the field; cease to your cattle from the house, nevertheless, the leases that fall are from the place of their attachment. And the branches which are attached are called, “their fathers,” and since they fall, they have removal.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:6:1)


**וֶרֶד.** שׁוֹשָׁן:


וורד – rose.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:6:2)


**וְהַכֹּפֶר.** הוּא שֶׁקּוֹרִים לוֹ בַּעֲרָבִי אלחנ"א. וְיֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים שֶׁהוּא בֹּשֶׂם שֶׁקּוֹרִין גרופול"י:


והכופר (cyprus flower) – nit is what we call ALHANAH in Arabic. And there are those who say that it is a perfume that they call GROPOLI.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:6:3)


**וְהַקְּטָף.** אִילַן אֲפַרְסְמוֹן:


והקטף (balsam) – the balsam tree.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:6:4)


**לֹטֶם.** תַּרְגּוּם לוֹט וַלְטוֹס. יֵשׁ אוֹמְרִים שֶׁהוּא צינוב"ר בַּעֲרָבִי, פניולי"ש בְּלַעַ"ז:


לוטם (resin – used as a perfume; Maimonides calls it a chestnut or hazelnut) – It is the Aramaic translation of (Genesis 37:25): “[their camels bearing] gum, balm [and ladanum to be taken to Egypt];” And there are those who say that it is TZINBAR in Arabic; PANYULISH in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:6:5)


**יֵשׁ לָהֶם שְׁבִיעִית וְלִדְמֵיהֶן שְׁבִיעִית.** וְהוּא הַדִּין דְּיֵשׁ לָהֶן בִּעוּר וְלִדְמֵיהֶן בִּעוּר, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִזְכַּר בַּמִּשְׁנָה:


יש להם שביעית ולדמיהן שביעית – the same law applies that they have removal [from the house] and their monetary value has removal, and even though it is not mentioned in the Mishnah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:6:6)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֵינוֹ פֶרִי.** וְדִינָן כְּעֵצִים דְּאֵין לָהֶם קְדֻשַּׁת שְׁבִיעִית מִשּׁוּם דַּהֲנָאָתָן אַחַר בִּעוּרָן, וְהַתּוֹרָה אָמְרָה לְאָכְלָה, דּוּמְיָא דַאֲכִילָה, שֶׁהֲנָאָתָן וּבִעוּרָן שָׁוֶה, יָצְאוּ עֵצִים דַּהֲנָאָתָן אַחַר בִּעוּרָן. וְתַנָּא קַמָּא סֵבֶר דִּקְטָפוֹ זֶהוּ פִּרְיוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


מפני שאינו פרי ([that balsam is not subject to the laws of the Seventh Year]) – and their law is like wood that do not have the holiness of the Seventh Year because their benefit comes after their removal [from the house], and the Torah stated (Leviticus 25:6): “But you may eat whatever the land during its Sabbath will produce” – that which is similar to eating for their benefit and removal are equivalent, excluding the wood whose benefit comes after their removal. But the first Tanna/teacher [of this Mishnah] holds that the balsam is its fruit. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:7:1)


**וֶרֶד חָדָשׁ שֶׁכְּבָשׁוֹ בְּשֶׁמֶן יָשָׁן.** וֶרֶד שֶׁל שְׁבִיעִית שֶׁכְּבָשׁוֹ בְּשֶׁמֶן שֶׁל שִׁשִּׁית, יִלְקֹט אֶת הַוֶּרֶד קֹדֶם שֶׁיַּגִּיעַ זְמַן הַבִּעוּר וְהַכֹּל מֻתָּר. אֲבָל וֶרֶד שֶׁל שְׁבִיעִית שֶׁכְּבָשׁוֹ בְּשֶׁמֶן שֶׁל שְׁמִינִית וּכְבָר הִגִּיעַ זְמַן הַבִּעוּר שֶׁל הַוֶּרֶד, חַיָּב לְבַעֵר אֶת הַכֹּל, כִּדְקָתָנֵי סֵיפָא דִשְׁבִיעִית אוֹסֶרֶת מִין בְּשֶׁאֵינוֹ מִינוֹ בְּנוֹתֵן טַעַם:


ורד חדש שכבשו בשמן שין – a rose of the Seventh Year that was preserved/pickled in oil of the Sixth [Year], he will remove/gather the rose prior to the time of removal [from the house] arrives and everything is permitted. But a rose of the Seventh Year that was preserved/pickled in oil of the Eighth [Year] and the time for the removal has already arrived of the rose, he is liable to removal everything, as it is taught at the ending clause: [Produce of the] Sabbatical Year renders forbidden [subject to the laws of the Sabbatical year] all other [permitted produce] of the same species [with which it has been mixed]. But [if the two lots of produce are] not of the same species, [only if the produce of the Sabbatical year] imparts its flavor [does it render the other produce forbidden].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:7:2)


**וְחָרוּבִין חֲדָשִׁים וְכוּ'.** הָכָא מַיְרֵי בְּבָא לִשְׁתּוֹת הַיַּיִן אַחַר זְמַן הַבִּעוּר, דִּבְכָל עִנְיָן חַיָּב לְבַעֵר. וְאִם לֹא נִשְׁתַּהוּ הֶחָרוּבִים בַּיַּיִן עַד זְמַן הַבִּעוּר יִלְקֹט הֶחָרוּבִין וְהַיַּיִן מֻתָּר כְּמוֹ בְּוֶרֶד, וְהָכִי תַּנְיָא בְּהֶדְיָא בַּתּוֹסֶפְתָּא:


וחרובין חדשים וכו' – here we are speaking of one who comes to drink the wine after the time of the removal [from the house] and in every matter, e is liable to remove it. But if the carobs did not remain/tarry in the wine until the time of the removal, he gathers/collects the carobs and the wine is permitted like sifting, and this is taught explicitly in the Tosefta (see Tosefta Sheviit, Chapter 5, Halakhot 13-14 in the Lieberman edition to Seder Zeraim, page 188).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:7:3)


**זֶה הַכְּלָל וְכוּ'.** בְּכָל אִסּוּרִים שֶׁבַּתּוֹרָה מַיְרֵי, דִּבְכֻלָּן בְּמִינוֹ בְּמַשֶּׁהוּ וּמִין בְּשֶׁאֵינוֹ מִינוֹ בְּנוֹתֵן טַעַם. וְאֵינָהּ הֲלָכָה:


זה הכלל וכו' – it speaks about aoll the prohibitions in the Torah, that in all of them, of the [permitted produce of the] same species with however small the amount of produce and with two separate species when it imparts a flavor, and it is not the Halakha.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:7:4)


**הַשְּׁבִיעִית אוֹסֶרֶת בְּכָל שֶׁהוּא בְּמִינָהּ.** לְאַחַר הַבִּעוּר, אֲבָל קֹדֶם הַבִּעוּר אֵין בָּהֶם קְדֻשַּׁת שְׁבִיעִית שֶׁיְּהֵא אָסוּר לְהַפְסִידָהּ וְלַעֲשׂוֹת בָּהֶם סְחוֹרָה עַד שֶׁיְּהֵא בָהֶם בְּנוֹתֵן טַעַם, בֵּין בְּמִינָהּ בֵּין שֶׁלֹּא בְמִינָהּ:


השביעית אוסרת בכל שהוא במינה – after the removal. But prior to the removal [from the house], they lack the holiness of the Seventh [Year], which would be prohibited to cause it loss and to conduct business with them until there is the imparting of a flavor, whether of its own species or not of its own species.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 7:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/7:7:5)


**וְשֶׁלֹּא בְמִינָהּ.** אֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר הַבִּעוּר בְּנוֹתֵן טַעַם:


ושלא במינה – even after the removal when it imparts a flavor.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:1)


**כְּלָל גָּדוֹל.** מְלוּגְמָא. תַּחְבֹּשֶׁת וּרְטִיָּה, וּפֵרוּשׁ מְלוּגְמָא מְלֹא לוּגְמָא, שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ אָדָם לִלְעֹס מְלֹא לוּגְמָיו חִטִּים אוֹ תְאֵנִים וְנוֹתֵן עַל גַּבֵּי מַכָּתוֹ:


כלל גדול. מלודמא – bandage/dressing and a compress/plaster and the explanation of מלוגמא is מלא לוגמא/a mouthful or quantity of liquid filling up one cheek for it is the practice of a person to chew/masticate wheat or dates and place them on top of his wound.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:2)


**אֵין עוֹשִׂים מִמֶּנּוּ מְלוּגְמָא.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כה) וְהָיְתָה שַׁבַּת הָאָרֶץ לָכֶם לְאָכְלָה, מַה שֶּׁהוּא מְיֻחָד לָכֶם דְּהַיְנוּ לְמַאֲכַל אָדָם יִהְיֶה לְאָכְלָה וְלֹא לִמְלוּגְמָא, אֲבָל מַה שֶּׁאֵינוֹ מְיֻחָד לָכֶם יִהְיֶה בֵּין לְאָכְלָה בֵּין לִמְלוּגְמָא, הִלְכָּךְ כָּל שֶׁאֵינוֹ מַאֲכַל אָדָם עוֹשִׂין מִמֶּנוּ מְלוּגְמָא לְאָדָם:


אין עושים ממנו מלוגמא – ut you may eat whatever the land during its sabbath will produce,” what is particularly designated for you, which is food for human beings and not for soothing remedies , but what is not particularly designated for you will be, whether for eating, whether for soothing remedies/bandages. Therefore, whatever is not food for human beings, we make of it an emollient/ soothing remedies for human beings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:3)


**עוֹשִׂין מִמֶּנוּ מְלוּגְמָא לְאָדָם אֲבָל לֹא לִבְהֵמָה.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְלִבְהֶמְתְּךָ וְלַחַיָּה אֲשֶׁר בְּאַרְצֶךָ תִּהְיֶה כָּל תְּבוּאָתָהּ לֶאֱכֹל, דָּבָר הָרָאוּי לַחַיָּה וְלַבְּהֵמָה תִּהְיֶה לֶאֱכֹל לַבְּהֵמָה וְלֹא לַעֲשׂוֹת לָהּ מְלוּגְמָא:


עושין ממנו מלוגמא לאדם אבל לא לבהמה – as it is written (Leviticus 25:7): “and your cattle and the beasts in your land [may eat all its yield],” that which is appropriate for beasts and cattle , will be for food for cattle, but not to make of it a soothing remedy/bandage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:4)


**חֻמְרֵי אָדָם.** שֶׁאֵין עוֹשִׂין מֵהֶם מְלוּגְמָא:


חומרי אדם – and they don’t make from them an emollient/soothing remedy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:5)


**וְחֻמְרֵי בְהֵמָה.** שֶׁאָסוּר לְשָׁלְקָן:


וחומרי בהמה – that it is forbidden to boil them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:6)


**הַסִּיאָה.** עֵשֶׂב שֶׁקּוֹרִין לוֹ בַּעֲרָבִי צידר"ג:


הסיאה (plant classified with hyssop) – a grass that they call it in Arabic TZIDRAG.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:7)


**וְאֵזוֹב.** צעת"ר בַּעֲרָבִי וּבְלַעַ"ז איספ"ו:


ואזוב – TZATER in Arabic, and AISPO in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:1:8)


**וְקוּרְנִית.** בַּעֲרָבִי חש"א וּבְלַעַ"ז שדריא"ע:


וקורנית (thyme) – in Arabic HASSA, and in the foreign language and in the foreign language ASHRIAH.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:2:1)


**לַאֲכִילָה וּשְׁתִיָּה וְסִיכָה.** בַּעֲנָבִים וְזֵיתִים מַיְרֵי, שֶׁיֵּשׁ בָּהֶם אֲכִילָה וּשְׁתִיָּה וְסִיכָה:


וסיכה – we are speaking about grapes and olives that have in them eating, drinking and anointing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:2:2)


**לֶאֱכֹל דָּבָר שֶׁדַּרְכּוֹ לֶאֱכֹל.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּאָמְרָה תּוֹרָה לְאָכְלָה וְלֹא לְהֶפְסֵד, אֵין מְחַיְּבִים אוֹתוֹ לֶאֱכֹל דָּבָר שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם לְאָכְלוֹ, כְּגוֹן תַּבְשִׁיל שֶׁנִּתְקַלְקֵל, וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן אִם הוֹלֵךְ לְאִבּוּד. וְכֵן דָּבָר שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ בְּנֵי אָדָם לְאָכְלוֹ אֶלָּא עַל יְדֵי תִּקּוּן וּבָא הוּא לְאָכְלוֹ כְּמוֹת שֶׁהוּא בְּלֹא תִקּוּן, כְּגוֹן לֶאֱכֹל תְּרָדִין חַיִּין אוֹ לָכוֹס חִטִּין חַיּוֹת, אֵין שׁוֹמְעִין לוֹ:


לאכול דבר שדרכו לאכול – even though the Torah stated (Leviticus 25:6): “But you may eat [whatever the land during its sabbath will produce],” and not for loss, we do not obligate him to eat something that that generally human beings don’t eat, such as cooked dish that spoiled, and we don’t concern ourselves if it went to ruin . And similarly a thing that generally human beings don’t eat other than upon improvement/repair and he comes to eat it as it is without the improvement/repair, as for example, to eat a live species of beet or to masticate live wheat , we don’t listen to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:2:3)


**וְכֵן בִּתְרוּמָה וְכֵן בְּמַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי.** לֹא נִתְּנוּ אֶלָּא לַאֲכִילָה וּשְׁתִיָּה וְסִיכָה:


וכן בתרומה וכן במע"ש – which wee not given to him other than to eat, and to drink and to anoint with.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:2:4)


**שֶׁנִּתְּנָה לְהַדְלָקַת הַנֵּר.** מַה שֶּׁאֵין כֵּן בִּתְרוּמָה טְהוֹרָה, אֲבָל תְּרוּמָה טְמֵאָה מַדְלִיקִין בָּהּ אֶת הַנֵּר. וּבְמַעֲשֵׂר שֵׁנִי כְּתִיב (דברים כו) וְלֹא בִעַרְתִּי מִמֶּנּוּ בְּטָמֵא, אֲבָל בְּטָהוֹר מֻתָּר לְבַעֵר. וּבִשְׁבִיעִית בֵּין בְּטָמֵא בֵּין בְּטָהוֹר מֻתָּר:


שנתנה להדלקת הנר – which is not the case with pure heave-offering, but impure heave-offering, we kindle the file with it, And with Second Tithe, it is written (Deuteronomy 26:14): “I have not cleared out any of it while I was impure,” but when he is pure, he is permitted to clear it out/remove it. But on the Seventh Year, whether he is impure or pure, it is permitted [to remove it].



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:3:1)


**לֹא בְמִדָּה וְלֹא בְמִשְׁקָל וְלֹא בְמִנְיָן.** כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּמָּכְרוּ בְּזוֹל. אִי נַמִּי מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁאֵין מוֹכְרִין אוֹתָן כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁמּוֹכְרִים בִּשְׁאָר שָׁנִים, יִנְהֲגוּ בָּהֶם קְדֻשַּׁת שְׁבִיעִית וְלֹא אָתוּ לְזַלְזוֹלֵי בְהוּ:


לא במדה ולא משקל ולא במנין – in order that they can be sold cheaply. Alternatively, because they don’t sell them in the matter that they sell in the rest of the years, they will treat them with the Sanctity of the Seventh Year and not come to disregard/despise them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:3:2)


**אַף לֹא אֲגֻדּוֹת.** שֶׁלֹּא יִמְכֹּר כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁמּוֹכְרִים בִּשְׁאָר שָׁנִים:


אף לא אגודות – that he will not sell it in the same manner that they sell it in the rest of the years.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:3:3)


**וְנֵץ חָלָב.** מְפֹרָשׁ בְּפִרְקִין דִּלְעֵיל:


ועץ חלב – it is explained in the chapter above (Tractate Sheviit, Chapter 7, Mishnah 1).



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:4:1)


**שְׂכָרוֹ מֻתָּר.** וְאֵין בּוֹ קְדֻשַּׁת שְׁבִיעִית. וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּשְׁבִיעִית תּוֹפֶסֶת דָּמֶיהָ, הַאי לָאו דְּמֵי שְׁבִיעִית הוּא:


שכרו מותר – for it lacks the sanctify of the Seventh Year, and even though that the Seventh Year takes hold of its monetary value, this is not the money of the Seventh Year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:4:2)


**לְקֹט לִי בּוֹ יָרָק.** לְשׁוֹן מֶכֶר הוּא, דְּמַשְׁמַע לְקֹט לִי שָׁוְיוֹ יָרָק, אֲבָל לְקֹט לִי לְשׁוֹן שְׂכִירוּת הוּא וְלֹא לְשׁוֹן מֶכֶר:


לקוט לי בו ירק – this is the language of a sale, that implies, gather for me its equivalent in vegetables, but [the word] לקוט /gather-glean is the language of hiring someone, and not the language of a sale.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:4:3)


**כִּכָּר בְּפוּנְדְּיוֹן.** לֹא שֶׁאָמַר לוֹ אֶתֵּן לְךָ פּוּנְדְּיוֹן, רַק כִּכָּר שָׁוֶה פּוּנְדְּיוֹן:


ככר בפונדיון – not that he said to him, “I will give you a Pundiyon,” only a loaf worth a Pundiyon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:4:4)


**כְּשֶׁאֶלְקֹט יַרְקוֹת שָׂדֶה אָבִיא לָךְ מֻתָּר.** מִשּׁוּם דְּמַתְּנַת חִנָּם יַהֲבֵי זֶה לָזֶה, לְפִי שֶׁיַּרְקוֹת שָׂדֶה מְצוּיִין:


כשאלקט ירקות שדה אביא לך מותר – because this one is giving to the other a free gift, since the vegetables of the field are found/available.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:4:5)


**לָקַח מִמֶּנּוּ בִּסְתָם.** בְּהַקָּפָה כְּדֶרֶךְ מוֹכְרִים בְּחָנוּת:


לקח ממנו בסתם – debts for merchandise payable at certain seasons in the manner that they sell in the store.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:4:6)


**לֹא יְשַׁלֵּם לוֹ מִדְּמֵי שְׁבִיעִית.** דַּהֲוֵי כִּסְחוֹרָה:


לא ישלם לו מדמי שביעית – for it is like business [dealings].



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:5:1)


**בַּיָּר.** חוֹפֵר בּוֹרוֹת וּמַסְפִּיק מַיִם לִבְנֵי הָעִיר, לֹא יִתֵּן לוֹ מִדְּמֵי שְׁבִיעִית לְהַסְפִּיק לוֹ מַיִם לְכָל תַּשְׁמִישׁוֹ:


בייר (superintendent of the well in rural communities) – he who digs wells and provides water to the people of the city. He should not give him from the monies of the Seventh Year to supply water for him to all his utensil.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:5:2)


**וְלֹא לְבַלָּן.** מְחַמֵּם מֶרְחֲצָאוֹת:


ולא לבלן (and not to the bathing attendant) – who warms up the bathhouses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:5:3)


**וְלֹא לְסַפָּר.** שְׂכַר הַתִּסְפֹּרֶת:


ולא לספר – the wag of the haircut.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:5:4)


**וְלֹא לְסַפָּן.** שְׂכַר הַסְּפִינָה:


ולא ספן – the wage of the boat/ship.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:5:5)


**וּלְכֻלָּן.** לְכָל הַנֵּי דְתָנִינַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין יָכוֹל לִתֵּן מַתְּנַת חִנָּם שֶׁלֹּא לְשֵׁם שְׂכָרָן, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיּוֹדֵעַ שֶׁבִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁנָּתַן מַתָּנָה לֹא יִתְבַּע מִמֶּנּוּ שָׂכָר:


ולכולן – to all of these that are taught in our Mishnah, he can give a free gift that is not for the sake of their wages, for even though that he knows that it is for a gift that he gave, he will not demand from him a wage/payment.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:6:1)


**בַּמֻּקְצֶה.** כְּלִי הַמְּיֻחָד לָקוֹץ בּוֹ תְּאֵנִים קָרוּי מֻקְצֶה:


במוקצה – a utensil that is designated to harvest dates with is called מוקצה/Muktzeh.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:6:2)


**בַּחַרְבָּה.** סַכִּין, לְשׁוֹן חֶרֶב. וְטַעֲמָא מִשּׁוּם דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כה) וְאֶת עִנְבֵי נְזִירֶךָ לֹא תִבְצֹר, כְּדֶרֶךְ הַבּוֹצְרִים:


בחרבה (knife) – a akinife. I is the language of a sword. And the reason is because it is written (Leviticus 25:5): “or gather the grapes of your untrimmed vines,” in the manner of those who harvest grapes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:6:3)


**עֲרֵבָה.** שֶׁלָּשִׁים בָּהּ אֶת הָעִסָּה:


עריבה (kneading trough) – that they knead the started dough.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:6:4)


**וּבַקֹּטֶב.** עֵץ גָּדוֹל וְכָבֵד וּבְרֹאשׁוֹ אֶבֶן גְּדוֹלָה שֶׁנּוֹתְנִים עַל הַזֵּיתִים בְּבֵית הַבַּד, נִקְרָא קֹטֶב:


ובקטב (small wine or olive press with a cylindrical beam)- a large and heavy wood and on its top is a large stone that they place on the olives in the olive press, which is called a קוטב.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:6:5)


**בּוֹדִידָה.** בֵּית הַבַּד קָטָן כְּעֵין עֲרֵבָה:


בודידה (small olive press) – a small olive press like a kneading trough.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:6:6)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


רבי שמעון אומר כו' – And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:7:1)


**שֶׁלֹּא יְבִיאֶנּוּ לִידֵי פְסוּל.** שֶׁאִם נִפְסַל, בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁמֶן שֶׁל תְּרוּמָה יִשָּׂרֵף הַכֹּל, וְנִמְצָא גּוֹרֵם לְפֵרוֹת שְׁבִיעִית שֶׁלֹּא יֵאָכְלוּ:


לא יביאנו לידי פסול (they will not cause [the vegetables]- to become invalid – so that the produce of the Sabbatical Year will not be wasted in the event the oil becomes unclean and must be burned) – foer if iet becomes invalid because of the oil of heave-offering, everything must be burned, and it is found that it will cause the fruit of the Seventh Year that it won’t be eaten.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:7:2)


**וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מַתִּיר.** לְהָבִיא קָדָשִׁים לְבֵית הַפְּסוּל. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ורבי שמעון מתיר – to bring holy/dedicated objects to the House of Impurity. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:7:3)


**וְהָאַחֲרוֹן אַחֲרוֹן נִתְפָּס בַּשְּׁבִיעִית.** כֵּיצַד, לָקַח בְּפֵרוֹת שְׁבִיעִית בָּשָׂר, הַבָּשָׂר וְהַפֵּרוֹת מִתְבַּעֲרִין בַּשְּׁבִיעִית, לָקַח בַּבָּשָׂר דָּגִים, יָצָא בָּשָׂר וְנִתְפַּס דָּגִים, לָקַח בַּדָּגִים שֶׁמֶן, יָצְאוּ דָגִים וְנִתְפַּס הַשֶּׁמֶן, וְחַיָּב לְבַעֵר הַפֵּרוֹת וְהַשֶּׁמֶן, נִמְצָא אַחֲרוֹן אַחֲרוֹן נִתְפָּס. וְהַפְּרִי שֶׁל שְׁבִיעִית עַצְמוֹ אָסוּר לְעוֹלָם, וּמַפְּקִינַן לַהּ מִדִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כה) כִּי יוֹבֵל הִיא קֹדֶשׁ, מַה קֹּדֶשׁ תּוֹפֵס אֶת דָּמָיו אַף שְׁבִיעִית תּוֹפֶסֶת אֶת דָּמֶיהָ, אִי מַה קֹּדֶשׁ תּוֹפֵס אֶת דָּמָיו וְיוֹצֵא לְחֻלִּין אַף שְׁבִיעִית תּוֹפֶסֶת דָּמֶיהָ וְיוֹצֵאת לְחֻלִּין, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר (שם) תִּהְיֶה, בַּהֲוָיָתָהּ תְּהֵא:


ואחרון אחרון נתפס בשביעית (The very last [produce obtained in this manner] is subjected to the laws of the Sabbatical Year) – How so? He purchased meat with the fruit/produce of the Seventh Year; the meat and the produce/fruit are removed in the Seventh Year, if he purchased fish with the meat, the meat is excluded and the fish subjected to the laws of the Seventh Year. If he acquired oil with the fish, the fish is excluded and the oil is subjected to the laws of the Seventh Year, and he is obligated to remove the fruit/produce and the oil, it is found that the very last thing acquired in this manner is subjected to the laws of the Seventh Year. But the produce/fruit of the Seventh Year itself is always forbidden, and we derive it from what is written (Leviticus 25:12): “For it is a jubilee. It shall be holy to you.” Just as what is holy takes hold upon/takes effect its monetary value, so even the Seventh year takes hold upon/takes effect on its monetary value. Or, just as the holiness takes hold upon its monetary value and it goes to unconsecrated produce, even the Seventh Year takes hold upon its monetary value and it goes to unconsecrated produce, the inference teaches us – "תהיה"/it shall be [holy unto you], it will be in its status.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:8:1)


**יֹאכַל כְּנֶגְדָּן.** כְּנֶגֶד אוֹתָם דָּמִים יִקְנֶה מִשֶּׁלוֹ דְּבָרִים שֶׁהֵם לְצֹרֶךְ אֲכִילָה וְיֹאכַל בִּקְדֻשַּׁת שְׁבִיעִית:


יאכל כנגדן (and eat produce of equal value [to replace the produce of the Sabbatical year which he sold])- corresponding to the funds, he will purchase from his own [money] things that are for the needs of eating and consume them in the sanctity of the Seventh Year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:8:2)


**קִנֵּי זָבִים וְקִנֵּי זָבוֹת.** שְׁתֵּי תוֹרִים אוֹ שְׁנֵי בְנֵי יוֹנָה שֶׁחַיָּבִין לְהָבִיא הַזָּבִים וְהַיּוֹלְדוֹת:


קני זבים וקני זבות – two turtle-doves or two young pigeons that those who suffer a flux or have given birth are obligated to bring.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:9:1)


**שְׁתוֹקוּ לֹא אוֹמַר לָכֶם מַה שֶּׁרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר בּוֹ.** רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא קִבֵּל מֵרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר רַבּוֹ דְּמֵקֵל טְפֵי בְּסִיכָה, וּכְשֶׁאָמְרוּ לוֹ שֶׁהָיָה רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר יִדָּלֵק אָמַר לָהֶם שְׁתוֹקוּ, שֶׁאֵינִי רַשַּׁאי לְפָרֵשׁ כַּמָּה הָיָה רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מֵקֵל בּוֹ, וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


שתקו לא אומר לכם מה ר"ע אומר בו - Rabbi Akiva received [the tradition] from Rabbi Eliezer his teacher who was more lenient as regarding anointing . And when they told him that Rabbi Eliezer stated that it should be burned, he said to them: “Be silent,” for I am not permitted to interpet how much Rabbi Eliezer was lenient in this, as the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:10:1)


**כְּאוֹכֵל בְּשַׂר חֲזִיר.** לָאו דַּוְקָא, שֶׁאֵין הָאוֹכֵל פַּת כּוּתִי לוֹקֶה מִן הַתּוֹרָה, אֶלָּא מַכַּת מַרְדּוּת מִדְּרַבָּנָן:


כאוכל בשר חזיר – not exactly, for a person who eats the bread of Samaritans/Cutheans is not flogged from the [law of] the Torah, but rather receives stripes of rebellion from the Rabbis.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:10:2)


**מַה שֶּׁרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר בּוֹ.** דְּמֵקֵל בּוֹ טְפֵי:


מה ר"א אומר בו – for he (i.e., Rabbi Eliezer) is more lenient in this.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 8:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/8:11:1)


**וְאִם מִתְחַשֵּׁב הוּא.** אִם אָדָם חָשׁוּב הוּא שֶׁבְּנֵי אָדָם לְמֵדִים מִמֶּנּוּ צָרִיךְ לְהַחְמִיר עַל עַצְמוֹ:


ואם מתחשב הוא – if he is an important person that people learn from him, he must be stringent upon himself.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:1)


**הַפֵּגָם.** עֵשֶׂב שֶׁקּוֹרִין רוט"א בְּלַעַ"ז:


הפיגם (the herb rue – with leaves that a re wide below and narrow on top) – a grass that is called RUTA in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:2)


**וְהַיַּרְבּוּזִין.** אספרג"י בְּלַעַ"ז. וְהַשּׁוֹטִים הֵם אספרג"י שֶׁל שָׂדוֹת, אוֹתָן שֶׁאֵינָן שֶׁל גִּנּוֹת:


והירבוזין (asparagus, strawberry-blite) – ASPARGEE in the foreign language, and the wild ASPARGEE of the fields that are not of the gardens.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:3)


**וְהַחֲלַגְלוֹגוֹת.** בַּעֲרָבִי רגל"א וּבְלַעַ"ז ווירדולאג"ש:


והחלגלוגות (purslane)- in Arabic RAGLA and in he foreign language VIRDOLAGESH.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:4)


**וְכֻסְבָּר.** קוליאנד"ר בְּלַעַ"ז. וְדַוְקָא שֶׁל הָרִים אֲבָל שֶׁל גִּנָּה חָשׁוּב הוּא: **וְכֵן כַּרְפַּס.** שֶׁל נְהָרוֹת דַּוְקָא אֲבָל לֹא שֶׁל גִּנָּה:


וכוסבר (coriander) – COLIANDER in the foreign language. And especially of the mountains, but they of the gardens they are highly regarded. . And similarly, and parsley of the rivers especially but not of the garden.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:5)


**כַּרְפַּס.** אפיי"ו בְּלַעַ"ז:


כרפס – APIYU in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:6)


**גַּרְגֵּר.** אוריג"א בְּלַעַ"ז:


גרגיר (garden rocket) – ORIGA in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:7)


**שֶׁבַּאֲפָר.** הַגָּדֵל בָּאָחוּ. אֲבָל שֶׁל גִּנָּה חָשׁוּב הוּא:


שבאפר – that grows in the reed-grass. But of the garden it is highly regarded.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:8)


**פְּטוּרִים מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר.** בְּכָל הַשָּׁנִים כְּדִמְפָרֵשׁ טַעֲמָא שֶׁאֵין כַּיּוֹצֵא בָהֶן נִשְׁמָר, שֶׁאֵינָן נֶחֱשָׁבִין בְּעֵינֵי בַעֲלֵיהֶן וּמַפְקִירִין אוֹתָן, וְהֶפְקֵר פָּטוּר מִן הַמַּעֲשֵׂר דִּכְתִיב (דברים יד) וּבָא הַלֵּוִי כִּי אֵין לוֹ חֵלֶק וְנַחֲלָה עִמָּךְ, בְּדָבָר שֶׁאֵין לוֹ חֵלֶק וְנַחֲלָה עִמָּךְ יִטֹּל מַעַשְׂרוֹתָיו, יָצָא הֶפְקֵר לֶקֶט שִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ חֵלֶק עִמָּךְ:


פטורים מן המעשר – in all of the years, as it (i.e., the Mishnah) explains the reason, that there are not in a similar manner guarded and are not highly regarded in the eyes of their owners and they make them ownerless, and that which is ownerless is exempt from the tithes, as it is written (Deuteronomy 14:29): “Then the Levite, who has no hereditary portion as you have,” with a a matter that he does not have a hereditary portion as you have, he takes his tithes, excluding that which is ownerless, gleanings, the forgotten sheaf and the corner [of the field] that he does have a portion as you have.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:9)


**וְנִלְקָחִים מִכָּל אָדָם בַּשְּׁבִיעִית.** לְפִי שֶׁאֵין דֶּרֶךְ לְשָׁמְרָן. דְּכָל דָּבָר שֶׁדֶּרֶךְ לְשָׁמְרוֹ אָסוּר לִקְנוֹת אוֹתוֹ מֵעַם הָאָרֶץ בַּשְּׁבִיעִית, שֶׁהוּא חָשׁוּד לִשְׁמֹר הַפֵּרוֹת וְשֶׁלֹּא לְהַפְקִירָן, וְהַלּוֹקֵחַ מִן הַמְשֻׁמָּר אֲפִלּוּ כַּחֲצִי אִסָּר אָסוּר. וְכָל הֲנָךְ דְּמַתְנִיתִין שֶׁחֶזְקָתָן שֶׁאֵין מְשַׁמְּרִין אוֹתָן מֻתָּר לִקַּח מֵהֶן מֵעַם הָאָרֶץ כְּדֵי מָזוֹן שָׁלֹשׁ סְעֻדּוֹת בִּלְבָד וְלֹא יוֹתֵר, שֶׁאֵין מוֹסְרִין דְּמֵי פֵּרוֹת שְׁבִיעִית לְעַם הָאָרֶץ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַפֵּרוֹת הֵן מִן הַהֶפְקֵר, יוֹתֵר מִמָּזוֹן שָׁלֹשׁ סְעֻדּוֹת, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא חָשׁוּד לֶאֱצֹר הַדָּמִים וְאֵינוֹ נוֹהֵג בָּהֶם קְדֻשַּׁת שְׁבִיעִית:


ונלקחים מכל אדם בשביעית – (and are brought from anyone during the Sabbatical Year) – because there is no way to guard them, for every thing that has a way to guard it is prohibited to acquire it from an illiterate individual (i.e., a person who does not know/observe the laws of Levitical purity and the tithes) in the Seventh Year , for he is suspect to guard the produce, and not to make them ownerless, and a person who acquires it from that which guarded, even like one-half an Issar’s worth is prohibited. And all of these of our Mishnah whose legal presumption is that they don’t guard them are permitted to purchase them for an illiterate individual in order to have food for the three meals [of the Sabbath or Festival day] alone and not more, for we don’t transfer the monies of the produce of the Seventh Year to an illiterate individual, even though that this produce is from that which is ownerless, more than food for three meals, because he is suspect to store the monies and don’t practice with them the Sanctity of the Seventh Year.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:10)


**שֶׁלֹּא נֶחְשְׁדוּ עֲלֵיהֶם עוֹבְרֵי עֲבֵרָה.** לַהֲבִיאָם מִן הַשָּׁמוּר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


שלא נחשדו עליהם עוברי עבירה – because to bring them from that which is guarded. But the Halakha is not according to Rabi Yehuda.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:11)


**כָּל הַסְּפִיחִין מֻתָּרִין.** לָקַחַת מִכָּל אָדָם, דְּמַחְזְקִינַן לְהוּ שֶׁאֵינָם מִן הַשָּׁמוּר:


כל הספיחין מותרין – to acquire from all persons, for we legally presume that they are not from that which was guarded.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:12)


**חוּץ מִסְּפִיחֵי כְרוּב.** שֶׁחֶזְקָתָן מִן הַשָּׁמוּר:


חוץ מספיחי כרוב – that their legal presumption is that they are from that which is guarded.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:1:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:1:13)


**לְפִי שֶׁאֵין כַּיּוֹצֵא בָהֶם בְּיַרְקוֹת שָׂדֶה.** שֶׁאֵין גְּדֵלִים כְּמוֹתָן בַּמִּדְבָּר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


Because there is nothing of a similar manner among vegetables of the field that do not grow like them in the wilderness, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:2:1)


**שָׁלֹשׁ אֲרָצוֹת.** בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל חֲלוּקוֹת זוֹ מִזּוֹ לְעִנְיַן בִּעוּר. וּבִעוּר הָאָמוּר בְּכָל מָקוֹם בַּשְּׁבִיעִית הוּא שֶׁכָּל זְמַן שֶׁאֵין נִמְצָא בַּשָּׂדֶה מֵאוֹתוֹ הַמִּין שֶׁאָסַף אֵלָיו בַּבַּיִת, חַיָּב לְבַעֵר אוֹתוֹ הַמִּין מִן הַבַּיִת מִיָּד. כְּגוֹן אִם לָקַט תְּאֵנִים וְעָשָׂה מֵהֶן גְּרוֹגָרוֹת, אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לֶאֱכֹל מֵאוֹתָם גְּרוֹגָרוֹת אֲפִלּוּ בַּשָּׁנָה הַשְּׁבִיעִית אֶלָּא כָּל זְמַן שֶׁנִּמְצָאִים תְּאֵנִים מְחֻבָּרוֹת לָאִילָן, וּכְשֶׁכָּלוּ הַתְּאֵנִים מִן הָאִילָנוֹת חַיָּב לְבַעֵר הַגְּרוֹגָרוֹת שֶׁבְּבֵיתוֹ, אוֹ לְאָכְלָן כֻּלָּן מִיָּד, אוֹ לְהַפְקִירָן עַד שֶׁלֹּא יִשָּׁאֵר מֵהֶם בְּבֵיתוֹ, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כה) וְלִבְהֶמְתְּךָ וְלַחַיָּה, כָּל זְמַן שֶׁחַיָּה אוֹכֶלֶת בַּשָּׂדֶה הַאֲכֵל לִבְהֶמְתְּךָ מִן הַבַּיִת, כָּלָה לַחַיָּה מִן הַשָּׂדֶה כַּלֵּה לִבְהֶמְתְּךָ מִן הַבַּיִת. וְהַשְׁתָּא קָאָמַר שֶׁאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל כֻּלָּהּ נֶחְלֶקֶת לִשְׁלֹשָׁה חֲלָקִים לְעִנְיַן בִּעוּר, וְכָל חֵלֶק וְחֵלֶק מִמֶּנָּה נֶחְלָק לִשְׁלֹשָׁה חֲלָקִים:


שלש ארצות – in the Land of Israel separated one from the other for the matter of removal [from the house]. And the removal that is mentioned in each place in the Seventh Year is that whenever it is not found in the field from the same species that he collected for himself in the house, he is obligated to remove that species from the house immediately. As for example, if he gathered figs and made of them dry figs, he cannot eat from those dry figs, even during the Seventh Year, but rather, whenever they are found as figs attached to the tree, when the figs were used up from the trees, one is required to remove the dry-figs that are in his house, or to eat all of them immediately, or to make them ownerless until there not remain any of them in house, as it is written (Leviticus 25:7): “and your cattle and the beasts [in your land may eat all its yield],” whenever the beasts eat in the field, the food for the cattle is from the house; when it ceased for the beasts from the field, it ceased to your cattle from the house. And each individual section from it is divided into three parts.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:2:2)


**כָּל שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְגַדֵּל שִׁקְמִים גָּלִיל הָעֶלְיוֹן.** שֶׁהַשִּׁקְמִים אֵין גְּדֵלִים אֶלָּא בַּשְּׁפֵלָה, וְסִימָן לַדָּבָר כַּשִּׁקְמִים אֲשֶׁר בַּשְּׁפֵלָה (מלכים א י):


כל שאינו מגדל שקמים גליל העליון – for the sycamore [trees] do not gorw other than in the lowlands, and a sign for the matter, (I Kings 10:27 – there is a printer’s error in the text – as the “yud” was lengtnened to look like a “vav”): “[The King made silver as plentiful in Jerusalem as stones,] and cedars as plentiful as sycamores in the Shephelah.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:2:3)


**וּתְחוּם טְבֶרְיָה.** הוּא הָעֵמֶק. אֵלּוּ הַשְּׁלֹשָׁה הֵם מֵהַגָּלִיל:


ותחום טבריה – it is the valley, these three are from the Galilee.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:2:4)


**הָהָר וְהַשְּׁפֵלָה וְהָעֵמֶק.** הֵם שְׁלֹשָׁה חֲלָקִים שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יְהוּדָה:


ההר והשפלה והעמק these are the three sections of the land of Judah/Judea.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:2:5)


**שְׁפֵלַת לוּד.** וְהַר [שֶׁלָּהּ] לוּד. וּמִבֵּית חוֹרוֹן עַד הַיָּם. הֵם שְׁלֹשָׁה חֲלוּקוֹת שֶׁל עֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן, וּשְׁפֵלַת לוּד כִּשְׁפֵלַת הַדָּרוֹם, דְּכָל זְמַן שֶׁאוֹכֵל בִּשְׁפֵלַת [הַדָּרוֹם] אוֹכֵל בִּשְׁפֵלַת [לוּד], וְהָהָר שֶׁל שְׁפֵלַת לוּד אוֹכְלִים בּוֹ כָּל זְמַן שֶׁאוֹכְלִים בְּהַר הַמֶּלֶךְ:


שפלת לוד. והר [שלה] לוד. ומבית חורון עד הים – these are the three sections of the Transjordan, and the lowlands of Lod are like the southern lowlands , and whenever one eats in the [southern] lowlands, one eats in the lowland [of Lod]. And the mountain of the lowland of Lod they eat there whenever they eat on the Mountain of the King.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:2:6)


**מִבֵּית חוֹרוֹן וְעַד הַיָּם מְדִינָה אֶחָת.** וְכָל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא כָלָה לַחַיָּה מֵאַחַת מִן הַמְּקוֹמוֹת שֶׁבֵּינֵיהֶם, יְכוֹלִים כָּל אַנְשֵׁי הַגְּבוּל הַהוּא לֶאֱכֹל עַל יְדֵי הַמָּקוֹם הָאֶחָד שֶׁלֹּא כָלָה בּוֹ לַחַיָּה מִן הַשָּׂדֶה:


מבית חורון ועד הים מדינה אחת – and whenever it did not cease for the beasts from one of these places that are between them, the people of this border are able to eat through the one place that it (i.e.,) food) did not cease there for the beasts from the field.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:3:1)


**וְלָמָּה אָמְרוּ שָׁלֹשׁ אֲרָצוֹת.** הוֹאִיל וְשָׁלֹשׁ אֲרָצוֹת בְּכָל אַחַת הֲרֵי כָּאן תֵּשַׁע אֲרָצוֹת, לְמַאי הִלְכְתָא אָמְרוּ שָׁלֹשׁ אֲרָצוֹת לַבִּעוּר וְתוּ לֹא:


ולמה אמרו ג' ארצות – since there are three lands in each one, that makes here nine lands, for what Halakha did they say that there are three lands for removal [from the house] and not more?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:3:2)


**שֶׁיִּהְיוּ אוֹכְלִים.** אוֹכְלִים בִּיהוּדָה עַד שֶׁיִּכְלֶה הָאַחֲרוֹן שֶׁבִּשְׁלֹשׁ אַרְצוֹתֶיהָ, וְכֵן בַּגָּלִיל, וְכֵן בְּעֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן. אֲבָל אֵין אוֹכְלִים בִּיהוּדָה עַל יְדֵי אוֹתָן שֶׁבַּגָּלִיל, וְלֹא בַגָּלִיל עַל יְדֵי אוֹתָן שֶׁבִּיהוּדָה, דִּגְמִירֵי דְאֵין חַיָּה שֶׁבִּיהוּדָה מִתְרַחֶקֶת לָצֵאת מִיהוּדָה לַגָּלִיל, וּמִגָּלִיל לִיהוּדָה, אֲבָל יוֹצְאָה הִיא מִגָּלִיל לַגָּלִיל וּמִיְּהוּדָה לִיהוּדָה:


שיהיו אוכלים בכל אחת ואחת עד שיכלה האחרון שבה – they eat in Judea/Yehuda until the last [of the Seventh Year produce] runs out in its three lands, and similarly in the Galilee and similarly in the Transjordan. But if they don’t eat in Judea through that which is in the Galilee, nor in the Galilee through that which is in Judea, and we learn/derive that no beast that is in Judea distances itself to leave from Judea to the Galilee, or from the Galilee to Judea, but they leave from [one part of] Galilee to [another part of] Galilee and from [one part of] Judea to another [part of] Judea.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:3:3)


**וּשְׁאָר כָּל הָאֲרָצוֹת כְּהַר הַמֶּלֶךְ.** שְׁאָר כָּל הָאֲרָצוֹת שֶׁבְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל אוֹכְלִים עַל יְדֵי הַפֵּרוֹת הַנִּמְצָאִים בְּהַר הַמֶּלֶךְ, וְהוּא הָיָה רַב הַפֵּרוֹת וְאַחֲרוֹן מִכָּל שְׁאָר אֲרָצוֹת שֶׁבְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


ושאר כל הארצות כהר המלך – the rest of all the lands that are in the Land of Israel eat through the produce/fruit that are found in the Mountain of the King and they were the majority of the produce and last from all the est of the lands in the Land of Israel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:3:4)


**וְכָל הָאֲרָצוֹת כְּאַחַת לַזֵּיתִים וְלַתְּמָרִים.** כָּל הָאֲרָצוֹת שֶׁבְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל אוֹכְלִים זֵיתִים וּתְמָרִים עַל יְדֵי אֶרֶץ אַחַת שֶׁלֹּא כָלוּ בָהּ הַזֵּיתִים וְהַתְּמָרִים לַחַיָּה מִן הַשָּׂדֶה, בֵּין שֶׁתִּהְיֶה הָאָרֶץ הַהִיא בִּיהוּדָה אוֹ בְעֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן אוֹ בַגָּלִיל. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


וכל הארצות כאחת לזיתים ולתמרים – all the lands that are in the Land o Israel eat olives and dates through one land that didn’t cease in it the olives and dates for beasts from the field, whether they are in this land in Judea or in Transjordan or in the Galilee. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:4:1)


**אוֹכְלִים עַל הַמֻּפְקָר.** אוֹכְלִים מִמַּה שֶּׁבַּבַּיִת עַל יְדֵי שֶׁמָּצוּי מֵאוֹתוֹ הַמִּין בַּשָּׂדֶה בְּמָקוֹם מֻפְקָר. אֲבָל אִם פָּסַק וְכָלָה אוֹתוֹ הַמִּין מִמָּקוֹם הַמֻּפְקָר, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּמְצָא מִמֶּנּוּ בְּמָקוֹם הַשָּׁמוּר בַּבָּתִּים אוֹ בַגִּנּוֹת חַיָּב לְבַעֵר, כִּדְאָמְרָן כָּלָה לַחַיָּה מִן הַשָּׂדֶה כַּלֵּה לִבְהֶמְתְּךָ מִן הַבַּיִת:


אוכלין על המפקר – they eat from what is in the house through that which is found from the same species in the field in an ownerless place. But if it that species stopped and ceased from the place that is ownerless, even though it is found of it in a guarded place in the houses or in the gardens, he is liable to remove them [from the house] as we say that it ceased for the beasts from the field, it ceased for the cattle from the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:4:2)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי מַתִּיר אַף עַל הַשָּׁמוּר.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ר' יוסי מתיר אף על השמור – but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:4:3)


**אוֹכְלִים עַל הַטְּפִיחִים.** עַל מַה שֶּׁמְּשִׂימִים הָעוֹפוֹת בַּטְּפִיחִים, שֶׁהֵן כְּלֵי חֶרֶס הַבְּנוּיִים בַּכֹּתֶל אֲשֶׁר שָׁם צִפֳּרִים יְקַנֵּנוּ. וְאוֹכְלִים מִמַּה שֶּׁבַּבַּיִת כָּל זְמַן שֶׁמָּצוּי מֵאוֹתוֹ הַמִּין בַּטְּפִיחִים:


אוכלים על הטפיחים (they eat the stinted/poor grains or the growth between the grass) – on what the birds place in the stinted poor/stinted grains, that are earthenware jugs that are built into the wall where there birds that build a nest. And they eat from what is in the house all the while that it is found from that species in the poor/stinted-grains.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:4:4)


**וְעַל הַדּוּפְרָא.** אִילָן שֶׁעוֹשֶׂה פֵּרוֹת שְׁנֵי פְעָמִים בַּשָּׁנָה, אוֹכְלִים בָּרִאשׁוֹן עַד שֶׁיִּכְלֶה הָאַחֲרוֹן שֶׁבְּמִינָם:


ועל הדופרא (a species that bears a species of figs twice a year) a tree that makes fruit twice in a year, they eat the first until the last runs out that is in their species.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:4:5)


**אֲבָל לֹא עַל הַסִּתְוָנִיּוֹת.** אֵין אוֹכְלִין בָּעֲנָבִים עַל יְדֵי הָעֲנָבִים הַגְּדֵלִים בִּימוֹת הַסְּתָיו:


אבל א על הסתווניות – they don’t eat the grapes through the grapes that grow in the days of the autumn (i.e., winter fruit).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:4:6)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה מַתִּיר.** לֶאֱכֹל בָּעֲנָבִים עַל יְדֵי הַסִּתְוָנִיּוֹת כָּל זְמַן שֶׁבִּכְּרוּ קֹדֶם שֶׁיִּכְלֶה הַקַּיִץ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה מתיר – [Rabbi Yehuda permits] to eat grapes through the winter fruit/that grow in the autumn all the while that they produce first fruits prior to the end of he summer. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:5:1)


**הַכּוֹבֵשׁ שְׁלֹשָׁה כְבָשִׁים.** שְׁלֹשָׁה מִינֵי יָרָק:


הכובש ג' כבשים (he who puts three sorts of pressed vegetables into a jar) – three kinds of vegetables.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:5:2)


**בְּחָבִית אַחַת.** שֶׁל חֹמֶץ אוֹ שֶׁל צִיר, כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּתְקַיְּמוּ שְׁלֹשָׁה מִינֵי הַיָּרָק יַחַד:


בחבית אחת – of vinegar or of brine, in order that they will preserve/maintain the three kinds of vegetables together.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:5:3)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר אוֹכְלִים עַל הָרִאשׁוֹן.** וְכֵיוָן דְּכָלָה מִין אֶחָד מֵהֶן מִן הַשָּׂדֶה נֶאֶסְרוּ כֻלָּם וַאֲפִלּוּ אוֹתָן שֶׁלֹּא כָלוּ, שֶׁאוֹתוֹ מִין שֶׁכָּלָה נָתַן טַעַם בְּאוֹתָן שֶׁלֹּא כָלוּ וַאֲסָרָן, דִּתְנַן לְעֵיל פֶּרֶק ז'‏ שְׁבִיעִית אוֹסֶרֶת בְּמִינָהּ בְּמַשֶּׁהוּ, שֶׁלֹּא בְמִינָהּ בְּנוֹתֵן טַעַם:


רבי אליעזר אומר אוכלים ע הראשון – and since the first species of them was completed from the field, all of them wer forbidden, and even those that were not completed, for that species that completed provided a flavor to those that had not been completed and forbade them, and it is taught in the Mishnah above in Chapter 7 of [Tractate] Sheviit (Mishnah 7), that the Seventh Year prohibits its species, however small, and not of its own species through its imparting a flavor.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:5:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אוֹמֵר אַף עַל הָאַחֲרוֹן.** עַל סְמַךְ מִין הַכָּלֶה אַחֲרוֹן יֹאכַל מִשְּׁלָשְׁתָּן אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁכְּבָר כָּלוּ הַשְּׁנַיִם לַחַיָּה שֶׁבַּשָּׂדֶה. וְטַעֲמָא דְרַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ, הוֹאִיל וְאֵלּוּ שֶׁכָּלוּ בְּלוּעִים מִטַּעַם אוֹתוֹ שֶׁלֹּא כָלָה, דְּאָמַר קְרָא (ויקרא כה) מִן הַשָּׂדֶה תֹּאכְלוּ אֶת תְּבוּאָתָהּ, כָּל זְמַן שֶׁאַתָּה אוֹכֵל מִן הַשָּׂדֶה אַתָּה אוֹכֵל מִן הַבַּיִת, וְהַאי קְרָא יְתֵרָא הוּא, דְּהָא דָרְשִׁינַן לַהּ מִן וְלִבְהֶמְתְּךָ וְלַחַיָּה אֲשֶׁר בְּאַרְצֶךָ, כָּל זְמַן שֶׁחַיָּה אוֹכֶלֶת בַּשָּׂדֶה הַאֲכֵל לִבְהֶמְתְּךָ מִן הַבַּיִת וְכוּ', אֶלָּא קְרָא לִכְבוּשִׁים הוּא דַּאֲתָא, וְהָכִי קָאָמַר מִן הַשָּׂדֶה תֹּאכְלוּ אֶת תְּבוּאָתָהּ, אִם יֵשׁ בַּשָּׂדֶה מִקְצָת מִן הַדָּבָר הַנִּכְבָּשׁ בַּבַּיִת, שֶׁהַטַּעַם הַבָּלוּעַ בַּדָּבָר הַנִּכְבָּשׁ יֵשׁ בְּמִינוֹ עֲדַיִן בַּשָּׂדֶה שֶׁעֲדַיִן לֹא כָלָה, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעִקַּר הַפְּרִי כָּלָה, אַף עַל פִּי כֵן תֹּאכְלוּ אֶת תְּבוּאָתָהּ וְאֵינוֹ זָקוּק לְבַעֵר:


רבי יהושע אומר עף על האחרון – with the support of the species that was completed last an be eaten from the three of them, even though the two had ceased for the beasts that are in the field, and the reason of Rabbi Yehoshua is since that these that were completed were absorbed from the the taste of that which had not completed, as the Biblical verse states (Leviticus 25:12): “you may onl eat the growth direct from the field,” all the while that ou eat from the field, you eat from the house, and this Biblical verse is extra, for we expound it from (Leviticus 25:7): “and your cattle and the beasts in your land may eat all its yield,” all the while that beasts eat in the field, your cattle eats from the house, etc. But, the Biblical verse comes for that which si pressed/pickled and this is what he said (Leviticus 25:12): “you may only eat the growth directly from the field,” if there is in the field a bit from what is pressed in the house, that the taste is absorbed in the the thing that is pressed, there is of its species still in the field that still was not completed, even though that the essence of the fruit/produce was completed, nevertheless, “you may eat the growth directl from the field,” and there is no need to remove it [from the house].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:5:5)


**כָּל יָרָק אֶחָד הוּא.** לֹא נָתְנוּ חֲכָמִים שִׁעוּר לְכָל יָרָק וְיָרָק בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ, אֶלָּא אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁשְּׁלָשְׁתָּן כָּלוּ מִן הַשָּׂדֶה, הוֹאִיל וְאִיכָּא שְׁאָר יְרָקוֹת שֶׁלֹּא כָלוּ אוֹכֵל עַל יְדֵי שְׁאָר יְרָקוֹת, וְלֹא יְבַעֲרָם מִן הֶחָבִית עַד שֶׁיִּכְלוּ כָּל הַיְרָקוֹת כֻּלָּן מִן הַשָּׂדֶה, וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל דְּכָל שֶׁכָּלָה מִינוֹ מִן הַשָּׂדֶה יְבַעֵר מִינוֹ מִן הֶחָבִית וְאוֹכֵל אֶת הַשְּׁאָר:


כל ירק אחד הוא – the Sages did not provide a measurement for each vegetable and every vegetable on its own, but rather, even though the three of them were completed from the field, but since there are the rest of the vegetables that did not cease, he eats through the other vegetables and does not remove them from the jar until all of the vegetables have run out from the field. And the Halakha is according to Rabban Gamaliel that all that its species have ceased from the field, he can remove its species from the jar and eat the rest.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:1)


**הַמְלַקֵּט עֲשָׂבִים לַחִים.** אוֹכְלָן בְּלֹא בִעוּר: **עַד שֶׁיִּיבַשׁ.** לַחְלוּחִית הָאָרֶץ הַמְמַתֵּק אֶת הַפֵּרוֹת, מִשָּׁם וְאֵילָךְ חַיָּב לְבַעֲרָן:


המלקט עשבים לחים – he eats them [in the Seventh Year] without removal until the moistness of the land dries up that sweetens the produce and fruit; from then and onward, he is liable to remove them from the house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:2)


**הַמְגַבֵּב בַּיָּבֵשׁ.** הַמְלַקֵּט בַּעֲשָׂבִים יְבֵשִׁים, אוֹכְלָן בְּלֹא בִעוּר:


המנגב ביבש – the person who gathers dry grasses, eats them without removal [from the house].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:3)


**עַד שֶׁתֵּרֵד רְבִיעָה שְׁנִיָּה.** שֶׁהִיא בְּשָׁנָה בֵּינוֹנִית בְּכ"ג בְּמַרְחֶשְׁוָן, מִשָּׁם וְאֵילָךְ חַיָּב לְבַעֵר:


עד שתרד רביעה שניה – which in an average/intermediate year is the twenty-third of Mar Heshvan; from then on, he is required to remove them [from the hosue].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:4)


**מְגַבֵּב.** תַּרְגּוּם לְקוֹשֵׁשׁ קַשׁ, לְגַבָּבָא:


מגבב (gathers dry plants/leaves) – the Aramaic translation of (Exodus 5:12): “[Then the people scattered throughout the land of Egypt] to gather stubble for straw,” for rakings.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:5)


**רְבִיעָה.** עַל שֵׁם שֶׁהַמָּטָר רוֹבֵעַ אֶת הָאָרֶץ וּמוֹלִידָהּ כְּזָכָר זֶה שֶׁרוֹבֵעַ אֶת הַנְּקֵבָה. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר שֶׁמַּרְבִּיץ הֶעָפָר וּמַשְׁכִּיבוֹ, תַּרְגּוּם רוֹבֵץ, רְבִיעַ:


רביעה – because the rain fructifies the ground and bears living brood like this male that copulates the female. Another interpretation: that it sprinkles the dust and causes it to lie down. The Aramaic translation of (Genesis 29:2) (the Biblical text referred to does not have the requested verb in the singular tense – but it does appear in the plural): “three flocks of sheep were lying there beside it,” -lying.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:6)


**וַעֲלֵי גְפָנִים.** לַחִים, אוֹכְלִים בָּהֶן כָּל זְמַן שֶׁנִּמְצָאִים בַּאֲבִיהֶן, כְּלוֹמַר בִּמְחֻבָּר לָאִילָן:


ועלי גפנים – moist [leaves of vines]. They eat them all the while that the are found in their early stage of ripening, meaning to say, when they are attached to the tree.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:7)


**שֶׁיִּשּׁוֹרוּ.** שֶׁיִּפְּלוּ:


שישורו – that they will fall.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:8)


**וְהַמְגַבֵּב בַּיָּבֵשׁ.** בַּעֲלֵי קָנִים וּגְפָנִים יְבֵשִׁים, אוֹכֵל עַד שֶׁתֵּרֵד רְבִיעָה שְׁנִיָּה, וְאַחַר כָּךְ יְבַעֵר:


והמגבב ביבש – owners of dry reeds and vines, he consumes them until the second rainfall occurs, and after that he should remove it [from the house].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:6:9)


**בְּכֻלָּן.** בֵּין עֲשָׂבִים בֵּין קָנִים וּגְפָנִים, בֵּין לַחִים בֵּין יְבֵשִׁים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


בכולן – whether grasses or reeds and vines whether moist or dry, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:7:1)


**עֲנִיִּים נִכְנָסִים לַפַּרְדְּסָאוֹת.** בִּשְׁאָר שְׁנֵי שָׁבוּעַ לִטֹּל לֶקֶט שִׁכְחָה וּפֵאָה, וְכֵן נִכְנָסִים לַגִּנּוֹת בְּמוֹצָאֵי שְׁבִיעִית:


עניים נכנסים לפרדסאות – in the rest of the years of the Seven Year [cycle], [the poor] gather gleanings and the forgotten sheaves and the corner of the field, and similarly, they enter to the gardens in the aftermath of the Seventh Year


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:7:2)


**עַד שֶׁתֵּרֵד רְבִיעָה שְׁנִיָּה.** מִפְּנֵי מוֹתְרוֹת פֵּרוֹת שְׁבִיעִית שֶׁנִּשְׁאֲרוּ בָהּ:


until the second rainfall occurs, because the fruits/produce of the Seventh Year that remained from it are permitted.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:7:3)


**בַּתֶּבֶן וּבַקַּשׁ שֶׁל שְׁבִיעִית.** דִּסְתָם תֶּבֶן וְקַשׁ חָזֵי לְמַאֲכַל בְּהֵמָה וְיֵשׁ בּוֹ קְדֻשַּׁת שְׁבִיעִית, וְאָסוּר לְשָׂרְפוֹ וְלֵהָנוֹת בּוֹ הֲנָאָה שֶׁל [אִבּוּד], מִשּׁוּם דְּדָרְשִׁינַן לְאָכְלָה וְלֹא לְהֶפְסֵד. אֲבָל כְּשֶׁנִּתְבַּטֵּל מַה שֶּׁבַּשָּׂדֶה מִמַּאֲכַל חַיָּה פָּקְעָה קְדֻשַּׁת שְׁבִיעִית מִמַּה שֶּׁבַּבַּיִת:


בתבן ובקש של שביעית – for mere straw and stubble are fit for consumption by animals and they have the sanctity of the Seventh Year, and it is prohibited to burn it and to derive the benefit of [destruction], because we expound (see Mishnah Sheviit, Chapter 8, Mishnah 1 – and Leviticus 25:6): “But you may eat whatever he land during its sabbath will produce,” and not for loss. But when it is abolished, what was in the field from the food for live beasts, it released the Sanctity of the Seventh Year from what is in the house.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:8:1)


**וְהִגִּיעַ שְׁעַת הַבִּעוּר.** זְמַן בִּעוּר הַפֵּרוֹת לְפִי מְקוֹמָן, כְּדִתְנַן לְעֵיל שָׁלֹשׁ אֲרָצוֹת לַבִּעוּר. וּבַבְּרַיְתָא [פְּסָחִים נג.] שָׁנִינוּ אוֹכְלִים בָּעֲנָבִים עַד הַפֶּסַח, בַּזֵּיתִים עַד הָעֲצֶרֶת, בַּגְּרוֹגָרוֹת עַד הַחֲנֻכָּה, בַּתְּמָרִים עַד הַפּוּרִים, וְכֻלָּן שֶׁל שָׁנָה הַשְּׁמִינִית. וּזְמַנִּים הַלָּלוּ בִּסְתָם, שֶׁאֵין יָדוּעַ אִם כָּלָה לַחַיָּה מִן הַשָּׂדֶה אִם לָאו, אֲבָל בְּיָדוּעַ, הַכֹּל לְפִי מַה שֶׁכָּלוּ:


והגיע שעת הביעור – the time of removal of he produce/produce according to their place, as is taught in the Mishnah above (Tractate Sheviit, Chapter 9, Mishnah 2): “Three regions with respect [to the laws] of removal, and in the Baraita (Tractate Pesahim 53a), we taught, that one consumes grapes until Passover, olives until Shavuot and dried figs until Hanukkah, dates until Purim and all of them of the Eighth Year. And these periods with mere [produce], if it was used up for the beasts of the field or not, but if it is known, everything is according to what had been used up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:8:2)


**מְחַלְּקָן מָזוֹן שָׁלשׁ סְעֻדּוֹת לְכָל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד.** מֵאַנְשֵׁי בֵיתוֹ, וְאַחַר כָּךְ מְחַלֵּק לִשְׁכֵנָיו וְלִקְרוֹבָיו וּמְיֻדָּעָיו, וּמוֹצִיא וּמַנִּיחַ עַל פֶּתַח בֵּיתוֹ וְאוֹמֵר, אַחֵינוּ בֵית יִשְׂרָאֵל, מִי שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לִטֹּל יִטֹּל:


מחלקן מזון שלש סעודות לכל אחד ואחד – from the men of the house, and afterwards, he distributes to his neighbors, to his relatives and to his acquaintances; and he removes and places it on the entrance of his house and states: “Our brethren of the House of Israel: He who needs to take should take.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:8:3)


**וַעֲנִיִּים אוֹכְלִים אַחַר הַבִּעוּר אֲבָל לֹא עֲשִׁירִים.** דִּכְתִיב (שמות כג) וְאָכְלוּ אֶבְיֹנֵי עַמֶּךָ וְיִתְרָם תֹּאכַל חַיַּת הַשָּׂדֶה, אֶבְיוֹנֵי עַמֶּךָ וְלֹא עַמֶּךָ:


ועניים אוכלים אחר הביעור אבל לא עשירים – as it is written (Exodus 23:11): “[but in the seventh you shall let it rest and lie fallow.] Let the needy among your people eat of it, and what they leave let the wild beasts eat.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Sheviit 9:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Sheviit/r/9:8:4)
